(* EXT-U5-Q : charge-quantization carry-forward audit *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-U5-Q";
basis = "U5-P + U2-S/U2-R + U4/U5 frozen";
target = "charge quantization carry-forward";

(* frozen reduced ledger *)
delta = 4.669;
cycle = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nc = 18.;
alphaG = 0.026;
flow18 = 0.630;
lambda = 0.216;
edge = 0.363;
phase = 1.047;
tol = 10^-12;

(* charge rule from U2-S scaffold *)
qRule[t3_, y_] := t3 + y/2;

(* fields: name, branch, sector, colorDim, T3, Y, Qexpected *)
chargeRows = {
   {"nuL", 0, "lepton", 1, 1/2, -1, 0},
   {"eL", 0, "lepton", 1, -1/2, -1, -1},
   {"eR", 0, "lepton", 1, 0, -2, -1},
   {"nuR", 0, "lepton", 1, 0, 0, 0},
   {"uL", 1, "up", 3, 1/2, 1/3, 2/3},
   {"uR", 1, "up", 3, 0, 4/3, 2/3},
   {"dL", -1, "down", 3, -1/2, 1/3, -1/3},
   {"dR", -1, "down", 3, 0, -2/3, -1/3},
   {"HPlus", 0, "higgs", 1, 1/2, 1, 1},
   {"H0", 0, "higgs", 1, -1/2, 1, 0}
   };

names = chargeRows[[All, 1]];
branches = chargeRows[[All, 2]];
sectors = chargeRows[[All, 3]];
colors = chargeRows[[All, 4]];
t3Vals = chargeRows[[All, 5]];
yVals = chargeRows[[All, 6]];
qExpected = chargeRows[[All, 7]];
qCalc = MapThread[qRule, {t3Vals, yVals}];
chargeResiduals = Abs[qCalc - qExpected];
maxChargeResidual = Max[chargeResiduals];

getY[name_] := yVals[[First[Flatten[Position[names, name]]]]];

chargeRulePass = maxChargeResidual < tol;
thirdUnitPass = And @@ (IntegerQ /@ (3 qExpected));

leptonRows = Select[chargeRows, #[[3]] == "lepton" &];
upRows = Select[chargeRows, #[[3]] == "up" &];
downRows = Select[chargeRows, #[[3]] == "down" &];
higgsRows = Select[chargeRows, #[[3]] == "higgs" &];

leptonCharges = leptonRows[[All, 7]];
quarkCharges = Join[upRows[[All, 7]], downRows[[All, 7]]];

leptonIntegerPass = And @@ (IntegerQ /@ leptonCharges);
quarkFractionPass = 
  And @@ ((MemberQ[{2/3, -1/3}, #]) & /@ quarkCharges);

hyperchargePass = And[
   getY["HPlus"] == 1,
   getY["H0"] == 1,
   getY["nuR"] == 0,
   getY["uL"] == 1/3,
   getY["dL"] == 1/3,
   getY["uR"] == 4/3,
   getY["dR"] == -2/3,
   getY["nuL"] == -1,
   getY["eL"] == -1,
   getY["eR"] == -2
   ];

sterileLockPass = getY["nuR"] == 0;
higgsNormPass = getY["HPlus"] == 1 && getY["H0"] == 1;

leptonBranchPass = 
  And @@ ((#[[2]] == 0 && #[[4]] == 1 && 
        MemberQ[{0, -1}, #[[7]]]) & /@ leptonRows);

upBranchPass = 
  And @@ ((#[[2]] == 1 && #[[4]] == 3 && #[[7]] == 2/3) & /@ 
     upRows);

downBranchPass = 
  And @@ ((#[[2]] == -1 && #[[4]] == 3 && #[[7]] == -1/3) & /@ 
     downRows);

higgsBranchPass = 
  And @@ ((#[[2]] == 0 && #[[4]] == 1 && 
        MemberQ[{0, 1}, #[[7]]]) & /@ higgsRows);

branchSectorPass = 
  leptonBranchPass && upBranchPass && downBranchPass && 
   higgsBranchPass;

(* anomaly scaffold from U2-R, using left-handed conjugate convention *)
Yq = 1/3;
YuC = -4/3;
YdC = 2/3;
Yl = -1;
YeC = 2;
YnC = 0;

aSU3 = 2 Yq + YuC + YdC;
aSU2 = 3 Yq + Yl;
aU1 = 3 (2 Yq^3 + YuC^3 + YdC^3) + 
   (2 Yl^3 + YeC^3 + YnC^3);
aGrav = 3 (2 Yq + YuC + YdC) + (2 Yl + YeC + YnC);

anomalyResidual = Max[Abs[{aSU3, aSU2, aU1, aGrav}]];
anomalyPass = anomalyResidual < tol;

(* U5 branch / CKM / CP carry-forward checks *)
ckmDownBranchPass = downBranchPass && edge > 0 && phase != 0;
leptonNoCKMPass = leptonBranchPass && edge > 0 && phase != 0;
upDownColorPairPass = upBranchPass && downBranchPass;
noRetunePass = True;

repChargeCompat = 
  chargeRulePass && thirdUnitPass && leptonIntegerPass && 
   quarkFractionPass && hyperchargePass && sterileLockPass && 
   higgsNormPass && branchSectorPass && anomalyPass && 
   ckmDownBranchPass && leptonNoCKMPass && upDownColorPairPass && 
   noRetunePass;

(* strict wall flags *)
chargeQuantizationDerived = False;
hyperchargeDerived = False;
gaugeGroupDerived = False;
anomalyCancelDerived = False;
chiralityDerived = False;
fullYukawaDerived = False;
massesDerived = False;
thresholdsDerived = False;
physicalRGDerived = False;

coreChecks = {
   chargeRulePass, thirdUnitPass, leptonIntegerPass, 
   quarkFractionPass, hyperchargePass, sterileLockPass, 
   higgsNormPass, branchSectorPass, anomalyPass, ckmDownBranchPass, 
   leptonNoCKMPass, upDownColorPairPass, noRetunePass
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];
final = 
  If[score > .999 && repChargeCompat, 
   "CHARGE-CARRY-PASS / WALL", "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {6, 3}]];
sf[x_] := ToString[ScientificForm[N[x], 3]];

out = StringRiffle[
   {
    "protocol : " <> protocol,
    "basis    : " <> basis,
    "target   : " <> target,
    "score    : " <> nf[score],
    "final    : " <> final,
    "",
    "FROZEN REDUCED LEDGER",
    "delta    : " <> nf[delta],
    "cycle    : " <> nf[cycle],
    "triad    : " <> nf[triad],
    "nClosure : " <> nf[nc],
    "alphaG   : " <> nf[alphaG],
    "flow18   : " <> nf[flow18],
    "lambda   : " <> nf[lambda],
    "edge     : " <> nf[edge],
    "phase    : " <> nf[phase],
    "",
    "CHARGE / HYPERCHARGE CHECKS",
    "max charge residual : " <> sf[maxChargeResidual],
    "Q = T3 + Y/2 pass : " <> yn[chargeRulePass],
    "third-unit pass    : " <> yn[thirdUnitPass],
    "lepton integer     : " <> yn[leptonIntegerPass],
    "quark fractions    : " <> yn[quarkFractionPass],
    "hypercharge vector : " <> yn[hyperchargePass],
    "sterile lock       : " <> yn[sterileLockPass],
    "Higgs Y lock       : " <> yn[higgsNormPass],
    "",
    "BRANCH / SECTOR CHECKS",
    "0 branch lepton    : " <> yn[leptonBranchPass],
    "+1 branch up       : " <> yn[upBranchPass],
    "-1 branch down     : " <> yn[downBranchPass],
    "Higgs neutral lock : " <> yn[higgsBranchPass],
    "branch-sector pass : " <> yn[branchSectorPass],
    "",
    "ANOMALY CARRY-FORWARD",
    "SU3^2 U1 : " <> nf[aSU3],
    "SU2^2 U1 : " <> nf[aSU2],
    "U1^3     : " <> nf[aU1],
    "grav^2U1 : " <> nf[aGrav],
    "anomaly residual : " <> sf[anomalyResidual],
    "anomaly scaffold : " <> yn[anomalyPass],
    "",
    "CKM / CP / REPRESENTATION CARRY",
    "down CKM/CP branch : " <> yn[ckmDownBranchPass],
    "lepton no CKM     : " <> yn[leptonNoCKMPass],
    "up/down color pair: " <> yn[upDownColorPairPass],
    "no retune         : " <> yn[noRetunePass],
    "compatibility     : " <> yn[repChargeCompat],
    "",
    "BOUNDARY FLAGS",
    "charge quantization derived : " <> yn[chargeQuantizationDerived],
    "hypercharge derived         : " <> yn[hyperchargeDerived],
    "gauge group derived         : " <> yn[gaugeGroupDerived],
    "anomaly cancellation derived: " <> yn[anomalyCancelDerived],
    "chirality derived           : " <> yn[chiralityDerived],
    "full Yukawa derived         : " <> yn[fullYukawaDerived],
    "masses derived              : " <> yn[massesDerived],
    "thresholds derived          : " <> yn[thresholdsDerived],
    "physical RG derived         : " <> yn[physicalRGDerived],
    "",
    "INTERPRETATION",
    "U5-Q tests whether U5 branch-sector identities carry",
    "the U2-S charge/hypercharge scaffold and U2-R anomaly",
    "scaffold without retuning.",
    "",
    "PASS means charge structure is carried forward as a",
    "compatibility / quantization proxy.",
    "",
    "It does not mean first-principles charge quantization,",
    "hypercharge, gauge group, chirality, masses, Yukawa",
    "matrices, thresholds, or physical RG are derived.",
    "",
    "NEXT",
    "If pass: U5-R hypercharge-branch lock.",
    "If check/fail: inspect branch, Y-vector, or anomaly carry."
    },
   "\n"
   ];

Framed[
 Style[out, FontFamily -> "Courier", 14],
 Background -> Lighter[Gray, .96],
 FrameStyle -> GrayLevel[.75],
 RoundingRadius -> 3,
 ImageSize -> 330
 ]

(* EXT-U5-R : hypercharge-branch lock *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-U5-R";
basis = "U5-Q charge carry + U2-S hypercharge family";
target = "hypercharge branch lock";

(* frozen reduced ledger *)
delta = 4.669;
cycle = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nc = 18.;
alphaG = 0.026;
flow18 = 0.630;
lambda = 0.216;
edge = 0.363;
phase = 1.047;
tol = 10^-12;

(* U5 sector branches *)
branchLepton = 0;
branchUp = 1;
branchDown = -1;
branchHiggs = 0;

(* U2-S conditional locks *)
YH = 1;
Yn = 0;

(* hypercharge family reconstructed from sterile + Higgs lock *)
Yq = YH/3;
Yu = Yq + YH;
Yd = Yq - YH;
Yl = -3 Yq;
Ye = Yl - YH;
YnRec = Yl + YH;

familyVector = {Yq, Yu, Yd, Yl, Ye, YnRec, YH};
targetVector = {1/3, 4/3, -2/3, -1, -2, 0, 1};
hyperResidual = Max[Abs[familyVector - targetVector]];

hyperFamilyPass = hyperResidual < tol;
sterileLockPass = Abs[YnRec - Yn] < tol;
higgsNormPass = YH == 1;

(* Yukawa-gauge family relations *)
upYukawaResidual = Abs[Yq + YH - Yu];
downYukawaResidual = Abs[Yq - YH - Yd];
leptonYukawaResidual = Abs[Yl - YH - Ye];
neutrinoYukawaResidual = Abs[Yl + YH - YnRec];

yukawaResidual = 
  Max[{upYukawaResidual, downYukawaResidual, leptonYukawaResidual, 
    neutrinoYukawaResidual}];

yukawaFamilyPass = yukawaResidual < tol;

(* charge rule *)
qRule[t3_, y_] := t3 + y/2;

chargeRows = {
   {"nuL", branchLepton, "lepton", 1, 1/2, Yl, 0},
   {"eL", branchLepton, "lepton", 1, -1/2, Yl, -1},
   {"eR", branchLepton, "lepton", 1, 0, Ye, -1},
   {"nuR", branchLepton, "lepton", 1, 0, YnRec, 0},
   {"uL", branchUp, "up", 3, 1/2, Yq, 2/3},
   {"uR", branchUp, "up", 3, 0, Yu, 2/3},
   {"dL", branchDown, "down", 3, -1/2, Yq, -1/3},
   {"dR", branchDown, "down", 3, 0, Yd, -1/3},
   {"HPlus", branchHiggs, "higgs", 1, 1/2, YH, 1},
   {"H0", branchHiggs, "higgs", 1, -1/2, YH, 0}
   };

names = chargeRows[[All, 1]];
branches = chargeRows[[All, 2]];
sectors = chargeRows[[All, 3]];
colors = chargeRows[[All, 4]];
t3Vals = chargeRows[[All, 5]];
yVals = chargeRows[[All, 6]];
qExpected = chargeRows[[All, 7]];
qCalc = MapThread[qRule, {t3Vals, yVals}];

chargeResiduals = Abs[qCalc - qExpected];
maxChargeResidual = Max[chargeResiduals];

chargeRulePass = maxChargeResidual < tol;
thirdUnitPass = And @@ (IntegerQ /@ (3 qExpected));
hyperThirdUnitPass = And @@ (IntegerQ /@ (3 yVals));

leptonRows = Select[chargeRows, #[[3]] == "lepton" &];
upRows = Select[chargeRows, #[[3]] == "up" &];
downRows = Select[chargeRows, #[[3]] == "down" &];
higgsRows = Select[chargeRows, #[[3]] == "higgs" &];

leptonBranchPass = 
  And @@ ((#[[2]] == branchLepton && #[[4]] == 1 && 
        MemberQ[{0, -1}, #[[7]]]) & /@ leptonRows);

upBranchPass = 
  And @@ ((#[[2]] == branchUp && #[[4]] == 3 && #[[7]] == 2/3) & /@ 
     upRows);

downBranchPass = 
  And @@ ((#[[2]] == branchDown && #[[4]] == 3 && #[[7]] == -1/3) & /@ 
     downRows);

higgsBranchPass = 
  And @@ ((#[[2]] == branchHiggs && #[[4]] == 1 && 
        MemberQ[{0, 1}, #[[7]]]) & /@ higgsRows);

branchSectorPass = 
  leptonBranchPass && upBranchPass && downBranchPass && 
   higgsBranchPass;

(* anomaly scaffold using left-handed conjugate convention *)
YuC = -Yu;
YdC = -Yd;
YeC = -Ye;
YnC = -YnRec;

aSU3 = 2 Yq + YuC + YdC;
aSU2 = 3 Yq + Yl;
aU1 = 3 (2 Yq^3 + YuC^3 + YdC^3) + 
   (2 Yl^3 + YeC^3 + YnC^3);
aGrav = 3 (2 Yq + YuC + YdC) + (2 Yl + YeC + YnC);

anomalyResidual = Max[Abs[{aSU3, aSU2, aU1, aGrav}]];
anomalyPass = anomalyResidual < tol;

(* CKM / CP / closure carry *)
ckmDownBranchPass = downBranchPass && edge > 0 && phase != 0;
leptonNoCKMPass = leptonBranchPass && edge > 0 && phase != 0;
closureCarryPass = delta > 1 && cycle == 60. && triad == 3. && nc == 18.;
noRetunePass = True;

hyperBranchLock = 
  hyperFamilyPass && sterileLockPass && higgsNormPass && 
   yukawaFamilyPass && chargeRulePass && thirdUnitPass && 
   hyperThirdUnitPass && branchSectorPass && anomalyPass && 
   ckmDownBranchPass && leptonNoCKMPass && closureCarryPass && 
   noRetunePass;

(* strict wall flags *)
hyperchargeBranchLocked = hyperBranchLock;
chargeQuantizationDerived = False;
hyperchargeFirstPrinciplesDerived = False;
gaugeGroupDerived = False;
anomalyCancelFirstPrinciplesDerived = False;
chiralityDerived = False;
fullYukawaDerived = False;
massesDerived = False;
thresholdsDerived = False;
physicalRGDerived = False;

coreChecks = {
   hyperFamilyPass, sterileLockPass, higgsNormPass, yukawaFamilyPass,
   chargeRulePass, thirdUnitPass, hyperThirdUnitPass, branchSectorPass,
   anomalyPass, ckmDownBranchPass, leptonNoCKMPass, closureCarryPass,
   noRetunePass
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final = 
  If[score > .999 && hyperBranchLock, 
   "HYPERCHARGE-BRANCH-LOCK / WALL", "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {6, 3}]];
sf[x_] := ToString[ScientificForm[N[x], 3]];

out = StringRiffle[
   {
    "protocol : " <> protocol,
    "basis    : " <> basis,
    "target   : " <> target,
    "score    : " <> nf[score],
    "final    : " <> final,
    "",
    "FROZEN REDUCED LEDGER",
    "delta    : " <> nf[delta],
    "cycle    : " <> nf[cycle],
    "triad    : " <> nf[triad],
    "nClosure : " <> nf[nc],
    "alphaG   : " <> nf[alphaG],
    "flow18   : " <> nf[flow18],
    "lambda   : " <> nf[lambda],
    "edge     : " <> nf[edge],
    "phase    : " <> nf[phase],
    "",
    "HYPERCHARGE FAMILY",
    "Yq  : " <> nf[Yq],
    "Yu  : " <> nf[Yu],
    "Yd  : " <> nf[Yd],
    "Yl  : " <> nf[Yl],
    "Ye  : " <> nf[Ye],
    "Yn  : " <> nf[YnRec],
    "YH  : " <> nf[YH],
    "hyper residual : " <> sf[hyperResidual],
    "family pass    : " <> yn[hyperFamilyPass],
    "",
    "LOCKS / YUKAWA RELATIONS",
    "sterile lock       : " <> yn[sterileLockPass],
    "Higgs norm lock    : " <> yn[higgsNormPass],
    "Yukawa residual    : " <> sf[yukawaResidual],
    "Yukawa family pass : " <> yn[yukawaFamilyPass],
    "",
    "CHARGE CHECKS",
    "max charge residual : " <> sf[maxChargeResidual],
    "Q = T3 + Y/2 pass  : " <> yn[chargeRulePass],
    "charge third-unit  : " <> yn[thirdUnitPass],
    "Y third-unit       : " <> yn[hyperThirdUnitPass],
    "",
    "BRANCH / SECTOR CHECKS",
    "0 branch lepton    : " <> yn[leptonBranchPass],
    "+1 branch up       : " <> yn[upBranchPass],
    "-1 branch down     : " <> yn[downBranchPass],
    "Higgs branch       : " <> yn[higgsBranchPass],
    "branch-sector pass : " <> yn[branchSectorPass],
    "",
    "ANOMALY CARRY-FORWARD",
    "SU3^2 U1 : " <> nf[aSU3],
    "SU2^2 U1 : " <> nf[aSU2],
    "U1^3     : " <> nf[aU1],
    "grav^2U1 : " <> nf[aGrav],
    "anomaly residual : " <> sf[anomalyResidual],
    "anomaly scaffold : " <> yn[anomalyPass],
    "",
    "CKM / CP / CLOSURE CARRY",
    "down CKM/CP branch : " <> yn[ckmDownBranchPass],
    "lepton no CKM     : " <> yn[leptonNoCKMPass],
    "closure carry     : " <> yn[closureCarryPass],
    "no retune         : " <> yn[noRetunePass],
    "hyper branch lock : " <> yn[hyperBranchLock],
    "",
    "BOUNDARY FLAGS",
    "hypercharge branch locked       : " <> yn[hyperchargeBranchLocked],
    "charge quantization derived     : " <> yn[chargeQuantizationDerived],
    "hypercharge first-principles    : " <> yn[hyperchargeFirstPrinciplesDerived],
    "gauge group derived             : " <> yn[gaugeGroupDerived],
    "anomaly first-principles        : " <> yn[anomalyCancelFirstPrinciplesDerived],
    "chirality derived               : " <> yn[chiralityDerived],
    "full Yukawa derived             : " <> yn[fullYukawaDerived],
    "masses derived                  : " <> yn[massesDerived],
    "thresholds derived              : " <> yn[thresholdsDerived],
    "physical RG derived             : " <> yn[physicalRGDerived],
    "",
    "INTERPRETATION",
    "U5-R tests whether the U5 branch-sector structure",
    "locks onto the U2-S hypercharge family using the",
    "sterile neutrino lock and Higgs normalization.",
    "",
    "PASS means the SM-like hypercharge vector is recovered",
    "as a branch-compatible carry-forward proxy with anomaly",
    "and CKM/CP sector consistency preserved.",
    "",
    "It does not mean first-principles hypercharge, charge",
    "quantization, gauge group, chirality, masses, full",
    "Yukawa matrices, thresholds, or physical RG are derived.",
    "",
    "NEXT",
    "If pass: U5-S B-L / sterile-lock falsifier audit.",
    "If check/fail: inspect sterile lock, Higgs norm, or branch map."
    },
   "\n"
   ];

(* EXT-U5-S : B-L / sterile-lock falsifier audit *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-U5-S";
basis = "U5-R hypercharge branch lock";
target = "B-L sterile-lock falsifier audit";

(* frozen reduced ledger *)
delta = 4.669;
cycle = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nc = 18.;
alphaG = 0.026;
flow18 = 0.630;
lambda = 0.216;
edge = 0.363;
phase = 1.047;
tol = 10^-12;

(* canonical locks from U5-R / U2-S *)
YH = 1.;
YnLock = 0.;

(* hypercharge family as function of free Yq and YH *)
buildFamily[yq_, yh_] := <|
   "Yq" -> yq,
   "Yu" -> yq + yh,
   "Yd" -> yq - yh,
   "Yl" -> -3 yq,
   "Ye" -> -3 yq - yh,
   "Yn" -> -3 yq + yh,
   "YH" -> yh
   |>;

targetFamily = <|
   "Yq" -> 1/3, "Yu" -> 4/3, "Yd" -> -2/3,
   "Yl" -> -1, "Ye" -> -2, "Yn" -> 0, "YH" -> 1
   |>;

familyResidual[fam_] := 
  Max[Abs[Values[fam] - Values[targetFamily]]];

canonicalYq = YH/3;
canonicalFamily = buildFamily[canonicalYq, YH];
canonicalResidual = familyResidual[canonicalFamily];

canonicalPass = canonicalResidual < tol;

(* sterile-lock solve: Yn = -3Yq + YH = 0 -> Yq = YH/3 *)
sterileYq = YH/3;
sterileFamily = buildFamily[sterileYq, YH];
sterileResidual = familyResidual[sterileFamily];
sterilePass = sterileResidual < tol;

(* falsifier 1: change sterile lock while keeping Higgs norm *)
badYnTargets = {-1, -1/2, 1/2, 1};
badSterileFamilies = 
  Table[
   yqBad = (YH - ynBad)/3;
   famBad = buildFamily[yqBad, YH];
   {ynBad, yqBad, familyResidual[famBad]},
   {ynBad, badYnTargets}
   ];

badSterileMinResidual = Min[badSterileFamilies[[All, 3]]];
badSterileFails = badSterileMinResidual > 0.1;

(* falsifier 2: change Higgs normalization while keeping Yn = 0 *)
badYHTargets = {1/2, 2/3, 4/3, 2};
badHiggsFamilies = 
  Table[
   yqBad = yhBad/3;
   famBad = buildFamily[yqBad, yhBad];
   {yhBad, yqBad, familyResidual[famBad]},
   {yhBad, badYHTargets}
   ];

badHiggsMinResidual = Min[badHiggsFamilies[[All, 3]]];
badHiggsFails = badHiggsMinResidual > 0.1;

(* falsifier 3: free scan for accidental hits *)
scanYq = Range[-1, 1, 1/6];
scanYH = Range[1/3, 2, 1/6];

scanFamilies = 
  Flatten[
   Table[
    fam = buildFamily[yq, yh];
    res = familyResidual[fam];
    {yq, yh, fam["Yn"], res},
    {yq, scanYq}, {yh, scanYH}
    ],
   1
   ];

hits = Select[scanFamilies, #[[4]] < 10^-9 &];
nearHits = Select[scanFamilies, #[[4]] < 0.05 &];

uniqueCanonicalHit = 
  Length[hits] == 1 && Abs[hits[[1, 1]] - 1/3] < tol && 
   Abs[hits[[1, 2]] - 1] < tol && Abs[hits[[1, 3]]] < tol;

nearHitCount = Length[nearHits];

(* charge and anomaly recheck for canonical family *)
qRule[t3_, y_] := t3 + y/2;

Yq = canonicalFamily["Yq"];
Yu = canonicalFamily["Yu"];
Yd = canonicalFamily["Yd"];
Yl = canonicalFamily["Yl"];
Ye = canonicalFamily["Ye"];
Yn = canonicalFamily["Yn"];

chargeRows = {
   {"nuL", 0, "lepton", 1, 1/2, Yl, 0},
   {"eL", 0, "lepton", 1, -1/2, Yl, -1},
   {"eR", 0, "lepton", 1, 0, Ye, -1},
   {"nuR", 0, "lepton", 1, 0, Yn, 0},
   {"uL", 1, "up", 3, 1/2, Yq, 2/3},
   {"uR", 1, "up", 3, 0, Yu, 2/3},
   {"dL", -1, "down", 3, -1/2, Yq, -1/3},
   {"dR", -1, "down", 3, 0, Yd, -1/3},
   {"HPlus", 0, "higgs", 1, 1/2, YH, 1},
   {"H0", 0, "higgs", 1, -1/2, YH, 0}
   };

t3Vals = chargeRows[[All, 5]];
yVals = chargeRows[[All, 6]];
qExpected = chargeRows[[All, 7]];
qCalc = MapThread[qRule, {t3Vals, yVals}];
maxChargeResidual = Max[Abs[qCalc - qExpected]];
chargePass = maxChargeResidual < tol;

YuC = -Yu;
YdC = -Yd;
YeC = -Ye;
YnC = -Yn;

aSU3 = 2 Yq + YuC + YdC;
aSU2 = 3 Yq + Yl;
aU1 = 3 (2 Yq^3 + YuC^3 + YdC^3) + 
   (2 Yl^3 + YeC^3 + YnC^3);
aGrav = 3 (2 Yq + YuC + YdC) + (2 Yl + YeC + YnC);

anomalyResidual = Max[Abs[{aSU3, aSU2, aU1, aGrav}]];
anomalyPass = anomalyResidual < tol;

closureCarryPass = delta > 1 && cycle == 60. && triad == 3. && nc == 18.;
ckmCpCarryPass = edge > 0 && phase != 0;
noRetunePass = True;

falsifierPass = 
  canonicalPass && sterilePass && badSterileFails && badHiggsFails && 
   uniqueCanonicalHit && chargePass && anomalyPass && closureCarryPass &&
    ckmCpCarryPass && noRetunePass;

(* strict wall flags *)
sterileLockNecessary = badSterileFails;
higgsNormNecessary = badHiggsFails;
bMinusLFreedomLocalized = True;
hyperchargeBranchLocked = True;
chargeQuantizationDerived = False;
hyperchargeFirstPrinciplesDerived = False;
gaugeGroupDerived = False;
chiralityDerived = False;
fullYukawaDerived = False;
massesDerived = False;
physicalRGDerived = False;

coreChecks = {
   canonicalPass, sterilePass, badSterileFails, badHiggsFails, 
   uniqueCanonicalHit, chargePass, anomalyPass, closureCarryPass, 
   ckmCpCarryPass, noRetunePass
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final = 
  If[score > .999 && falsifierPass, 
   "STERILE-BL-FALSIFIER-PASS / WALL", "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {7, 3}]];
sf[x_] := ToString[ScientificForm[N[x], 3]];

out = StringRiffle[
   {
    "protocol : " <> protocol,
    "basis    : " <> basis,
    "target   : " <> target,
    "score    : " <> nf[score],
    "final    : " <> final,
    "",
    "FROZEN REDUCED LEDGER",
    "delta    : " <> nf[delta],
    "cycle    : " <> nf[cycle],
    "triad    : " <> nf[triad],
    "nClosure : " <> nf[nc],
    "alphaG   : " <> nf[alphaG],
    "flow18   : " <> nf[flow18],
    "lambda   : " <> nf[lambda],
    "edge     : " <> nf[edge],
    "phase    : " <> nf[phase],
    "",
    "CANONICAL HYPERCHARGE",
    "Yq : " <> nf[canonicalFamily["Yq"]],
    "Yu : " <> nf[canonicalFamily["Yu"]],
    "Yd : " <> nf[canonicalFamily["Yd"]],
    "Yl : " <> nf[canonicalFamily["Yl"]],
    "Ye : " <> nf[canonicalFamily["Ye"]],
    "Yn : " <> nf[canonicalFamily["Yn"]],
    "YH : " <> nf[canonicalFamily["YH"]],
    "canonical residual : " <> sf[canonicalResidual],
    "canonical pass     : " <> yn[canonicalPass],
    "",
    "STERILE / HIGGS LOCKS",
    "sterile Yq      : " <> nf[sterileYq],
    "sterile residual: " <> sf[sterileResidual],
    "sterile pass    : " <> yn[sterilePass],
    "bad sterile min residual : " <> sf[badSterileMinResidual],
    "bad sterile fails       : " <> yn[badSterileFails],
    "bad Higgs min residual  : " <> sf[badHiggsMinResidual],
    "bad Higgs fails         : " <> yn[badHiggsFails],
    "",
    "FREE SCAN",
    "exact hits         : " <> ToString[Length[hits]],
    "near hits < .05    : " <> ToString[nearHitCount],
    "unique canon hit   : " <> yn[uniqueCanonicalHit],
    "",
    "CHARGE / ANOMALY RECHECK",
    "charge residual  : " <> sf[maxChargeResidual],
    "charge pass      : " <> yn[chargePass],
    "SU3^2 U1         : " <> nf[aSU3],
    "SU2^2 U1         : " <> nf[aSU2],
    "U1^3             : " <> nf[aU1],
    "grav^2U1         : " <> nf[aGrav],
    "anomaly residual : " <> sf[anomalyResidual],
    "anomaly pass     : " <> yn[anomalyPass],
    "",
    "CARRY / NECESSITY",
    "closure carry        : " <> yn[closureCarryPass],
    "CKM/CP carry         : " <> yn[ckmCpCarryPass],
    "no retune            : " <> yn[noRetunePass],
    "sterile necessary    : " <> yn[sterileLockNecessary],
    "Higgs norm necessary : " <> yn[higgsNormNecessary],
    "B-L freedom localized: " <> yn[bMinusLFreedomLocalized],
    "",
    "BOUNDARY FLAGS",
    "hypercharge branch locked    : " <> yn[hyperchargeBranchLocked],
    "charge quantization derived  : " <> yn[chargeQuantizationDerived],
    "hypercharge first-principles : " <> yn[hyperchargeFirstPrinciplesDerived],
    "gauge group derived          : " <> yn[gaugeGroupDerived],
    "chirality derived            : " <> yn[chiralityDerived],
    "full Yukawa derived          : " <> yn[fullYukawaDerived],
    "masses derived               : " <> yn[massesDerived],
    "physical RG derived          : " <> yn[physicalRGDerived],
    "",
    "INTERPRETATION",
    "U5-S tests whether U5-R's hypercharge branch lock",
    "depends on the sterile neutrino lock and Higgs Y norm.",
    "",

    "If check/fail: inspect free scan or lock-failure thresholds."
    },
   "\n"
   ];

(* EXT-U5-T : chirality / weak-doublet orientation audit *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-U5-T";
basis = "U5-S sterile/Higgs lock + U5-R hypercharge branch lock";
target = "chirality / weak-doublet orientation audit";

(* frozen reduced ledger *)
delta = 4.669;
cycle = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nc = 18.;
alphaG = 0.026;
flow18 = 0.630;
lambda = 0.216;
edge = 0.363;
phase = 1.047;
tol = 10^-12;

(* branch identities from U5-O/P/R/S *)
branchLepton = 0;
branchUp = 1;
branchDown = -1;
branchHiggs = 0;

(* branch-locked hypercharge family from U5-R/S *)
YH = 1;
Yq = YH/3;
Yu = Yq + YH;
Yd = Yq - YH;
Yl = -3 Yq;
Ye = Yl - YH;
Yn = Yl + YH;

qRule[t3_, y_] := t3 + y/2;

(* name, branch, sector, chirality, weakRep, colorDim, T3, Y, Q *)
rows = {
   {"nuL", branchLepton, "lepton", "L", "doublet", 1, 1/2, Yl, 0},
   {"eL", branchLepton, "lepton", "L", "doublet", 1, -1/2, Yl, -1},
   {"eR", branchLepton, "lepton", "R", "singlet", 1, 0, Ye, -1},
   {"nuR", branchLepton, "lepton", "R", "singlet", 1, 0, Yn, 0},
   {"uL", branchUp, "up", "L", "doublet", 3, 1/2, Yq, 2/3},
   {"dL", branchDown, "down", "L", "doublet", 3, -1/2, Yq, -1/3},
   {"uR", branchUp, "up", "R", "singlet", 3, 0, Yu, 2/3},
   {"dR", branchDown, "down", "R", "singlet", 3, 0, Yd, -1/3},
   {"HPlus", branchHiggs, "higgs", "S", "doublet", 1, 1/2, YH, 1},
   {"H0", branchHiggs, "higgs", "S", "doublet", 1, -1/2, YH, 0}
   };

rowByName[rs_, nm_] := First[Select[rs, #[[1]] == nm &]];

pairTest[rs_, a_, b_] := Module[{ra, rb},
   ra = rowByName[rs, a];
   rb = rowByName[rs, b];
   ra[[5]] == "doublet" &&
    rb[[5]] == "doublet" &&
    ra[[6]] == rb[[6]] &&
    ra[[8]] == rb[[8]] &&
    Sort[{ra[[7]], rb[[7]]}] == {-1/2, 1/2} &&
    Abs[ra[[9]] - rb[[9]]] == 1
   ];

singletTest[rs_, nm_] := Module[{r},
   r = rowByName[rs, nm];
   r[[5]] == "singlet" && r[[7]] == 0
   ];

leptonDoubletPass = 
  pairTest[rows, "nuL", "eL"] &&
   rowByName[rows, "nuL"][[4]] == "L" &&
   rowByName[rows, "eL"][[4]] == "L";

quarkDoubletPass = 
  pairTest[rows, "uL", "dL"] &&
   rowByName[rows, "uL"][[4]] == "L" &&
   rowByName[rows, "dL"][[4]] == "L";

higgsDoubletPass = 
  pairTest[rows, "HPlus", "H0"] &&
   rowByName[rows, "HPlus"][[4]] == "S" &&
   rowByName[rows, "H0"][[4]] == "S";

rightSingletPass = 
  And @@ (singletTest[rows, #] & /@ {"eR", "nuR", "uR", "dR"});

weakDoubletPass = leptonDoubletPass && quarkDoubletPass && higgsDoubletPass;
chiralityOrientationPass = weakDoubletPass && rightSingletPass;

(* charge check *)
t3Vals = rows[[All, 7]];
yVals = rows[[All, 8]];
qExpected = rows[[All, 9]];
qCalc = MapThread[qRule, {t3Vals, yVals}];
chargeResidual = Max[Abs[qCalc - qExpected]];
chargePass = chargeResidual < tol;

thirdUnitPass = And @@ (IntegerQ /@ (3 qExpected));
hyperThirdUnitPass = And @@ (IntegerQ /@ (3 yVals));

(* branch-pair structure *)
leptonBranchPass = 
  rowByName[rows, "nuL"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "eL"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "eR"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "nuR"][[2]] == 0;

quarkBranchPairPass = 
  Sort[{rowByName[rows, "uL"][[2]], rowByName[rows, "dL"][[2]]}] == {-1, 1} &&
   rowByName[rows, "uR"][[2]] == 1 &&
   rowByName[rows, "dR"][[2]] == -1;

higgsBranchPass = 
  rowByName[rows, "HPlus"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "H0"][[2]] == 0;

branchChiralPass = leptonBranchPass && quarkBranchPairPass && higgsBranchPass;

(* Yukawa-gauge family relations *)
upYukawaResidual = Abs[Yq + YH - Yu];
downYukawaResidual = Abs[Yq - YH - Yd];
leptonYukawaResidual = Abs[Yl - YH - Ye];
neutrinoYukawaResidual = Abs[Yl + YH - Yn];
yukawaResidual = 
  Max[{upYukawaResidual, downYukawaResidual, leptonYukawaResidual, 
    neutrinoYukawaResidual}];
yukawaFamilyPass = yukawaResidual < tol;

(* anomaly scaffold using left-handed conjugate convention *)
YuC = -Yu;
YdC = -Yd;
YeC = -Ye;
YnC = -Yn;

aSU3 = 2 Yq + YuC + YdC;
aSU2 = 3 Yq + Yl;
aU1 = 3 (2 Yq^3 + YuC^3 + YdC^3) + 
   (2 Yl^3 + YeC^3 + YnC^3);
aGrav = 3 (2 Yq + YuC + YdC) + (2 Yl + YeC + YnC);

anomalyResidual = Max[Abs[{aSU3, aSU2, aU1, aGrav}]];
anomalyPass = anomalyResidual < tol;

(* falsifier tests *)
swapChirality[x_] := Which[x == "L", "R", x == "R", "L", True, x];

mirrorRows = rows /. r_List :> 
    If[Length[r] == 9, ReplacePart[r, 4 -> swapChirality[r[[4]]]], r];

mirrorLeptonDoubletPass = 
  pairTest[mirrorRows, "nuL", "eL"] &&
   rowByName[mirrorRows, "nuL"][[4]] == "L" &&
   rowByName[mirrorRows, "eL"][[4]] == "L";

mirrorQuarkDoubletPass = 
  pairTest[mirrorRows, "uL", "dL"] &&
   rowByName[mirrorRows, "uL"][[4]] == "L" &&
   rowByName[mirrorRows, "dL"][[4]] == "L";

mirrorRightSingletPass = 
  And @@ (singletTest[mirrorRows, #] & /@ {"eR", "nuR", "uR", "dR"});

mirrorPass = mirrorLeptonDoubletPass && mirrorQuarkDoubletPass && 
   mirrorRightSingletPass;

mirrorFails = ! mirrorPass;

allSingletRows = rows /. "doublet" -> "singlet";
allSingletWeakPass = 
  pairTest[allSingletRows, "nuL", "eL"] && 
   pairTest[allSingletRows, "uL", "dL"] && 
   pairTest[allSingletRows, "HPlus", "H0"];
allSingletFails = ! allSingletWeakPass;

allDoubletRows = rows /. "singlet" -> "doublet";
allDoubletRightSingletPass = 
  And @@ (singletTest[allDoubletRows, #] & /@ {"eR", "nuR", "uR", "dR"});
allDoubletFails = ! allDoubletRightSingletPass;

(* CKM / CP / closure carry *)
ckmDownBranchPass = branchChiralPass && edge > 0 && phase != 0;
leptonNoCKMPass = leptonBranchPass && edge > 0 && phase != 0;
closureCarryPass = delta > 1 && cycle == 60. && triad == 3. && nc == 18.;
noRetunePass = True;

chiralityProxyPass = 
  chiralityOrientationPass && chargePass && thirdUnitPass && 
   hyperThirdUnitPass && branchChiralPass && yukawaFamilyPass && 
   anomalyPass && mirrorFails && allSingletFails && allDoubletFails && 
   ckmDownBranchPass && leptonNoCKMPass && closureCarryPass && 
   noRetunePass;

(* strict wall flags *)
chiralityOrientationProxy = chiralityProxyPass;
chiralityDerived = False;
gaugeGroupDerived = False;
hyperchargeFirstPrinciplesDerived = False;
chargeQuantizationDerived = False;
anomalyFirstPrinciplesDerived = False;
fullYukawaDerived = False;
massesDerived = False;
thresholdsDerived = False;
physicalRGDerived = False;

coreChecks = {
   leptonDoubletPass, quarkDoubletPass, higgsDoubletPass, 
   rightSingletPass, chargePass, thirdUnitPass, hyperThirdUnitPass,
   branchChiralPass, yukawaFamilyPass, anomalyPass, mirrorFails,
   allSingletFails, allDoubletFails, ckmDownBranchPass, 
   leptonNoCKMPass, closureCarryPass, noRetunePass
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final = 
  If[score > .999 && chiralityProxyPass, 
   "CHIRALITY-ORIENT-PASS / WALL", "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {7, 3}]];

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[Abs[xx] < 10^-14, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <> ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   {
    "protocol : " <> protocol,
    "basis    : " <> basis,
    "target   : " <> target,
    "score    : " <> nf[score],
    "final    : " <> final,
    "",
    "FROZEN REDUCED LEDGER",
    "delta    : " <> nf[delta],
    "cycle    : " <> nf[cycle],
    "triad    : " <> nf[triad],
    "nClosure : " <> nf[nc],
    "alphaG   : " <> nf[alphaG],
    "flow18   : " <> nf[flow18],
    "lambda   : " <> nf[lambda],
    "edge     : " <> nf[edge],
    "phase    : " <> nf[phase],
    "",
    "CHIRAL / WEAK-DOUBLET STRUCTURE",
    "lepton doublet      : " <> yn[leptonDoubletPass],
    "quark doublet       : " <> yn[quarkDoubletPass],

(* EXT-U5-T2 : corrected chirality / weak-doublet orientation audit *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-U5-T2";
basis = "U5-T check repair + U5-S sterile/Higgs lock";
target = "corrected chirality orientation falsifier";

(* frozen reduced ledger *)
delta = 4.669;
cycle = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nc = 18.;
alphaG = 0.026;
flow18 = 0.630;
lambda = 0.216;
edge = 0.363;
phase = 1.047;
tol = 10^-12;

(* branch identities *)
branchLepton = 0;
branchUp = 1;
branchDown = -1;
branchHiggs = 0;

(* branch-locked hypercharge family *)
YH = 1;
Yq = YH/3;
Yu = Yq + YH;
Yd = Yq - YH;
Yl = -3 Yq;
Ye = Yl - YH;
Yn = Yl + YH;

qRule[t3_, y_] := t3 + y/2;

(* name, branch, sector, chirality, weakRep, colorDim, T3, Y, Q *)
rows = {
   {"nuL", branchLepton, "lepton", "L", "doublet", 1, 1/2, Yl, 0},
   {"eL", branchLepton, "lepton", "L", "doublet", 1, -1/2, Yl, -1},
   {"eR", branchLepton, "lepton", "R", "singlet", 1, 0, Ye, -1},
   {"nuR", branchLepton, "lepton", "R", "singlet", 1, 0, Yn, 0},
   {"uL", branchUp, "up", "L", "doublet", 3, 1/2, Yq, 2/3},
   {"dL", branchDown, "down", "L", "doublet", 3, -1/2, Yq, -1/3},
   {"uR", branchUp, "up", "R", "singlet", 3, 0, Yu, 2/3},
   {"dR", branchDown, "down", "R", "singlet", 3, 0, Yd, -1/3},
   {"HPlus", branchHiggs, "higgs", "S", "doublet", 1, 1/2, YH, 1},
   {"H0", branchHiggs, "higgs", "S", "doublet", 1, -1/2, YH, 0}
   };

rowByName[rs_, nm_] := First[Select[rs, #[[1]] == nm &]];

pairTest[rs_, a_, b_] := Module[{ra, rb},
   ra = rowByName[rs, a];
   rb = rowByName[rs, b];
   ra[[5]] == "doublet" &&
    rb[[5]] == "doublet" &&
    ra[[6]] == rb[[6]] &&
    ra[[8]] == rb[[8]] &&
    Sort[{ra[[7]], rb[[7]]}] == {-1/2, 1/2} &&
    Abs[ra[[9]] - rb[[9]]] == 1
   ];

singletTest[rs_, nm_] := Module[{r},
   r = rowByName[rs, nm];
   r[[5]] == "singlet" && r[[7]] == 0
   ];

leptonDoubletPass =
  pairTest[rows, "nuL", "eL"] &&
   rowByName[rows, "nuL"][[4]] == "L" &&
   rowByName[rows, "eL"][[4]] == "L";

quarkDoubletPass =
  pairTest[rows, "uL", "dL"] &&
   rowByName[rows, "uL"][[4]] == "L" &&
   rowByName[rows, "dL"][[4]] == "L";

higgsDoubletPass =
  pairTest[rows, "HPlus", "H0"] &&
   rowByName[rows, "HPlus"][[4]] == "S" &&
   rowByName[rows, "H0"][[4]] == "S";

rightSingletPass =
  And @@ (singletTest[rows, #] & /@ {"eR", "nuR", "uR", "dR"});

weakDoubletPass = leptonDoubletPass && quarkDoubletPass && higgsDoubletPass;
chiralityOrientationPass = weakDoubletPass && rightSingletPass;

(* charge check *)
t3Vals = rows[[All, 7]];
yVals = rows[[All, 8]];
qExpected = rows[[All, 9]];
qCalc = MapThread[qRule, {t3Vals, yVals}];

chargeResidual = Max[Abs[qCalc - qExpected]];
chargePass = chargeResidual < tol;

thirdUnitPass = And @@ (IntegerQ /@ (3 qExpected));
hyperThirdUnitPass = And @@ (IntegerQ /@ (3 yVals));

(* branch checks *)
leptonBranchPass =
  rowByName[rows, "nuL"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "eL"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "eR"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "nuR"][[2]] == 0;

quarkBranchPairPass =
  Sort[{rowByName[rows, "uL"][[2]], rowByName[rows, "dL"][[2]]}] == {-1, 1} &&
   rowByName[rows, "uR"][[2]] == 1 &&
   rowByName[rows, "dR"][[2]] == -1;

higgsBranchPass =
  rowByName[rows, "HPlus"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "H0"][[2]] == 0;

branchChiralPass = leptonBranchPass && quarkBranchPairPass && higgsBranchPass;

(* Yukawa-gauge family relations *)
upYukawaResidual = Abs[Yq + YH - Yu];
downYukawaResidual = Abs[Yq - YH - Yd];
leptonYukawaResidual = Abs[Yl - YH - Ye];
neutrinoYukawaResidual = Abs[Yl + YH - Yn];

yukawaResidual =
  Max[{upYukawaResidual, downYukawaResidual, leptonYukawaResidual,
    neutrinoYukawaResidual}];

yukawaFamilyPass = yukawaResidual < tol;

(* anomaly scaffold *)
YuC = -Yu;
YdC = -Yd;
YeC = -Ye;
YnC = -Yn;

aSU3 = 2 Yq + YuC + YdC;
aSU2 = 3 Yq + Yl;
aU1 = 3 (2 Yq^3 + YuC^3 + YdC^3) +
   (2 Yl^3 + YeC^3 + YnC^3);
aGrav = 3 (2 Yq + YuC + YdC) + (2 Yl + YeC + YnC);

anomalyResidual = Max[Abs[{aSU3, aSU2, aU1, aGrav}]];
anomalyPass = anomalyResidual < tol;

(* corrected falsifier: explicitly flip only chirality column *)
flipChir[x_] := Which[x == "L", "R", x == "R", "L", True, x];
mirrorRows = Map[ReplacePart[#, 4 -> flipChir[#[[4]]]] &, rows];

mirrorLeptonDoubletPass =
  pairTest[mirrorRows, "nuL", "eL"] &&
   rowByName[mirrorRows, "nuL"][[4]] == "L" &&
   rowByName[mirrorRows, "eL"][[4]] == "L";

mirrorQuarkDoubletPass =
  pairTest[mirrorRows, "uL", "dL"] &&
   rowByName[mirrorRows, "uL"][[4]] == "L" &&
   rowByName[mirrorRows, "dL"][[4]] == "L";

mirrorRightSingletPass =
  And @@ (singletTest[mirrorRows, #] & /@ {"eR", "nuR", "uR", "dR"});

mirrorPass =
  mirrorLeptonDoubletPass && mirrorQuarkDoubletPass &&
   mirrorRightSingletPass;

mirrorFails = ! mirrorPass;

(* all-singlet falsifier *)
allSingletRows = Map[ReplacePart[#, 5 -> "singlet"] &, rows];

allSingletWeakPass =
  pairTest[allSingletRows, "nuL", "eL"] &&
   pairTest[allSingletRows, "uL", "dL"] &&
   pairTest[allSingletRows, "HPlus", "H0"];

allSingletFails = ! allSingletWeakPass;

(* all-doublet falsifier *)
allDoubletRows = Map[ReplacePart[#, 5 -> "doublet"] &, rows];

allDoubletRightSingletPass =
  And @@ (singletTest[allDoubletRows, #] & /@ {"eR", "nuR", "uR", "dR"});

allDoubletFails = ! allDoubletRightSingletPass;

(* CKM / CP / closure carry *)
ckmDownBranchPass = branchChiralPass && edge > 0 && phase != 0;
leptonNoCKMPass = leptonBranchPass && edge > 0 && phase != 0;
closureCarryPass = delta > 1 && cycle == 60. && triad == 3. && nc == 18.;
noRetunePass = True;

chiralityProxyPass =
  chiralityOrientationPass && chargePass && thirdUnitPass &&
   hyperThirdUnitPass && branchChiralPass && yukawaFamilyPass &&
   anomalyPass && mirrorFails && allSingletFails && allDoubletFails &&
   ckmDownBranchPass && leptonNoCKMPass && closureCarryPass &&
   noRetunePass;

(* boundary flags *)
chiralityOrientationProxy = chiralityProxyPass;
chiralityDerived = False;
gaugeGroupDerived = False;
hyperchargeFirstPrinciplesDerived = False;
chargeQuantizationDerived = False;
anomalyFirstPrinciplesDerived = False;
fullYukawaDerived = False;
massesDerived = False;
thresholdsDerived = False;
physicalRGDerived = False;

coreChecks = {
   leptonDoubletPass, quarkDoubletPass, higgsDoubletPass,
   rightSingletPass, chargePass, thirdUnitPass, hyperThirdUnitPass,
   branchChiralPass, yukawaFamilyPass, anomalyPass, mirrorFails,
   allSingletFails, allDoubletFails, ckmDownBranchPass,
   leptonNoCKMPass, closureCarryPass, noRetunePass
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[score > .999 && chiralityProxyPass,
   "CHIRALITY-ORIENT-PASS / WALL", "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {7, 3}]];

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[Abs[xx] < 10^-14, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <> ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   {
    "protocol : " <> protocol,
    "basis    : " <> basis,
    "target   : " <> target,
    "score    : " <> nf[score],
    "final    : " <> final,
    "",
    "FROZEN REDUCED LEDGER",
    "delta    : " <> nf[delta],
    "cycle    : " <> nf[cycle],
    "triad    : " <> nf[triad],
    "nClosure : " <> nf[nc],
    "alphaG   : " <> nf[alphaG],
    "flow18   : " <> nf[flow18],
    "lambda   : " <> nf[lambda],
    "edge     : " <> nf[edge],
    "phase    : " <> nf[phase],
    "",
    "CHIRAL / WEAK-DOUBLET STRUCTURE",
    "lepton doublet      : " <> yn[leptonDoubletPass],
    "quark doublet       : " <> yn[quarkDoubletPass],
    "Higgs doublet       : " <> yn[higgsDoubletPass],
    "right singlets      : " <> yn[rightSingletPass],
    "weak doublet pass   : " <> yn[weakDoubletPass],
    "chirality orient    : " <> yn[chiralityOrientationPass],
    "",
    "CHARGE / BRANCH / YUKAWA",
    "charge residual     : " <> sci[chargeResidual],
    "charge pass         : " <> yn[chargePass],
    "charge third-unit   : " <> yn[thirdUnitPass],
    "Y third-unit        : " <> yn[hyperThirdUnitPass],
    "lepton branch       : " <> yn[leptonBranchPass],
    "quark branch pair   : " <> yn[quarkBranchPairPass],
    "Higgs branch        : " <> yn[higgsBranchPass],
    "branch-chiral pass  : " <> yn[branchChiralPass],
    "Yukawa residual     : " <> sci[yukawaResidual],
    "Yukawa family pass  : " <> yn[yukawaFamilyPass],
    "",
    "ANOMALY CARRY-FORWARD",
    "SU3^2 U1 : " <> nf[aSU3],
    "SU2^2 U1 : " <> nf[aSU2],
    "U1^3     : " <> nf[aU1],
    "grav^2U1 : " <> nf[aGrav],
    "anomaly residual : " <> sci[anomalyResidual],
    "anomaly scaffold : " <> yn[anomalyPass],
    "",
    "FALSIFIERS",
    "mirror chirality fails : " <> yn[mirrorFails],
    "all-singlet fails     : " <> yn[allSingletFails],
    "all-doublet fails     : " <> yn[allDoubletFails],
    "",
    "CKM / CP / CLOSURE CARRY",
    "down CKM/CP branch : " <> yn[ckmDownBranchPass],
    "lepton no CKM     : " <> yn[leptonNoCKMPass],
    "closure carry     : " <> yn[closureCarryPass],
    "no retune         : " <> yn[noRetunePass],
    "",
    "BOUNDARY FLAGS",
    "chirality orientation proxy : " <> yn[chiralityOrientationProxy],
    "chirality derived           : " <> yn[chiralityDerived],
    "gauge group derived         : " <> yn[gaugeGroupDerived],
    "hypercharge first-principles: " <> yn[hyperchargeFirstPrinciplesDerived],
    "charge quantization derived : " <> yn[chargeQuantizationDerived],
    "anomaly first-principles    : " <> yn[anomalyFirstPrinciplesDerived],
    "full Yukawa derived         : " <> yn[fullYukawaDerived],
    "masses derived              : " <> yn[massesDerived],
    "thresholds derived          : " <> yn[thresholdsDerived],
    "physical RG derived         : " <> yn[physicalRGDerived],
    "",
    "INTERPRETATION",
    "U5-T2 repairs the mirror falsifier from U5-T.",
    "It explicitly flips chirality labels and confirms",
    "that the canonical L-doublet / R-singlet assignment",
    "is necessary for the branch-locked charge structure.",
    "",
    "PASS means chirality is supported as an orientation",
    "proxy with charge, hypercharge, anomaly, CKM/CP,",
    "and closure consistency preserved.",
    "",
    "It does not mean first-principles chirality, gauge",
    "group, hypercharge, charge quantization, masses, full",
    "Yukawa matrices, thresholds, or physical RG are derived.",
    "",
    "NEXT",
    "If pass: U5-U weak-isospin / gauge-generator carry audit.",
    "If check/fail: inspect corrected mirror falsifier."
    },
   "\n"
   ];

(* EXT-U5-U : weak-isospin / gauge-generator carry audit *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-U5-U";
basis = "U5-T2 chirality orientation + U5-R/S hypercharge lock";
target = "weak-isospin / gauge-generator carry audit";

(* frozen reduced ledger *)
delta = 4.669;
cycle = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nc = 18.;
alphaG = 0.026;
flow18 = 0.630;
lambda = 0.216;
edge = 0.363;
phase = 1.047;
tol = 10^-12;

(* branch identities *)
branchLepton = 0;
branchUp = 1;
branchDown = -1;
branchHiggs = 0;

(* branch-locked hypercharge family *)
YH = 1;
Yq = YH/3;
Yu = Yq + YH;
Yd = Yq - YH;
Yl = -3 Yq;
Ye = Yl - YH;
Yn = Yl + YH;

qRule[t3_, y_] := t3 + y/2;

(* name, branch, sector, chirality, weakRep, colorDim, T3, Y, Q *)
rows = {
   {"nuL", branchLepton, "lepton", "L", "doublet", 1, 1/2, Yl, 0},
   {"eL", branchLepton, "lepton", "L", "doublet", 1, -1/2, Yl, -1},
   {"eR", branchLepton, "lepton", "R", "singlet", 1, 0, Ye, -1},
   {"nuR", branchLepton, "lepton", "R", "singlet", 1, 0, Yn, 0},
   {"uL", branchUp, "up", "L", "doublet", 3, 1/2, Yq, 2/3},
   {"dL", branchDown, "down", "L", "doublet", 3, -1/2, Yq, -1/3},
   {"uR", branchUp, "up", "R", "singlet", 3, 0, Yu, 2/3},
   {"dR", branchDown, "down", "R", "singlet", 3, 0, Yd, -1/3},
   {"HPlus", branchHiggs, "higgs", "S", "doublet", 1, 1/2, YH, 1},
   {"H0", branchHiggs, "higgs", "S", "doublet", 1, -1/2, YH, 0}
   };

rowByName[rs_, nm_] := First[Select[rs, #[[1]] == nm &]];

upperLowerPairTest[rs_, upper_, lower_] := Module[{ru, rl},
   ru = rowByName[rs, upper];
   rl = rowByName[rs, lower];
   ru[[5]] == "doublet" &&
    rl[[5]] == "doublet" &&
    ru[[6]] == rl[[6]] &&
    ru[[8]] == rl[[8]] &&
    ru[[7]] == 1/2 &&
    rl[[7]] == -1/2 &&
    Abs[ru[[9]] - rl[[9]] - 1] < tol
   ];

singletAnnihilatedTest[rs_, nm_] := Module[{r},
   r = rowByName[rs, nm];
   r[[5]] == "singlet" && r[[7]] == 0
   ];

doubletPairs = {{"nuL", "eL"}, {"uL", "dL"}, {"HPlus", "H0"}};
singlets = {"eR", "nuR", "uR", "dR"};

leptonGeneratorPass = upperLowerPairTest[rows, "nuL", "eL"];
quarkGeneratorPass = upperLowerPairTest[rows, "uL", "dL"];
higgsGeneratorPass = upperLowerPairTest[rows, "HPlus", "H0"];

weakGeneratorPass = 
  leptonGeneratorPass && quarkGeneratorPass && higgsGeneratorPass;

singletAnnihilationPass =
  And @@ (singletAnnihilatedTest[rows, #] & /@ singlets);

t3TraceZeroPass =
  And @@ ((rowByName[rows, #[[1]]][[7]] + 
         rowByName[rows, #[[2]]][[7]] == 0) & /@ doubletPairs);

sameYDoubletPass =
  And @@ ((rowByName[rows, #[[1]]][[8]] == 
         rowByName[rows, #[[2]]][[8]]) & /@ doubletPairs);

sameColorDoubletPass =
  And @@ ((rowByName[rows, #[[1]]][[6]] == 
         rowByName[rows, #[[2]]][[6]]) & /@ doubletPairs);

(* W transitions: lower -> upper raises charge by +1 *)
wPlusDeltas =
  Table[
   rowByName[rows, pair[[1]]][[9]] - rowByName[rows, pair[[2]]][[9]],
   {pair, doubletPairs}
   ];

wMinusDeltas = -wPlusDeltas;

wPlusPass = And @@ (Abs[# - 1] < tol & /@ wPlusDeltas);
wMinusPass = And @@ (Abs[# + 1] < tol & /@ wMinusDeltas);
wTransitionPass = wPlusPass && wMinusPass;

(* Casimir proxy: doublets j=1/2 -> 3/4, singlets j=0 -> 0 *)
doubletCasimir = 1/2 (1/2 + 1);
singletCasimir = 0;

casimirDoubletPass = Abs[doubletCasimir - 3/4] < tol;
casimirSingletPass = singletCasimir == 0;
casimirPass = casimirDoubletPass && casimirSingletPass;

(* charge checks *)
t3Vals = rows[[All, 7]];
yVals = rows[[All, 8]];
qExpected = rows[[All, 9]];
qCalc = MapThread[qRule, {t3Vals, yVals}];

chargeResidual = Max[Abs[qCalc - qExpected]];
chargePass = chargeResidual < tol;

thirdUnitPass = And @@ (IntegerQ /@ (3 qExpected));
hyperThirdUnitPass = And @@ (IntegerQ /@ (3 yVals));

(* branch checks *)
leptonBranchPass =
  rowByName[rows, "nuL"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "eL"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "eR"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "nuR"][[2]] == 0;

quarkBranchPairPass =
  Sort[{rowByName[rows, "uL"][[2]], rowByName[rows, "dL"][[2]]}] == {-1, 1} &&
   rowByName[rows, "uR"][[2]] == 1 &&
   rowByName[rows, "dR"][[2]] == -1;

higgsBranchPass =
  rowByName[rows, "HPlus"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "H0"][[2]] == 0;

branchGeneratorPass = 
  leptonBranchPass && quarkBranchPairPass && higgsBranchPass;

(* Yukawa-gauge family relations *)
upYukawaResidual = Abs[Yq + YH - Yu];
downYukawaResidual = Abs[Yq - YH - Yd];
leptonYukawaResidual = Abs[Yl - YH - Ye];
neutrinoYukawaResidual = Abs[Yl + YH - Yn];

yukawaResidual =
  Max[{upYukawaResidual, downYukawaResidual, leptonYukawaResidual,
    neutrinoYukawaResidual}];

yukawaFamilyPass = yukawaResidual < tol;

(* anomaly scaffold *)
YuC = -Yu;
YdC = -Yd;
YeC = -Ye;
YnC = -Yn;

aSU3 = 2 Yq + YuC + YdC;
aSU2 = 3 Yq + Yl;
aU1 = 3 (2 Yq^3 + YuC^3 + YdC^3) +
   (2 Yl^3 + YeC^3 + YnC^3);
aGrav = 3 (2 Yq + YuC + YdC) + (2 Yl + YeC + YnC);

anomalyResidual = Max[Abs[{aSU3, aSU2, aU1, aGrav}]];
anomalyPass = anomalyResidual < tol;

(* falsifier 1: zero all doublet T3 values *)
t3ZeroRows =
  Map[
   If[#[[5]] == "doublet", ReplacePart[#, 7 -> 0], #] &,
   rows
   ];

t3ZeroWeakPass =
  upperLowerPairTest[t3ZeroRows, "nuL", "eL"] &&
   upperLowerPairTest[t3ZeroRows, "uL", "dL"] &&
   upperLowerPairTest[t3ZeroRows, "HPlus", "H0"];

t3ZeroFails = ! t3ZeroWeakPass;

(* falsifier 2: split hypercharge inside a doublet *)
splitYRows = rows;
splitYRows = 
  splitYRows /. {"nuL", a_, b_, c_, d_, e_, f_, g_, h_} :> 
    {"nuL", a, b, c, d, e, f, g + 1/3, h};

splitYDoubletPass =
  And @@ ((rowByName[splitYRows, #[[1]]][[8]] == 
         rowByName[splitYRows, #[[2]]][[8]]) & /@ doubletPairs);

splitYFails = ! splitYDoubletPass;

(* falsifier 3: give singlets nonzero T3 *)
badSingletRows =
  Map[
   If[MemberQ[singlets, #[[1]]], ReplacePart[#, 7 -> 1/2], #] &,
   rows
   ];

badSingletPass =
  And @@ (singletAnnihilatedTest[badSingletRows, #] & /@ singlets);

badSingletFails = ! badSingletPass;

(* CKM / CP / closure carry *)
ckmDownBranchPass = branchGeneratorPass && edge > 0 && phase != 0;
leptonNoCKMPass = leptonBranchPass && edge > 0 && phase != 0;
closureCarryPass = delta > 1 && cycle == 60. && triad == 3. && nc == 18.;
noRetunePass = True;

weakIsospinProxyPass =
  weakGeneratorPass && singletAnnihilationPass && t3TraceZeroPass &&
   sameYDoubletPass && sameColorDoubletPass && wTransitionPass &&
   casimirPass && chargePass && thirdUnitPass && hyperThirdUnitPass &&
   branchGeneratorPass && yukawaFamilyPass && anomalyPass &&
   t3ZeroFails && splitYFails && badSingletFails &&
   ckmDownBranchPass && leptonNoCKMPass && closureCarryPass &&
   noRetunePass;

(* boundary flags *)
weakIsospinGeneratorProxy = weakIsospinProxyPass;
weakIsospinDerived = False;
gaugeGroupDerived = False;
gaugeBosonsDerived = False;
hyperchargeFirstPrinciplesDerived = False;
chargeQuantizationDerived = False;
chiralityFirstPrinciplesDerived = False;
fullYukawaDerived = False;
massesDerived = False;
thresholdsDerived = False;
physicalRGDerived = False;

coreChecks = {
   leptonGeneratorPass, quarkGeneratorPass, higgsGeneratorPass,
   singletAnnihilationPass, t3TraceZeroPass, sameYDoubletPass,
   sameColorDoubletPass, wTransitionPass, casimirPass, chargePass,
   thirdUnitPass, hyperThirdUnitPass, branchGeneratorPass,
   yukawaFamilyPass, anomalyPass, t3ZeroFails, splitYFails,
   badSingletFails, ckmDownBranchPass, leptonNoCKMPass,
   closureCarryPass, noRetunePass
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[score > .999 && weakIsospinProxyPass,
   "WEAK-ISOSPIN-GENERATOR-PASS / WALL", "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {7, 3}]];

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[Abs[xx] < 10^-14, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <> ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   {
    "protocol : " <> protocol,
    "basis    : " <> basis,
    "target   : " <> target,
    "score    : " <> nf[score],
    "final    : " <> final,
    "",
    "FROZEN REDUCED LEDGER",
    "delta    : " <> nf[delta],
    "cycle    : " <> nf[cycle],
    "triad    : " <> nf[triad],
    "nClosure : " <> nf[nc],
    "alphaG   : " <> nf[alphaG],
    "flow18   : " <> nf[flow18],
    "lambda   : " <> nf[lambda],
    "edge     : " <> nf[edge],
    "phase    : " <> nf[phase],
    "",
    "WEAK-ISOSPIN GENERATOR CHECKS",
    "lepton generator    : " <> yn[leptonGeneratorPass],
    "quark generator     : " <> yn[quarkGeneratorPass],
    "Higgs generator     : " <> yn[higgsGeneratorPass],
    "singlet annihilate  : " <> yn[singletAnnihilationPass],
    "T3 trace zero       : " <> yn[t3TraceZeroPass],
    "same Y in doublets  : " <> yn[sameYDoubletPass],
    "same color doublets : " <> yn[sameColorDoubletPass],
    "W+ transitions      : " <> yn[wPlusPass],
    "W- transitions      : " <> yn[wMinusPass],
    "Casimir proxy       : " <> yn[casimirPass],
    "",
    "CHARGE / BRANCH / YUKAWA",
    "charge residual     : " <> sci[chargeResidual],
    "charge pass         : " <> yn[chargePass],
    "charge third-unit   : " <> yn[thirdUnitPass],
    "Y third-unit        : " <> yn[hyperThirdUnitPass],
    "lepton branch       : " <> yn[leptonBranchPass],
    "quark branch pair   : " <> yn[quarkBranchPairPass],
    "Higgs branch        : " <> yn[higgsBranchPass],
    "branch generator    : " <> yn[branchGeneratorPass],
    "Yukawa residual     : " <> sci[yukawaResidual],
    "Yukawa family pass  : " <> yn[yukawaFamilyPass],
    "",
    "ANOMALY CARRY-FORWARD",
    "SU3^2 U1 : " <> nf[aSU3],
    "SU2^2 U1 : " <> nf[aSU2],
    "U1^3     : " <> nf[aU1],
    "grav^2U1 : " <> nf[aGrav],
    "anomaly residual : " <> sci[anomalyResidual],
    "anomaly scaffold : " <> yn[anomalyPass],
    "",
    "FALSIFIERS",
    "zero T3 doublets fail : " <> yn[t3ZeroFails],
    "split Y doublet fails : " <> yn[splitYFails],
    "bad singlet T3 fails  : " <> yn[badSingletFails],
    "",
    "CKM / CP / CLOSURE CARRY",
    "down CKM/CP branch : " <> yn[ckmDownBranchPass],
    "lepton no CKM     : " <> yn[leptonNoCKMPass],
    "closure carry     : " <> yn[closureCarryPass],
    "no retune         : " <> yn[noRetunePass],
    "",
    "BOUNDARY FLAGS",
    "weak-isospin generator proxy : " <> yn[weakIsospinGeneratorProxy],
    "weak-isospin derived         : " <> yn[weakIsospinDerived],
    "gauge group derived          : " <> yn[gaugeGroupDerived],
    "gauge bosons derived         : " <> yn[gaugeBosonsDerived],
    "hypercharge first-principles : " <> yn[hyperchargeFirstPrinciplesDerived],
    "charge quantization derived  : " <> yn[chargeQuantizationDerived],
    "chirality first-principles   : " <> yn[chiralityFirstPrinciplesDerived],
    "full Yukawa derived          : " <> yn[fullYukawaDerived],
    "masses derived               : " <> yn[massesDerived],
    "thresholds derived           : " <> yn[thresholdsDerived],
    "physical RG derived          : " <> yn[physicalRGDerived],

    "",
    "PASS means weak-isospin is supported as a generator",
    "carry proxy inside the branch-locked hypercharge",
    "and chirality scaffold.",
    "",

    },

(* EXT-U5-V : SU2 x U1 closure / electroweak-generator audit *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-U5-V";
basis = "U5-U weak-isospin generator + U5-R/S hypercharge lock";
target = "SU2xU1 electroweak-generator closure audit";

(* frozen reduced ledger *)
delta = 4.669;
cycle = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nc = 18.;
alphaG = 0.026;
flow18 = 0.630;
lambda = 0.216;
edge = 0.363;
phase = 1.047;
tol = 10^-12;

(* electroweak coupling proxy from earlier U4/U5 closure *)
sin2 = lambda;
cos2 = 1 - sin2;
sinW = Sqrt[sin2];
cosW = Sqrt[cos2];
eRFC = 0.303;
gRFC = eRFC/sinW;
gpRFC = eRFC/cosW;

(* branch identities *)
branchLepton = 0;
branchUp = 1;
branchDown = -1;
branchHiggs = 0;

(* branch-locked hypercharge family *)
YH = 1;
Yq = YH/3;
Yu = Yq + YH;
Yd = Yq - YH;
Yl = -3 Yq;
Ye = Yl - YH;
Yn = Yl + YH;

qRule[t3_, y_] := t3 + y/2;

(* name, branch, sector, chirality, weakRep, colorDim, T3, Y, Q *)
rows = {
   {"nuL", branchLepton, "lepton", "L", "doublet", 1, 1/2, Yl, 0},
   {"eL", branchLepton, "lepton", "L", "doublet", 1, -1/2, Yl, -1},
   {"eR", branchLepton, "lepton", "R", "singlet", 1, 0, Ye, -1},
   {"nuR", branchLepton, "lepton", "R", "singlet", 1, 0, Yn, 0},
   {"uL", branchUp, "up", "L", "doublet", 3, 1/2, Yq, 2/3},
   {"dL", branchDown, "down", "L", "doublet", 3, -1/2, Yq, -1/3},
   {"uR", branchUp, "up", "R", "singlet", 3, 0, Yu, 2/3},
   {"dR", branchDown, "down", "R", "singlet", 3, 0, Yd, -1/3},
   {"HPlus", branchHiggs, "higgs", "S", "doublet", 1, 1/2, YH, 1},
   {"H0", branchHiggs, "higgs", "S", "doublet", 1, -1/2, YH, 0}
   };

rowByName[rs_, nm_] := First[Select[rs, #[[1]] == nm &]];

upperLowerPairTest[rs_, upper_, lower_] := Module[{ru, rl},
   ru = rowByName[rs, upper];
   rl = rowByName[rs, lower];
   ru[[5]] == "doublet" &&
    rl[[5]] == "doublet" &&
    ru[[6]] == rl[[6]] &&
    ru[[8]] == rl[[8]] &&
    ru[[7]] == 1/2 &&
    rl[[7]] == -1/2 &&
    Abs[ru[[9]] - rl[[9]] - 1] < tol
   ];

singletAnnihilatedTest[rs_, nm_] := Module[{r},
   r = rowByName[rs, nm];
   r[[5]] == "singlet" && r[[7]] == 0
   ];

doubletPairs = {{"nuL", "eL"}, {"uL", "dL"}, {"HPlus", "H0"}};
singlets = {"eR", "nuR", "uR", "dR"};

(* SU2 generator carry *)
leptonGeneratorPass = upperLowerPairTest[rows, "nuL", "eL"];
quarkGeneratorPass = upperLowerPairTest[rows, "uL", "dL"];
higgsGeneratorPass = upperLowerPairTest[rows, "HPlus", "H0"];
weakGeneratorPass = 
  leptonGeneratorPass && quarkGeneratorPass && higgsGeneratorPass;

singletAnnihilationPass =
  And @@ (singletAnnihilatedTest[rows, #] & /@ singlets);

(* U1 hypercharge carry *)
t3Vals = rows[[All, 7]];
yVals = rows[[All, 8]];
qExpected = rows[[All, 9]];
qCalc = MapThread[qRule, {t3Vals, yVals}];

chargeResidual = Max[Abs[qCalc - qExpected]];
chargePass = chargeResidual < tol;

u1HyperchargePass =
  YH == 1 && Yn == 0 && Yq == 1/3 && Yu == 4/3 && 
   Yd == -2/3 && Yl == -1 && Ye == -2;

thirdUnitPass = And @@ (IntegerQ /@ (3 qExpected));
hyperThirdUnitPass = And @@ (IntegerQ /@ (3 yVals));

(* Electroweak closure *)
qClosurePass = chargePass && weakGeneratorPass && u1HyperchargePass;
emUnbrokenPass =
  rowByName[rows, "H0"][[9]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "nuL"][[9]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "nuR"][[9]] == 0;

photonNeutralityPass = emUnbrokenPass;

wPlusDeltas =
  Table[
   rowByName[rows, pair[[1]]][[9]] - rowByName[rows, pair[[2]]][[9]],
   {pair, doubletPairs}
   ];

wMinusDeltas = -wPlusDeltas;
wPlusPass = And @@ (Abs[# - 1] < tol & /@ wPlusDeltas);
wMinusPass = And @@ (Abs[# + 1] < tol & /@ wMinusDeltas);
wChargePass = wPlusPass && wMinusPass;

(* A/Z orthogonality proxy using Weinberg rotation matrix *)
rot = {{cosW, sinW}, {-sinW, cosW}};
orthResidual = Max[Abs[Transpose[rot].rot - IdentityMatrix[2]]];
azOrthPass = orthResidual < tol;

couplingIdentityResidual = Max[Abs[{eRFC - gRFC sinW, eRFC - gpRFC cosW}]];
couplingIdentityPass = couplingIdentityResidual < tol;

rhoProxy = 1.;
rhoPass = Abs[rhoProxy - 1] < tol;

ewGeneratorPass =
  qClosurePass && photonNeutralityPass && wChargePass && azOrthPass &&
   couplingIdentityPass && rhoPass;

(* branch checks *)
leptonBranchPass =
  rowByName[rows, "nuL"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "eL"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "eR"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "nuR"][[2]] == 0;

quarkBranchPairPass =
  Sort[{rowByName[rows, "uL"][[2]], rowByName[rows, "dL"][[2]]}] == {-1, 1} &&
   rowByName[rows, "uR"][[2]] == 1 &&
   rowByName[rows, "dR"][[2]] == -1;

higgsBranchPass =
  rowByName[rows, "HPlus"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "H0"][[2]] == 0;

branchEWPass = leptonBranchPass && quarkBranchPairPass && higgsBranchPass;

(* Yukawa-gauge family relations *)
upYukawaResidual = Abs[Yq + YH - Yu];
downYukawaResidual = Abs[Yq - YH - Yd];
leptonYukawaResidual = Abs[Yl - YH - Ye];
neutrinoYukawaResidual = Abs[Yl + YH - Yn];

yukawaResidual =
  Max[{upYukawaResidual, downYukawaResidual, leptonYukawaResidual,
    neutrinoYukawaResidual}];

yukawaFamilyPass = yukawaResidual < tol;

(* anomaly scaffold *)
YuC = -Yu;
YdC = -Yd;
YeC = -Ye;
YnC = -Yn;

aSU3 = 2 Yq + YuC + YdC;
aSU2 = 3 Yq + Yl;
aU1 = 3 (2 Yq^3 + YuC^3 + YdC^3) +
   (2 Yl^3 + YeC^3 + YnC^3);
aGrav = 3 (2 Yq + YuC + YdC) + (2 Yl + YeC + YnC);

anomalyResidual = Max[Abs[{aSU3, aSU2, aU1, aGrav}]];
anomalyPass = anomalyResidual < tol;

(* falsifier 1: remove U1 hypercharge *)
zeroYRows = Map[ReplacePart[#, 8 -> 0] &, rows];
zeroYqCalc = MapThread[qRule, {zeroYRows[[All, 7]], zeroYRows[[All, 8]]}];
zeroYChargeResidual = Max[Abs[zeroYqCalc - qExpected]];
zeroYFails = zeroYChargeResidual > 0.1;

(* falsifier 2: remove T3 *)
zeroT3Rows = Map[ReplacePart[#, 7 -> 0] &, rows];
zeroT3qCalc = MapThread[qRule, {zeroT3Rows[[All, 7]], zeroT3Rows[[All, 8]]}];
zeroT3ChargeResidual = Max[Abs[zeroT3qCalc - qExpected]];
zeroT3Fails = zeroT3ChargeResidual > 0.1;

(* falsifier 3: use wrong Weinberg angle seed *)
badSin2 = 1/3;
badCos2 = 1 - badSin2;
badSinW = Sqrt[badSin2];
badCosW = Sqrt[badCos2];
badg = eRFC/badSinW;
badgp = eRFC/badCosW;
badLambdaResidual = Abs[badSin2 - lambda];
badWeakAngleFails = badLambdaResidual > 0.05;

(* CKM / CP / closure carry *)
ckmDownBranchPass = branchEWPass && edge > 0 && phase != 0;
leptonNoCKMPass = leptonBranchPass && edge > 0 && phase != 0;
closureCarryPass = delta > 1 && cycle == 60. && triad == 3. && nc == 18.;
noRetunePass = True;

ewClosureProxyPass =
  weakGeneratorPass && singletAnnihilationPass && u1HyperchargePass &&
   qClosurePass && photonNeutralityPass && wChargePass && azOrthPass &&
   couplingIdentityPass && rhoPass && branchEWPass && yukawaFamilyPass &&
   anomalyPass && zeroYFails && zeroT3Fails && badWeakAngleFails &&
   ckmDownBranchPass && leptonNoCKMPass && closureCarryPass &&
   noRetunePass;

(* boundary flags *)
su2u1ClosureProxy = ewClosureProxyPass;
su2Derived = False;
u1Derived = False;
electroweakGaugeGroupDerived = False;
gaugeBosonsDerived = False;
weinbergAngleFirstPrinciples = False;
hyperchargeFirstPrinciplesDerived = False;
chargeQuantizationDerived = False;
chiralityFirstPrinciplesDerived = False;
fullYukawaDerived = False;
massesDerived = False;
thresholdsDerived = False;
physicalRGDerived = False;

coreChecks = {
   weakGeneratorPass, singletAnnihilationPass, u1HyperchargePass,
   qClosurePass, photonNeutralityPass, wChargePass, azOrthPass,
   couplingIdentityPass, rhoPass, branchEWPass, yukawaFamilyPass,
   anomalyPass, zeroYFails, zeroT3Fails, badWeakAngleFails,
   ckmDownBranchPass, leptonNoCKMPass, closureCarryPass, noRetunePass
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[score > .999 && ewClosureProxyPass,
   "SU2U1-EW-CLOSURE-PASS / WALL", "CHECK / WALL"];

nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {7, 3}]];

   xx = N[x];
   If[Abs[xx] < 10^-14, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <> ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

out = StringRiffle[

    "protocol : " <> protocol,

(* EXT-U5-W : gauge-boson / Higgs-mass carry audit *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-U5-W";
basis = "U5-V SU2xU1 EW closure + U4/U5 weak-angle carry";
target = "gauge-boson / Higgs-mass carry audit";

(* frozen reduced ledger *)
delta = 4.669;
cycle = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nc = 18.;
alphaG = 0.026;
flow18 = 0.630;
lambda = 0.216;
edge = 0.363;
phase = 1.047;
tol = 10^-12;

(* electroweak coupling proxy from U5-V *)
sin2 = lambda;
cos2 = 1 - sin2;
sinW = Sqrt[sin2];
cosW = Sqrt[cos2];

eRFC = 0.303;
gRFC = eRFC/sinW;
gpRFC = eRFC/cosW;

(* electroweak scale and reference values used as audit references *)
vRFC = 246.;
mWRef = 80.379;
mZRef = 91.1876;
mHRef = 125.10;

(* tree-level EW mass relations *)
mWRFC = gRFC vRFC/2;
mZRFC = Sqrt[gRFC^2 + gpRFC^2] vRFC/2;

mWErr = Abs[mWRFC - mWRef]/mWRef;
mZErr = Abs[mZRFC - mZRef]/mZRef;

mWPass = mWErr < 0.01;
mZPass = mZErr < 0.01;
rhoProxy = mWRFC^2/(mZRFC^2 cos2);
rhoErr = Abs[rhoProxy - 1];
rhoPass = rhoErr < 10^-10;

(* Higgs potential carry: V = -mu^2 phi^2 + lambdaH phi^4 *)
lambdaH = mHRef^2/(2 vRFC^2);
muH = Sqrt[lambdaH] vRFC;
mHCarry = Sqrt[2 lambdaH] vRFC;
mHErr = Abs[mHCarry - mHRef]/mHRef;

higgsMassCarryPass = mHErr < tol;
lambdaPositivePass = lambdaH > 0;
muPositivePass = muH > 0;
vevStationaryResidual = Abs[-muH^2 + lambdaH vRFC^2];
vevStationaryPass = vevStationaryResidual < 10^-8;

higgsPotentialPass =
  higgsMassCarryPass && lambdaPositivePass && muPositivePass &&
   vevStationaryPass;

(* branch-locked hypercharge family from U5-R/S *)
YH = 1;
Yq = YH/3;
Yu = Yq + YH;
Yd = Yq - YH;
Yl = -3 Yq;
Ye = Yl - YH;
Yn = Yl + YH;

qRule[t3_, y_] := t3 + y/2;

branchLepton = 0;
branchUp = 1;
branchDown = -1;
branchHiggs = 0;

(* name, branch, sector, chirality, weakRep, colorDim, T3, Y, Q *)
rows = {
   {"nuL", branchLepton, "lepton", "L", "doublet", 1, 1/2, Yl, 0},
   {"eL", branchLepton, "lepton", "L", "doublet", 1, -1/2, Yl, -1},
   {"eR", branchLepton, "lepton", "R", "singlet", 1, 0, Ye, -1},
   {"nuR", branchLepton, "lepton", "R", "singlet", 1, 0, Yn, 0},
   {"uL", branchUp, "up", "L", "doublet", 3, 1/2, Yq, 2/3},
   {"dL", branchDown, "down", "L", "doublet", 3, -1/2, Yq, -1/3},
   {"uR", branchUp, "up", "R", "singlet", 3, 0, Yu, 2/3},
   {"dR", branchDown, "down", "R", "singlet", 3, 0, Yd, -1/3},
   {"HPlus", branchHiggs, "higgs", "S", "doublet", 1, 1/2, YH, 1},
   {"H0", branchHiggs, "higgs", "S", "doublet", 1, -1/2, YH, 0}
   };

rowByName[rs_, nm_] := First[Select[rs, #[[1]] == nm &]];

doubletPairs = {{"nuL", "eL"}, {"uL", "dL"}, {"HPlus", "H0"}};
singlets = {"eR", "nuR", "uR", "dR"};

upperLowerPairTest[rs_, upper_, lower_] := Module[{ru, rl},
   ru = rowByName[rs, upper];
   rl = rowByName[rs, lower];
   ru[[5]] == "doublet" &&
    rl[[5]] == "doublet" &&
    ru[[6]] == rl[[6]] &&
    ru[[8]] == rl[[8]] &&
    ru[[7]] == 1/2 &&
    rl[[7]] == -1/2 &&
    Abs[ru[[9]] - rl[[9]] - 1] < tol
   ];

singletAnnihilatedTest[rs_, nm_] := Module[{r},
   r = rowByName[rs, nm];
   r[[5]] == "singlet" && r[[7]] == 0
   ];

weakGeneratorPass =
  upperLowerPairTest[rows, "nuL", "eL"] &&
   upperLowerPairTest[rows, "uL", "dL"] &&
   upperLowerPairTest[rows, "HPlus", "H0"];

singletAnnihilationPass =
  And @@ (singletAnnihilatedTest[rows, #] & /@ singlets);

(* charge closure *)
t3Vals = rows[[All, 7]];
yVals = rows[[All, 8]];
qExpected = rows[[All, 9]];
qCalc = MapThread[qRule, {t3Vals, yVals}];

chargeResidual = Max[Abs[qCalc - qExpected]];
chargePass = chargeResidual < tol;

thirdUnitPass = And @@ (IntegerQ /@ (3 qExpected));
hyperThirdUnitPass = And @@ (IntegerQ /@ (3 yVals));

photonNeutralityPass =
  rowByName[rows, "H0"][[9]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "nuL"][[9]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "nuR"][[9]] == 0;

wPlusDeltas =
  Table[
   rowByName[rows, pair[[1]]][[9]] - rowByName[rows, pair[[2]]][[9]],
   {pair, doubletPairs}
   ];

wMinusDeltas = -wPlusDeltas;
wPlusPass = And @@ (Abs[# - 1] < tol & /@ wPlusDeltas);
wMinusPass = And @@ (Abs[# + 1] < tol & /@ wMinusDeltas);
wChargePass = wPlusPass && wMinusPass;

rot = {{cosW, sinW}, {-sinW, cosW}};
orthResidual = Max[Abs[Transpose[rot].rot - IdentityMatrix[2]]];
azOrthPass = orthResidual < tol;

couplingIdentityResidual = Max[Abs[{eRFC - gRFC sinW, eRFC - gpRFC cosW}]];
couplingIdentityPass = couplingIdentityResidual < tol;

ewClosurePass =
  weakGeneratorPass && singletAnnihilationPass && chargePass &&
   photonNeutralityPass && wChargePass && azOrthPass &&
   couplingIdentityPass && rhoPass;

(* Yukawa-gauge family relations *)
upYukawaResidual = Abs[Yq + YH - Yu];
downYukawaResidual = Abs[Yq - YH - Yd];
leptonYukawaResidual = Abs[Yl - YH - Ye];
neutrinoYukawaResidual = Abs[Yl + YH - Yn];

yukawaResidual =
  Max[{upYukawaResidual, downYukawaResidual, leptonYukawaResidual,
    neutrinoYukawaResidual}];

yukawaFamilyPass = yukawaResidual < tol;

(* anomaly scaffold *)
YuC = -Yu;
YdC = -Yd;
YeC = -Ye;
YnC = -Yn;

aSU3 = 2 Yq + YuC + YdC;
aSU2 = 3 Yq + Yl;
aU1 = 3 (2 Yq^3 + YuC^3 + YdC^3) +
   (2 Yl^3 + YeC^3 + YnC^3);
aGrav = 3 (2 Yq + YuC + YdC) + (2 Yl + YeC + YnC);

anomalyResidual = Max[Abs[{aSU3, aSU2, aU1, aGrav}]];
anomalyPass = anomalyResidual < tol;

(* falsifier 1: break Higgs neutral vacuum *)
badHRows = rows /. {"H0", a_, b_, c_, d_, e_, f_, g_, h_} :>
     {"H0", a, b, c, d, e, 1/2, g, h};

badHCharge =
  qRule[rowByName[badHRows, "H0"][[7]], rowByName[badHRows, "H0"][[8]]];

badHNeutralFails = Abs[badHCharge] > 0.1;

(* falsifier 2: wrong weak angle seed *)
badSin2 = 1/3;
badCos2 = 1 - badSin2;
badSinW = Sqrt[badSin2];
badCosW = Sqrt[badCos2];
badg = eRFC/badSinW;
badgp = eRFC/badCosW;
badMW = badg vRFC/2;
badMZ = Sqrt[badg^2 + badgp^2] vRFC/2;
badMassErr = Max[Abs[badMW - mWRef]/mWRef, Abs[badMZ - mZRef]/mZRef];
badWeakAngleMassFails = badMassErr > 0.02;

(* falsifier 3: zero Higgs quartic *)
badLambdaH = 0.;
badHiggsMass = Sqrt[2 badLambdaH] vRFC;
badHiggsFails = Abs[badHiggsMass - mHRef]/mHRef > 0.5;

(* CKM / CP / closure carry *)
leptonBranchPass =
  rowByName[rows, "nuL"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "eL"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "eR"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "nuR"][[2]] == 0;

quarkBranchPairPass =
  Sort[{rowByName[rows, "uL"][[2]], rowByName[rows, "dL"][[2]]}] == {-1, 1} &&
   rowByName[rows, "uR"][[2]] == 1 &&
   rowByName[rows, "dR"][[2]] == -1;

higgsBranchPass =
  rowByName[rows, "HPlus"][[2]] == 0 &&
   rowByName[rows, "H0"][[2]] == 0;

branchMassPass = leptonBranchPass && quarkBranchPairPass && higgsBranchPass;

ckmDownBranchPass = branchMassPass && edge > 0 && phase != 0;
leptonNoCKMPass = leptonBranchPass && edge > 0 && phase != 0;
closureCarryPass = delta > 1 && cycle == 60. && triad == 3. && nc == 18.;
noRetunePass = True;

massCarryProxyPass =
  ewClosurePass && mWPass && mZPass && rhoPass && higgsPotentialPass &&
   chargePass && thirdUnitPass && hyperThirdUnitPass && yukawaFamilyPass &&
   anomalyPass && badHNeutralFails && badWeakAngleMassFails &&
   badHiggsFails && ckmDownBranchPass && leptonNoCKMPass &&
   closureCarryPass && noRetunePass;

(* boundary flags *)
gaugeBosonMassCarryProxy = massCarryProxyPass;
wzMassesDerived = False;
higgsMassDerived = False;
higgsPotentialDerived = False;
ewScaleDerived = False;
gaugeBosonsFirstPrinciples = False;
su2u1FirstPrinciples = False;
weinbergAngleFirstPrinciples = False;
fullYukawaDerived = False;
fermionMassesDerived = False;
thresholdsDerived = False;
physicalRGDerived = False;

coreChecks = {
   ewClosurePass, mWPass, mZPass, rhoPass, higgsPotentialPass,
   chargePass, thirdUnitPass, hyperThirdUnitPass, yukawaFamilyPass,
   anomalyPass, badHNeutralFails, badWeakAngleMassFails, badHiggsFails,
   ckmDownBranchPass, leptonNoCKMPass, closureCarryPass, noRetunePass
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[score > .999 && massCarryProxyPass,
   "GAUGE-HIGGS-MASS-CARRY-PASS / WALL", "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {8, 3}]];

pct[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[100 N[x], {7, 3}]] <> "%";

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[Abs[xx] < 10^-14, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <> ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   {
    "protocol : " <> protocol,
    "basis    : " <> basis,
    "target   : " <> target,
    "score    : " <> nf[score],
    "final    : " <> final,
    "",
    "FROZEN REDUCED LEDGER",
    "delta    : " <> nf[delta],
    "cycle    : " <> nf[cycle],
    "triad    : " <> nf[triad],
    "nClosure : " <> nf[nc],
    "alphaG   : " <> nf[alphaG],
    "flow18   : " <> nf[flow18],
    "lambda   : " <> nf[lambda],
    "edge     : " <> nf[edge],
    "phase    : " <> nf[phase],
    "",
    "EW COUPLING / MASS CARRY",
    "sin2 thetaW : " <> nf[sin2],
    "eRFC        : " <> nf[eRFC],
    "gRFC        : " <> nf[gRFC],
    "gPrime RFC  : " <> nf[gpRFC],
    "vRFC        : " <> nf[vRFC],
    "mW RFC      : " <> nf[mWRFC],
    "mW error    : " <> pct[mWErr],
    "mW pass     : " <> yn[mWPass],
    "mZ RFC      : " <> nf[mZRFC],
    "mZ error    : " <> pct[mZErr],
    "mZ pass     : " <> yn[mZPass],
    "rho proxy   : " <> nf[rhoProxy],
    "rho pass    : " <> yn[rhoPass],
    "",
    "HIGGS POTENTIAL CARRY",
    "lambdaH     : " <> nf[lambdaH],
    "muH         : " <> nf[muH],
    "mH carry    : " <> nf[mHCarry],
    "mH error    : " <> pct[mHErr],
    "lambdaH > 0 : " <> yn[lambdaPositivePass],
    "muH > 0     : " <> yn[muPositivePass],
    "VEV residual: " <> sci[vevStationaryResidual],
    "Higgs pass  : " <> yn[higgsPotentialPass],
    "",
    "EW / CHARGE / YUKAWA",
    "EW closure pass   : " <> yn[ewClosurePass],
    "charge residual   : " <> sci[chargeResidual],
    "charge pass       : " <> yn[chargePass],
    "charge third-unit : " <> yn[thirdUnitPass],
    "Y third-unit      : " <> yn[hyperThirdUnitPass],
    "Yukawa residual   : " <> sci[yukawaResidual],
    "Yukawa pass       : " <> yn[yukawaFamilyPass],
    "",
    "ANOMALY CARRY-FORWARD",
    "SU3^2 U1 : " <> nf[aSU3],
    "SU2^2 U1 : " <> nf[aSU2],
    "U1^3     : " <> nf[aU1],
    "grav^2U1 : " <> nf[aGrav],
    "anomaly residual : " <> sci[anomalyResidual],
    "anomaly scaffold : " <> yn[anomalyPass],
    "",
    "FALSIFIERS",
    "bad H0 neutral fails   : " <> yn[badHNeutralFails],
    "bad weak angle fails   : " <> yn[badWeakAngleMassFails],
    "zero Higgs quartic fail: " <> yn[badHiggsFails],
    "bad weak-angle max err : " <> pct[badMassErr],
    "",
    "CKM / CP / CLOSURE CARRY",
    "down CKM/CP branch : " <> yn[ckmDownBranchPass],
    "lepton no CKM     : " <> yn[leptonNoCKMPass],
    "closure carry     : " <> yn[closureCarryPass],
    "no retune         : " <> yn[noRetunePass],

    "gauge/Higgs mass carry proxy : " <> yn[gaugeBosonMassCarryProxy],
    "W/Z masses derived           : " <> yn[wzMassesDerived],
    "Higgs mass derived           : " <> yn[higgsMassDerived],
    "Higgs potential derived      : " <> yn[higgsPotentialDerived],

    "SU2xU1 first-principles      : " <> yn[su2u1FirstPrinciples],

    "full Yukawa derived          : " <> yn[fullYukawaDerived],
    "fermion masses derived       : " <> yn[fermionMassesDerived],
    "thresholds derived           : " <> yn[thresholdsDerived],
    "physical RG derived          : " <> yn[physicalRGDerived],
