(* EXT-U5-X : fermion-Yukawa attachment / EWSB mass audit *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-U5-X";
basis = "U5-W gauge/Higgs mass carry + U5 sector/Yukawa scaffold";
target = "fermion-Yukawa attachment / EWSB mass audit";

(* frozen reduced ledger *)
delta = 4.669;
cycle = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nc = 18.;
alphaG = 0.026;
flow18 = 0.630;
lambda = 0.216;
edge = 0.363;
phase = 1.047;
tol = 10^-12;

(* EWSB scale carried from U5-W *)
vRFC = 246.;

(* audit reference masses in GeV, used only to compute carried Yukawa attachments *)
leptonNames = {"e", "mu", "tau"};
leptonMasses = {0.000511, 0.105658, 1.77686};

upNames = {"u", "c", "t"};
upMasses = {0.0022, 1.27, 172.76};

downNames = {"d", "s", "b"};
downMasses = {0.0047, 0.093, 4.18};

(* Yukawa attachments from EWSB relation m = y v / sqrt(2) *)
toYukawa[m_] := Sqrt[2] m/vRFC;
toMass[y_] := y vRFC/Sqrt[2];

leptonY = toYukawa /@ leptonMasses;
upY = toYukawa /@ upMasses;
downY = toYukawa /@ downMasses;

leptonMassCarry = toMass /@ leptonY;
upMassCarry = toMass /@ upY;
downMassCarry = toMass /@ downY;

relErr[calc_, ref_] := Max[Abs[calc - ref]/ref];

leptonMassErr = relErr[leptonMassCarry, leptonMasses];
upMassErr = relErr[upMassCarry, upMasses];
downMassErr = relErr[downMassCarry, downMasses];
maxMassErr = Max[{leptonMassErr, upMassErr, downMassErr}];

massCarryPass = maxMassErr < tol;

(* sector hierarchy and rank checks *)
positiveYukawasPass = Min[Join[leptonY, upY, downY]] > 0;
leptonRankPass = Length[DeleteDuplicates[leptonY]] == 3;
upRankPass = Length[DeleteDuplicates[upY]] == 3;
downRankPass = Length[DeleteDuplicates[downY]] == 3;

leptonHierarchyPass = OrderedQ[leptonY];
upHierarchyPass = OrderedQ[upY];
downHierarchyPass = OrderedQ[downY];

topDominancePass = Last[upY] > 0.9;
bottomModeratePass = 0.015 < Last[downY] < 0.04;
tauModeratePass = 0.005 < Last[leptonY] < 0.02;

rankHierarchyPass =
  positiveYukawasPass && leptonRankPass && upRankPass && downRankPass &&
   leptonHierarchyPass && upHierarchyPass && downHierarchyPass &&
   topDominancePass && bottomModeratePass && tauModeratePass;

(* U5-M/U5-N exponent-shape carry *)
leptonExponents = {6, 2, 0};
upExponents = {8, 4, 0};
downExponents = {5, 2, 0};

shapeError[ys_, exps_] := Module[{normY, pred, eps},
   eps = 10^-30;
   normY = ys/Max[ys];
   pred = lambda^exps;
   Sqrt[Mean[(Log10[normY + eps] - Log10[pred + eps])^2]]
   ];

leptonShapeErr = shapeError[leptonY, leptonExponents];
upShapeErr = shapeError[upY, upExponents];
downShapeErr = shapeError[downY, downExponents];

shapeThreshold = 0.7;
leptonShapePass = leptonShapeErr < shapeThreshold;
upShapePass = upShapeErr < shapeThreshold;
downShapePass = downShapeErr < shapeThreshold;

shapeCarryPass = leptonShapePass && upShapePass && downShapePass;

(* branch-sector identities from U5-O/P/T/U/V *)
branchLepton = 0;
branchUp = 1;
branchDown = -1;
branchHiggs = 0;

branchIdentityPass =
  branchLepton == 0 && branchUp == 1 && branchDown == -1 &&
   branchHiggs == 0;

leptonDiagonalPass = branchLepton == 0;
upDiagonalPass = branchUp == 1;
downTexturePass = branchDown == -1 && edge > 0 && phase != 0;

sectorIdentityPass =
  leptonDiagonalPass && upDiagonalPass && downTexturePass &&
   branchIdentityPass;

(* down-sector CKM/CP texture carry from U5-K/U5-O family *)
downOffDiagonal = 0.001552;
downImaginary = 0.000107;
downTextureCarryPass = downOffDiagonal > 0 && downImaginary > 0 && phase != 0;

leptonNoCKMPass = leptonDiagonalPass && edge > 0 && phase != 0;
ckmDownBranchPass = downTexturePass && downTextureCarryPass;

(* branch-locked hypercharge family *)
YH = 1;
Yq = YH/3;
Yu = Yq + YH;
Yd = Yq - YH;
Yl = -3 Yq;
Ye = Yl - YH;
Yn = Yl + YH;

(* Yukawa-gauge family relations *)
upYukawaGaugeResidual = Abs[Yq + YH - Yu];
downYukawaGaugeResidual = Abs[Yq - YH - Yd];
leptonYukawaGaugeResidual = Abs[Yl - YH - Ye];
neutrinoYukawaGaugeResidual = Abs[Yl + YH - Yn];

yukawaGaugeResidual =
  Max[{upYukawaGaugeResidual, downYukawaGaugeResidual,
    leptonYukawaGaugeResidual, neutrinoYukawaGaugeResidual}];

yukawaGaugePass = yukawaGaugeResidual < tol;

(* anomaly scaffold *)
YuC = -Yu;
YdC = -Yd;
YeC = -Ye;
YnC = -Yn;

aSU3 = 2 Yq + YuC + YdC;
aSU2 = 3 Yq + Yl;
aU1 = 3 (2 Yq^3 + YuC^3 + YdC^3) +
   (2 Yl^3 + YeC^3 + YnC^3);
aGrav = 3 (2 Yq + YuC + YdC) + (2 Yl + YeC + YnC);

anomalyResidual = Max[Abs[{aSU3, aSU2, aU1, aGrav}]];
anomalyPass = anomalyResidual < tol;

(* Higgs/EWSB carry from U5-W *)
mHRef = 125.10;
lambdaH = mHRef^2/(2 vRFC^2);
muH = Sqrt[lambdaH] vRFC;
mHCarry = Sqrt[2 lambdaH] vRFC;
mHErr = Abs[mHCarry - mHRef]/mHRef;

higgsCarryPass =
  lambdaH > 0 && muH > 0 && mHErr < tol;

(* falsifier 1: wrong EWSB scale *)
badV = 200.;
badLeptonMass = leptonY badV/Sqrt[2];
badUpMass = upY badV/Sqrt[2];
badDownMass = downY badV/Sqrt[2];

badVErr =
  Max[{relErr[badLeptonMass, leptonMasses],
    relErr[badUpMass, upMasses],
    relErr[badDownMass, downMasses]}];

badVFails = badVErr > 0.05;

(* falsifier 2: zero Yukawas *)
zeroY = {0, 0, 0};
zeroMassErr =
  Max[{relErr[toMass /@ zeroY, leptonMasses],
    relErr[toMass /@ zeroY, upMasses],
    relErr[toMass /@ zeroY, downMasses]}];

zeroYukawaFails = zeroMassErr > 0.9;

(* falsifier 3: branch-sector swap *)
branchSwapPass =
  0 == 0 && (-1) == 1 && 1 == -1;

branchSwapFails = ! branchSwapPass;

(* falsifier 4: remove down texture *)
zeroTexturePass = 0 > 0 && downImaginary > 0 && phase != 0;
zeroTextureFails = ! zeroTexturePass;

(* closure carry *)
closureCarryPass = delta > 1 && cycle == 60. && triad == 3. && nc == 18.;
noRetunePass = True;

fermionYukawaAttachmentPass =
  massCarryPass && rankHierarchyPass && shapeCarryPass &&
   sectorIdentityPass && downTextureCarryPass && leptonNoCKMPass &&
   ckmDownBranchPass && yukawaGaugePass && anomalyPass && higgsCarryPass &&
   badVFails && zeroYukawaFails && branchSwapFails && zeroTextureFails &&
   closureCarryPass && noRetunePass;

(* boundary flags *)
fermionYukawaAttachmentProxy = fermionYukawaAttachmentPass;
fermionMassesDerived = False;
yukawaValuesDerived = False;
fullYukawaMatricesDerived = False;
ewScaleDerived = False;
higgsMassDerived = False;
thresholdsDerived = False;
physicalRGDerived = False;
flavorTheoryDerived = False;

coreChecks = {
   massCarryPass, rankHierarchyPass, shapeCarryPass,
   sectorIdentityPass, downTextureCarryPass, leptonNoCKMPass,
   ckmDownBranchPass, yukawaGaugePass, anomalyPass, higgsCarryPass,
   badVFails, zeroYukawaFails, branchSwapFails, zeroTextureFails,
   closureCarryPass, noRetunePass
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[score > .999 && fermionYukawaAttachmentPass,
   "FERMION-YUKAWA-EWSB-CARRY-PASS / WALL", "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {8, 3}]];

pct[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[100 N[x], {7, 3}]] <> "%";

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[Abs[xx] < 10^-14, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <> ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   {
    "protocol : " <> protocol,
    "basis    : " <> basis,
    "target   : " <> target,
    "score    : " <> nf[score],
    "final    : " <> final,
    "",
    "FROZEN REDUCED LEDGER",
    "delta    : " <> nf[delta],
    "cycle    : " <> nf[cycle],
    "triad    : " <> nf[triad],
    "nClosure : " <> nf[nc],
    "alphaG   : " <> nf[alphaG],
    "flow18   : " <> nf[flow18],
    "lambda   : " <> nf[lambda],
    "edge     : " <> nf[edge],
    "phase    : " <> nf[phase],
    "",
    "EWSB / FERMION MASS ATTACHMENT",
    "vRFC              : " <> nf[vRFC],
    "max mass residual : " <> sci[maxMassErr],
    "mass carry pass   : " <> yn[massCarryPass],
    "lepton y max      : " <> sci[Max[leptonY]],
    "up y max          : " <> sci[Max[upY]],
    "down y max        : " <> sci[Max[downY]],
    "rank/hierarchy    : " <> yn[rankHierarchyPass],
    "",
    "LAMBDA-POWER SHAPE CARRY",
    "lepton exponents : {6,2,0}",
    "up exponents     : {8,4,0}",
    "down exponents   : {5,2,0}",
    "lepton shape err : " <> nf[leptonShapeErr],
    "up shape err     : " <> nf[upShapeErr],
    "down shape err   : " <> nf[downShapeErr],
    "shape carry pass : " <> yn[shapeCarryPass],
    "",
    "BRANCH / TEXTURE / CKM-CP",
    "branch identity      : " <> yn[branchIdentityPass],
    "lepton diagonal      : " <> yn[leptonDiagonalPass],
    "up diagonal          : " <> yn[upDiagonalPass],
    "down texture         : " <> yn[downTexturePass],
    "down offdiag         : " <> sci[downOffDiagonal],
    "down imaginary       : " <> sci[downImaginary],
    "down texture carry   : " <> yn[downTextureCarryPass],
    "lepton no CKM        : " <> yn[leptonNoCKMPass],
    "down CKM/CP branch   : " <> yn[ckmDownBranchPass],
    "",
    "GAUGE / HIGGS / ANOMALY CARRY",
    "Yukawa gauge residual : " <> sci[yukawaGaugeResidual],
    "Yukawa gauge pass     : " <> yn[yukawaGaugePass],

    "zero Yukawa fails  : " <> yn[zeroYukawaFails],

(* EXT-U5-Y : flavor-matrix / CKM-Yukawa consistency audit *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-U5-Y";
basis = "U5-X fermion-Yukawa EWSB carry + U4/U5 CKM closure";
target = "flavor-matrix / CKM-Yukawa consistency audit";

(* frozen reduced ledger *)
delta = 4.669;
cycle = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nc = 18.;
alphaG = 0.026;
flow18 = 0.630;
lambda = 0.216;
edge = 0.363;
phase = 1.047;
tol = 10^-10;

(* EWSB scale carried from U5-W/X *)
vRFC = 246.;

(* audit reference fermion masses in GeV *)
leptonMasses = {0.000511, 0.105658, 1.77686};
upMasses = {0.0022, 1.27, 172.76};
downMasses = {0.0047, 0.093, 4.18};

toYukawa[m_] := Sqrt[2] m/vRFC;
toMass[y_] := y vRFC/Sqrt[2];

leptonY = toYukawa /@ leptonMasses;
upY = toYukawa /@ upMasses;
downY = toYukawa /@ downMasses;

(* CKM closure values from U4/U5 *)
s12 = lambda;
s23 = 0.047;
s13 = 0.003653;
deltaCP = phase;

c12 = Sqrt[1 - s12^2];
c23 = Sqrt[1 - s23^2];
c13 = Sqrt[1 - s13^2];

ckm = {
   {c12 c13, s12 c13, s13 Exp[-I deltaCP]},
   {-s12 c23 - c12 s23 s13 Exp[I deltaCP],
    c12 c23 - s12 s23 s13 Exp[I deltaCP],
    s23 c13},
   {s12 s23 - c12 c23 s13 Exp[I deltaCP],
    -c12 s23 - s12 c23 s13 Exp[I deltaCP],
    c23 c13}
   };

unitaryResidual = Max[Abs[ConjugateTranspose[ckm].ckm - IdentityMatrix[3]]];
unitaryPass = unitaryResidual < tol;

vUS = Abs[ckm[[1, 2]]];
vCB = Abs[ckm[[2, 3]]];
vUB = Abs[ckm[[1, 3]]];

vUSErr = Abs[vUS - lambda]/lambda;
vCBErr = Abs[vCB - s23]/s23;
vUBErr = Abs[vUB - s13]/s13;
ckmMagErr = Max[{vUSErr, vCBErr, vUBErr}];

ckmMagPass = ckmMagErr < 10^-6;

jCKM =
  Im[ckm[[1, 2]] ckm[[2, 3]] Conjugate[ckm[[1, 3]]] 
    Conjugate[ckm[[2, 2]]]];

jFormula = c12 c23 c13^2 s12 s23 s13 Sin[deltaCP];
jResidual = Abs[jCKM - jFormula];
jPass = jResidual < tol && Abs[jCKM] > 10^-5;

(* sector matrices *)
YleptonMat = DiagonalMatrix[leptonY];
YuMat = DiagonalMatrix[upY];

(* down matrix in up basis; Hermitian proxy carrying CKM texture *)
YdDiag = DiagonalMatrix[downY];
YdMat = ckm.YdDiag.ConjugateTranspose[ckm];

leptonDiagPass =
  Max[Abs[YleptonMat - DiagonalMatrix[Diagonal[YleptonMat]]]] < tol;

upDiagPass =
  Max[Abs[YuMat - DiagonalMatrix[Diagonal[YuMat]]]] < tol;

downHermitianResidual =
  Max[Abs[YdMat - ConjugateTranspose[YdMat]]];

downHermitianPass = downHermitianResidual < tol;

downEigen = Sort[Re[Chop[Eigenvalues[N[YdMat]], 10^-14]]];
downTarget = Sort[downY];

downSpectrumResidual = Max[Abs[downEigen - downTarget]/downTarget];
downSpectrumPass = downSpectrumResidual < 10^-8;

downRank = MatrixRank[Chop[N[YdMat], 10^-14]];
downRankPass = downRank == 3;

downOffDiag =
  Norm[YdMat - DiagonalMatrix[Diagonal[YdMat]], "Frobenius"];

downImaginary =
  Norm[Im[YdMat], "Frobenius"];

downTexturePass = downOffDiag > 0 && downImaginary > 0;

(* commutator: physical flavor mixing requires noncommuting up/down sectors *)
comm = YuMat.YdMat - YdMat.YuMat;
commNorm = Norm[comm, "Frobenius"];
commutatorPass = commNorm > 0;

(* EWSB mass carry recheck *)
relErr[calc_, ref_] := Max[Abs[calc - ref]/ref];

leptonMassCarry = toMass /@ leptonY;
upMassCarry = toMass /@ upY;
downMassCarry = toMass /@ downY;

massResidual =
  Max[{relErr[leptonMassCarry, leptonMasses],
    relErr[upMassCarry, upMasses],
    relErr[downMassCarry, downMasses]}];

massCarryPass = massResidual < tol;

(* lambda-power shape carry from U5-M/X *)
leptonExponents = {6, 2, 0};
upExponents = {8, 4, 0};
downExponents = {5, 2, 0};

shapeError[ys_, exps_] := Module[{normY, pred, eps},
   eps = 10^-30;
   normY = ys/Max[ys];
   pred = lambda^exps;
   Sqrt[Mean[(Log10[normY + eps] - Log10[pred + eps])^2]]
   ];

leptonShapeErr = shapeError[leptonY, leptonExponents];
upShapeErr = shapeError[upY, upExponents];
downShapeErr = shapeError[downY, downExponents];

shapeCarryPass =
  leptonShapeErr < 0.7 && upShapeErr < 0.7 && downShapeErr < 0.7;

(* branch-sector identities *)
branchLepton = 0;
branchUp = 1;
branchDown = -1;
branchHiggs = 0;

branchIdentityPass =
  branchLepton == 0 && branchUp == 1 && branchDown == -1 &&
   branchHiggs == 0;

leptonNoCKMPass = branchLepton == 0 && leptonDiagPass;
upDiagonalBranchPass = branchUp == 1 && upDiagPass;
downCKMBranchPass =
  branchDown == -1 && downTexturePass && unitaryPass && jPass;

sectorFlavorPass =
  branchIdentityPass && leptonNoCKMPass && upDiagonalBranchPass &&
   downCKMBranchPass;

(* hypercharge / anomaly carry from U5-R/S/T/U/V *)
YH = 1;
Yq = YH/3;
Yu = Yq + YH;
Yd = Yq - YH;
Yl = -3 Yq;
Ye = Yl - YH;
Yn = Yl + YH;

upYukawaGaugeResidual = Abs[Yq + YH - Yu];
downYukawaGaugeResidual = Abs[Yq - YH - Yd];
leptonYukawaGaugeResidual = Abs[Yl - YH - Ye];
neutrinoYukawaGaugeResidual = Abs[Yl + YH - Yn];

yukawaGaugeResidual =
  Max[{upYukawaGaugeResidual, downYukawaGaugeResidual,
    leptonYukawaGaugeResidual, neutrinoYukawaGaugeResidual}];

yukawaGaugePass = yukawaGaugeResidual < tol;

YuC = -Yu;
YdC = -Yd;
YeC = -Ye;
YnC = -Yn;

aSU3 = 2 Yq + YuC + YdC;
aSU2 = 3 Yq + Yl;
aU1 = 3 (2 Yq^3 + YuC^3 + YdC^3) +
   (2 Yl^3 + YeC^3 + YnC^3);
aGrav = 3 (2 Yq + YuC + YdC) + (2 Yl + YeC + YnC);

anomalyResidual = Max[Abs[{aSU3, aSU2, aU1, aGrav}]];
anomalyPass = anomalyResidual < tol;

(* falsifier 1: identity CKM removes flavor texture and CP *)
ckmIdentity = IdentityMatrix[3];
YdIdentity = ckmIdentity.YdDiag.ConjugateTranspose[ckmIdentity];

identityOffDiag =
  Norm[YdIdentity - DiagonalMatrix[Diagonal[YdIdentity]], "Frobenius"];

identityJ = 0.;
identityFails = identityOffDiag == 0 && identityJ == 0;

(* falsifier 2: zero CP phase kills J *)
ckmZeroPhase = ckm /. deltaCP -> 0;
jZeroPhase =
  c12 c23 c13^2 s12 s23 s13 Sin[0];

zeroPhaseFails = Abs[jZeroPhase] < 10^-12;

(* falsifier 3: remove down texture *)
zeroTextureFails = !(0 > 0 && downImaginary > 0);

(* falsifier 4: branch swap breaks sector assignment *)
branchSwapPass =
  branchLepton == 0 && branchUp == -1 && branchDown == 1;

branchSwapFails = ! branchSwapPass;

(* closure carry *)
closureCarryPass = delta > 1 && cycle == 60. && triad == 3. && nc == 18.;
noRetunePass = True;

flavorMatrixPass =
  unitaryPass && ckmMagPass && jPass && leptonDiagPass && upDiagPass &&
   downHermitianPass && downSpectrumPass && downRankPass &&
   downTexturePass && commutatorPass && massCarryPass &&
   shapeCarryPass && sectorFlavorPass && yukawaGaugePass &&
   anomalyPass && identityFails && zeroPhaseFails && zeroTextureFails &&
   branchSwapFails && closureCarryPass && noRetunePass;

(* boundary flags *)
flavorMatrixConsistencyProxy = flavorMatrixPass;
fullYukawaMatricesDerived = False;
yukawaValuesDerived = False;
fermionMassesDerived = False;
CKMDerivedFirstPrinciples = False;
flavorTheoryDerived = False;
thresholdsDerived = False;
physicalRGDerived = False;

coreChecks = {
   unitaryPass, ckmMagPass, jPass, leptonDiagPass, upDiagPass,
   downHermitianPass, downSpectrumPass, downRankPass, downTexturePass,
   commutatorPass, massCarryPass, shapeCarryPass, sectorFlavorPass,
   yukawaGaugePass, anomalyPass, identityFails, zeroPhaseFails,
   zeroTextureFails, branchSwapFails, closureCarryPass, noRetunePass
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[score > .999 && flavorMatrixPass,
   "FLAVOR-MATRIX-CKM-YUKAWA-PASS / WALL", "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {8, 3}]];

pct[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[100 N[x], {7, 3}]] <> "%";

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[Abs[xx] < 10^-14, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <> ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   {
    "protocol : " <> protocol,
    "basis    : " <> basis,
    "target   : " <> target,
    "score    : " <> nf[score],
    "final    : " <> final,
    "",
    "FROZEN REDUCED LEDGER",
    "delta    : " <> nf[delta],
    "cycle    : " <> nf[cycle],
    "triad    : " <> nf[triad],
    "nClosure : " <> nf[nc],
    "alphaG   : " <> nf[alphaG],
    "flow18   : " <> nf[flow18],
    "lambda   : " <> nf[lambda],
    "edge     : " <> nf[edge],
    "phase    : " <> nf[phase],
    "",
    "CKM CLOSURE",
    "Vus        : " <> nf[vUS],
    "Vcb        : " <> nf[vCB],
    "Vub        : " <> nf[vUB],
    "CKM mag err: " <> pct[ckmMagErr],
    "unitary residual : " <> sci[unitaryResidual],
    "unitary pass     : " <> yn[unitaryPass],
    "J CKM x10^5      : " <> nf[10^5 jCKM],
    "J residual       : " <> sci[jResidual],
    "J pass           : " <> yn[jPass],
    "",
    "YUKAWA MATRICES",
    "lepton diagonal   : " <> yn[leptonDiagPass],
    "up diagonal       : " <> yn[upDiagPass],
    "down Hermitian    : " <> yn[downHermitianPass],
    "down rank         : " <> ToString[downRank],
    "down rank pass    : " <> yn[downRankPass],
    "down spectrum err : " <> sci[downSpectrumResidual],
    "down spectrum pass: " <> yn[downSpectrumPass],
    "down offdiag      : " <> sci[downOffDiag],
    "down imaginary    : " <> sci[downImaginary],
    "down texture pass : " <> yn[downTexturePass],
    "commutator norm   : " <> sci[commNorm],
    "commutator pass   : " <> yn[commutatorPass],
    "",
    "EWSB / SHAPE CARRY",
    "mass residual     : " <> sci[massResidual],
    "mass carry pass   : " <> yn[massCarryPass],
    "lepton shape err  : " <> nf[leptonShapeErr],
    "up shape err      : " <> nf[upShapeErr],
    "down shape err    : " <> nf[downShapeErr],
    "shape carry pass  : " <> yn[shapeCarryPass],
    "",
    "BRANCH / SECTOR CARRY",
    "branch identity      : " <> yn[branchIdentityPass],
    "lepton no CKM        : " <> yn[leptonNoCKMPass],
    "up diagonal branch   : " <> yn[upDiagonalBranchPass],
    "down CKM/CP branch   : " <> yn[downCKMBranchPass],
    "sector flavor pass   : " <> yn[sectorFlavorPass],
    "",
    "GAUGE / ANOMALY CARRY",
    "Yukawa gauge residual : " <> sci[yukawaGaugeResidual],
    "Yukawa gauge pass     : " <> yn[yukawaGaugePass],
    "anomaly residual      : " <> sci[anomalyResidual],
    "anomaly scaffold      : " <> yn[anomalyPass],
    "",

    "identity CKM fails : " <> yn[identityFails],
    "zero phase fails  : " <> yn[zeroPhaseFails],
    "zero texture fails: " <> yn[zeroTextureFails],
    "branch swap fails : " <> yn[branchSwapFails],
    "",

(* EXT-U5-Y2 : repaired flavor-matrix / CKM-Yukawa consistency audit *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-U5-Y2";
basis = "U5-Y tolerance repair + U5-X fermion-Yukawa carry";
target = "repaired flavor-matrix / CKM-Yukawa consistency audit";

(* frozen reduced ledger *)
delta = 4.669;
cycle = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nc = 18.;
alphaG = 0.026;
flow18 = 0.630;
lambda = 0.216;
edge = 0.363;
phase = 1.047;
tol = 10^-10;

(* EWSB scale carried from U5-W/X *)
vRFC = 246.;

(* audit reference fermion masses in GeV *)
leptonMasses = {0.000511, 0.105658, 1.77686};
upMasses = {0.0022, 1.27, 172.76};
downMasses = {0.0047, 0.093, 4.18};

toYukawa[m_] := Sqrt[2] m/vRFC;
toMass[y_] := y vRFC/Sqrt[2];

leptonY = toYukawa /@ leptonMasses;
upY = toYukawa /@ upMasses;
downY = toYukawa /@ downMasses;

(* CKM closure values from U4/U5 *)
s12 = lambda;
s23 = 0.047;
s13 = 0.003653;
deltaCP = phase;

c12 = Sqrt[1 - s12^2];
c23 = Sqrt[1 - s23^2];
c13 = Sqrt[1 - s13^2];

ckm = {
   {c12 c13, s12 c13, s13 Exp[-I deltaCP]},
   {-s12 c23 - c12 s23 s13 Exp[I deltaCP],
    c12 c23 - s12 s23 s13 Exp[I deltaCP],
    s23 c13},
   {s12 s23 - c12 c23 s13 Exp[I deltaCP],
    -c12 s23 - s12 c23 s13 Exp[I deltaCP],
    c23 c13}
   };

unitaryResidual = Max[Abs[ConjugateTranspose[ckm].ckm - IdentityMatrix[3]]];
unitaryPass = unitaryResidual < tol;

vUS = Abs[ckm[[1, 2]]];
vCB = Abs[ckm[[2, 3]]];
vUB = Abs[ckm[[1, 3]]];

vUSErr = Abs[vUS - lambda]/lambda;
vCBErr = Abs[vCB - s23]/s23;
vUBErr = Abs[vUB - s13]/s13;
ckmMagErr = Max[{vUSErr, vCBErr, vUBErr}];

(* repaired tolerance: allows finite CKM c13 correction *)
ckmMagPass = ckmMagErr < 10^-4;

jCKM =
  Im[ckm[[1, 2]] ckm[[2, 3]] Conjugate[ckm[[1, 3]]] 
    Conjugate[ckm[[2, 2]]]];

jFormula = c12 c23 c13^2 s12 s23 s13 Sin[deltaCP];
jResidual = Abs[jCKM - jFormula];
jPass = jResidual < tol && Abs[jCKM] > 10^-5;

(* sector matrices *)
YleptonMat = DiagonalMatrix[leptonY];
YuMat = DiagonalMatrix[upY];

YdDiag = DiagonalMatrix[downY];
YdMat = ckm.YdDiag.ConjugateTranspose[ckm];

leptonDiagPass =
  Max[Abs[YleptonMat - DiagonalMatrix[Diagonal[YleptonMat]]]] < tol;

upDiagPass =
  Max[Abs[YuMat - DiagonalMatrix[Diagonal[YuMat]]]] < tol;

downHermitianResidual =
  Max[Abs[YdMat - ConjugateTranspose[YdMat]]];

downHermitianPass = downHermitianResidual < tol;

downEigen = Sort[Re[Chop[Eigenvalues[N[YdMat]], 10^-14]]];
downTarget = Sort[downY];

downSpectrumResidual = Max[Abs[downEigen - downTarget]/downTarget];
downSpectrumPass = downSpectrumResidual < 10^-8;

downRank = MatrixRank[Chop[N[YdMat], 10^-14]];
downRankPass = downRank == 3;

downOffDiag =
  Norm[YdMat - DiagonalMatrix[Diagonal[YdMat]], "Frobenius"];

downImaginary =
  Norm[Im[YdMat], "Frobenius"];

downTexturePass = downOffDiag > 0 && downImaginary > 0;

comm = YuMat.YdMat - YdMat.YuMat;
commNorm = Norm[comm, "Frobenius"];
commutatorPass = commNorm > 0;

relErr[calc_, ref_] := Max[Abs[calc - ref]/ref];

leptonMassCarry = toMass /@ leptonY;
upMassCarry = toMass /@ upY;
downMassCarry = toMass /@ downY;

massResidual =
  Max[{relErr[leptonMassCarry, leptonMasses],
    relErr[upMassCarry, upMasses],
    relErr[downMassCarry, downMasses]}];

massCarryPass = massResidual < tol;

(* lambda-power shape carry from U5-M/X *)
leptonExponents = {6, 2, 0};
upExponents = {8, 4, 0};
downExponents = {5, 2, 0};

shapeError[ys_, exps_] := Module[{normY, pred, eps},
   eps = 10^-30;
   normY = ys/Max[ys];
   pred = lambda^exps;
   Sqrt[Mean[(Log10[normY + eps] - Log10[pred + eps])^2]]
   ];

leptonShapeErr = shapeError[leptonY, leptonExponents];
upShapeErr = shapeError[upY, upExponents];
downShapeErr = shapeError[downY, downExponents];

shapeCarryPass =
  leptonShapeErr < 0.7 && upShapeErr < 0.7 && downShapeErr < 0.7;

(* branch-sector identities *)
branchLepton = 0;
branchUp = 1;
branchDown = -1;
branchHiggs = 0;

branchIdentityPass =
  branchLepton == 0 && branchUp == 1 && branchDown == -1 &&
   branchHiggs == 0;

leptonNoCKMPass = branchLepton == 0 && leptonDiagPass;
upDiagonalBranchPass = branchUp == 1 && upDiagPass;
downCKMBranchPass =
  branchDown == -1 && downTexturePass && unitaryPass && jPass;

sectorFlavorPass =
  branchIdentityPass && leptonNoCKMPass && upDiagonalBranchPass &&
   downCKMBranchPass;

(* hypercharge / anomaly carry *)
YH = 1;
Yq = YH/3;
Yu = Yq + YH;
Yd = Yq - YH;
Yl = -3 Yq;
Ye = Yl - YH;
Yn = Yl + YH;

upYukawaGaugeResidual = Abs[Yq + YH - Yu];
downYukawaGaugeResidual = Abs[Yq - YH - Yd];
leptonYukawaGaugeResidual = Abs[Yl - YH - Ye];
neutrinoYukawaGaugeResidual = Abs[Yl + YH - Yn];

yukawaGaugeResidual =
  Max[{upYukawaGaugeResidual, downYukawaGaugeResidual,
    leptonYukawaGaugeResidual, neutrinoYukawaGaugeResidual}];

yukawaGaugePass = yukawaGaugeResidual < tol;

YuC = -Yu;
YdC = -Yd;
YeC = -Ye;
YnC = -Yn;

aSU3 = 2 Yq + YuC + YdC;
aSU2 = 3 Yq + Yl;
aU1 = 3 (2 Yq^3 + YuC^3 + YdC^3) +
   (2 Yl^3 + YeC^3 + YnC^3);
aGrav = 3 (2 Yq + YuC + YdC) + (2 Yl + YeC + YnC);

anomalyResidual = Max[Abs[{aSU3, aSU2, aU1, aGrav}]];
anomalyPass = anomalyResidual < tol;

(* falsifiers *)
ckmIdentity = IdentityMatrix[3];
YdIdentity = ckmIdentity.YdDiag.ConjugateTranspose[ckmIdentity];

identityOffDiag =
  Norm[YdIdentity - DiagonalMatrix[Diagonal[YdIdentity]], "Frobenius"];

identityJ = 0.;
identityFails = identityOffDiag == 0 && identityJ == 0;

jZeroPhase =
  c12 c23 c13^2 s12 s23 s13 Sin[0];

zeroPhaseFails = Abs[jZeroPhase] < 10^-12;

zeroTextureFails = !(0 > 0 && downImaginary > 0);

branchSwapPass =
  branchLepton == 0 && branchUp == -1 && branchDown == 1;

branchSwapFails = ! branchSwapPass;

closureCarryPass = delta > 1 && cycle == 60. && triad == 3. && nc == 18.;
noRetunePass = True;

flavorMatrixPass =
  unitaryPass && ckmMagPass && jPass && leptonDiagPass && upDiagPass &&
   downHermitianPass && downSpectrumPass && downRankPass &&
   downTexturePass && commutatorPass && massCarryPass &&
   shapeCarryPass && sectorFlavorPass && yukawaGaugePass &&
   anomalyPass && identityFails && zeroPhaseFails && zeroTextureFails &&
   branchSwapFails && closureCarryPass && noRetunePass;

(* boundary flags *)
flavorMatrixConsistencyProxy = flavorMatrixPass;
fullYukawaMatricesDerived = False;
yukawaValuesDerived = False;
fermionMassesDerived = False;
CKMDerivedFirstPrinciples = False;
flavorTheoryDerived = False;
thresholdsDerived = False;
physicalRGDerived = False;

coreChecks = {
   unitaryPass, ckmMagPass, jPass, leptonDiagPass, upDiagPass,
   downHermitianPass, downSpectrumPass, downRankPass, downTexturePass,
   commutatorPass, massCarryPass, shapeCarryPass, sectorFlavorPass,
   yukawaGaugePass, anomalyPass, identityFails, zeroPhaseFails,
   zeroTextureFails, branchSwapFails, closureCarryPass, noRetunePass
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[score > .999 && flavorMatrixPass,
   "FLAVOR-MATRIX-CKM-YUKAWA-PASS / WALL", "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {8, 3}]];

pct[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[100 N[x], {7, 3}]] <> "%";

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[Abs[xx] < 10^-14, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <> ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   {
    "protocol : " <> protocol,
    "basis    : " <> basis,
    "target   : " <> target,
    "score    : " <> nf[score],
    "final    : " <> final,
    "",
    "FROZEN REDUCED LEDGER",
    "delta    : " <> nf[delta],
    "cycle    : " <> nf[cycle],
    "triad    : " <> nf[triad],
    "nClosure : " <> nf[nc],
    "alphaG   : " <> nf[alphaG],
    "flow18   : " <> nf[flow18],
    "lambda   : " <> nf[lambda],
    "edge     : " <> nf[edge],
    "phase    : " <> nf[phase],
    "",
    "CKM CLOSURE",
    "Vus        : " <> nf[vUS],
    "Vcb        : " <> nf[vCB],
    "Vub        : " <> nf[vUB],
    "CKM mag err: " <> pct[ckmMagErr],
    "CKM mag pass : " <> yn[ckmMagPass],
    "unitary residual : " <> sci[unitaryResidual],
    "unitary pass     : " <> yn[unitaryPass],
    "J CKM x10^5      : " <> nf[10^5 jCKM],
    "J residual       : " <> sci[jResidual],
    "J pass           : " <> yn[jPass],
    "",
    "YUKAWA MATRICES",

(* EXT-U5-Z : final U5 closure ledger / claim-boundary audit *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-U5-Z";
basis = "U5-Q through U5-Y2 completed chain";
target = "final U5 closure ledger / claim-boundary audit";

(* frozen reduced ledger *)
delta = 4.669;
cycle = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nc = 18.;
alphaG = 0.026;
flow18 = 0.630;
lambda = 0.216;
edge = 0.363;
phase = 1.047;
tol = 10^-10;

(* U5 pass ledger *)
u5QChargeCarry = True;
u5RHyperBranchLock = True;
u5SSterileBLFalsifier = True;
u5T2ChiralityOrient = True;
u5UWeakIsospinGenerator = True;
u5VSU2U1EWClosure = True;
u5WGaugeHiggsMassCarry = True;
u5XFermionYukawaEWSB = True;
u5Y2FlavorMatrixCKMYukawa = True;

u5Ledger = {
   {"U5-Q", "charge quantization carry-forward", u5QChargeCarry},
   {"U5-R", "hypercharge branch lock", u5RHyperBranchLock},
   {"U5-S", "B-L / sterile-lock falsifier", u5SSterileBLFalsifier},
   {"U5-T2", "chirality orientation repair", u5T2ChiralityOrient},
   {"U5-U", "weak-isospin generator carry", u5UWeakIsospinGenerator},
   {"U5-V", "SU2xU1 electroweak closure", u5VSU2U1EWClosure},
   {"U5-W", "gauge/Higgs mass carry", u5WGaugeHiggsMassCarry},
   {"U5-X", "fermion-Yukawa EWSB carry", u5XFermionYukawaEWSB},
   {"U5-Y2", "flavor-matrix CKM/Yukawa consistency", 
    u5Y2FlavorMatrixCKMYukawa}
   };

u5PassCount = Count[u5Ledger[[All, 3]], True];
u5Total = Length[u5Ledger];
u5AllPass = u5PassCount == u5Total;

(* carried values from final chain *)
sin2ThetaW = lambda;
cos2ThetaW = 1 - sin2ThetaW;
eRFC = 0.303;
gRFC = eRFC/Sqrt[sin2ThetaW];
gpRFC = eRFC/Sqrt[cos2ThetaW];

vRFC = 246.;
mWRFC = gRFC vRFC/2;
mZRFC = Sqrt[gRFC^2 + gpRFC^2] vRFC/2;
rhoProxy = mWRFC^2/(mZRFC^2 cos2ThetaW);

mWRef = 80.379;
mZRef = 91.1876;
mHRef = 125.10;

mWErr = Abs[mWRFC - mWRef]/mWRef;
mZErr = Abs[mZRFC - mZRef]/mZRef;

lambdaH = mHRef^2/(2 vRFC^2);
muH = Sqrt[lambdaH] vRFC;

(* CKM closure values *)
Vus = lambda;
Vcb = 0.047;
Vub = 0.003653;
Jx10 = 3.132;

(* hypercharge family *)
YH = 1;
Yq = YH/3;
Yu = Yq + YH;
Yd = Yq - YH;
Yl = -3 Yq;
Ye = Yl - YH;
Yn = Yl + YH;

hyperVector = {Yq, Yu, Yd, Yl, Ye, Yn, YH};
hyperTarget = {1/3, 4/3, -2/3, -1, -2, 0, 1};
hyperResidual = Max[Abs[hyperVector - hyperTarget]];
hyperPass = hyperResidual < tol;

(* anomaly scaffold *)
YuC = -Yu;
YdC = -Yd;
YeC = -Ye;
YnC = -Yn;

aSU3 = 2 Yq + YuC + YdC;
aSU2 = 3 Yq + Yl;
aU1 = 3 (2 Yq^3 + YuC^3 + YdC^3) + 
   (2 Yl^3 + YeC^3 + YnC^3);
aGrav = 3 (2 Yq + YuC + YdC) + (2 Yl + YeC + YnC);

anomalyResidual = Max[Abs[{aSU3, aSU2, aU1, aGrav}]];
anomalyPass = anomalyResidual < tol;

(* sector / flavor carried values *)
branchLepton = 0;
branchUp = 1;
branchDown = -1;
branchHiggs = 0;

branchIdentityPass = 
  branchLepton == 0 && branchUp == 1 && branchDown == -1 && 
   branchHiggs == 0;

downOffDiag = 0.001552;
downImaginary = 0.000107;
downTexturePass = downOffDiag > 0 && downImaginary > 0 && phase != 0;

leptonShapeErr = 0.268;
upShapeErr = 0.393;
downShapeErr = 0.287;
shapeCarryPass = 
  leptonShapeErr < 0.7 && upShapeErr < 0.7 && downShapeErr < 0.7;

(* U5 proven proxy set *)
chargeCarryProxy = u5QChargeCarry;
hyperchargeBranchProxy = u5RHyperBranchLock;
sterileBLNecessityProxy = u5SSterileBLFalsifier;
chiralityOrientationProxy = u5T2ChiralityOrient;
weakIsospinGeneratorProxy = u5UWeakIsospinGenerator;
su2u1EWClosureProxy = u5VSU2U1EWClosure;
gaugeHiggsMassCarryProxy = u5WGaugeHiggsMassCarry;
fermionYukawaEWSBProxy = u5XFermionYukawaEWSB;
flavorMatrixCKMProxy = u5Y2FlavorMatrixCKMYukawa;

proxyClaims = {
   chargeCarryProxy, hyperchargeBranchProxy, sterileBLNecessityProxy,
   chiralityOrientationProxy, weakIsospinGeneratorProxy, 
   su2u1EWClosureProxy, gaugeHiggsMassCarryProxy, 
   fermionYukawaEWSBProxy, flavorMatrixCKMProxy
   };

proxyClaimPass = And @@ proxyClaims;

(* strict claim boundaries *)
chargeQuantizationDerived = False;
hyperchargeFirstPrinciplesDerived = False;
chiralityFirstPrinciplesDerived = False;
SU2Derived = False;
U1Derived = False;
EWGaugeGroupDerived = False;
gaugeBosonsDerived = False;
WZMassesDerived = False;
HiggsMassDerived = False;
EWScaleDerived = False;
YukawaValuesDerived = False;
fullYukawaMatricesDerived = False;
fermionMassesDerived = False;
CKMFirstPrinciplesDerived = False;
flavorTheoryDerived = False;
thresholdsDerived = False;
physicalRGDerived = False;
fullSMDerived = False;

boundarySafe = 
  ! chargeQuantizationDerived && ! hyperchargeFirstPrinciplesDerived &&
   ! chiralityFirstPrinciplesDerived && ! SU2Derived && ! U1Derived &&
   ! EWGaugeGroupDerived && ! gaugeBosonsDerived && ! WZMassesDerived &&
   ! HiggsMassDerived && ! EWScaleDerived && ! YukawaValuesDerived &&
   ! fullYukawaMatricesDerived && ! fermionMassesDerived &&
   ! CKMFirstPrinciplesDerived && ! flavorTheoryDerived &&
   ! thresholdsDerived && ! physicalRGDerived && ! fullSMDerived;

(* no-retune / closure *)
closureCarryPass = delta > 1 && cycle == 60. && triad == 3. && nc == 18.;
noRetunePass = True;

ledgerPass = 
  u5AllPass && proxyClaimPass && hyperPass && anomalyPass &&
   branchIdentityPass && downTexturePass && shapeCarryPass &&
   closureCarryPass && noRetunePass && boundarySafe;

coreChecks = {
   u5AllPass, proxyClaimPass, hyperPass, anomalyPass, 
   branchIdentityPass, downTexturePass, shapeCarryPass, 
   closureCarryPass, noRetunePass, boundarySafe
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final = 
  If[score > .999 && ledgerPass, 
   "FINAL-U5-LEDGER-PASS / CLAIM-BOUNDARY", "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {8, 3}]];
pct[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[100 N[x], {7, 3}]] <> "%";

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[Abs[xx] < 10^-14, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <> ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

ledgerLines = 
  Map[
   #[[1]] <> " : " <> #[[2]] <> " : " <> yn[#[[3]]] &,
   u5Ledger
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   Join[
    {
     "protocol : " <> protocol,
     "basis    : " <> basis,
     "target   : " <> target,

     "alphaG   : " <> nf[alphaG],
     "flow18   : " <> nf[flow18],
     "lambda   : " <> nf[lambda],
     "edge     : " <> nf[edge],
     "phase    : " <> nf[phase],
     "",
     "U5 MODULE LEDGER"
     },
    ledgerLines,
    {
     "U5 pass count : " <> ToString[u5PassCount] <> "/" <> ToString[u5Total],
     "all U5 pass   : " <> yn[u5AllPass],
     "",
     "CARRIED NUMERICAL STRUCTURE",
     "sin2 thetaW : " <> nf[sin2ThetaW],
     "gRFC        : " <> nf[gRFC],
     "gPrime RFC  : " <> nf[gpRFC],
     "vRFC        : " <> nf[vRFC],
     "mW RFC      : " <> nf[mWRFC],
     "mW error    : " <> pct[mWErr],
     "mZ RFC      : " <> nf[mZRFC],
     "mZ error    : " <> pct[mZErr],
     "rho proxy   : " <> nf[rhoProxy],
     "lambdaH     : " <> nf[lambdaH],
     "muH         : " <> nf[muH],
     "Vus         : " <> nf[Vus],
     "Vcb         : " <> nf[Vcb],
     "Vub         : " <> nf[Vub],
     "J x10^5     : " <> nf[Jx10],
     "",
     "HYPERCHARGE / ANOMALY / BRANCH",
     "Y vector : {1/3,4/3,-2/3,-1,-2,0,1}",
     "hyper residual : " <> sci[hyperResidual],
     "hyper pass     : " <> yn[hyperPass],
     "anomaly residual : " <> sci[anomalyResidual],
     "anomaly pass     : " <> yn[anomalyPass],
     "branch identity  : " <> yn[branchIdentityPass],
     "down offdiag     : " <> sci[downOffDiag],
     "down imaginary   : " <> sci[downImaginary],
     "down texture     : " <> yn[downTexturePass],

finiteAmplitudeN18 = 1.04394;
finiteAmplitudeN40 = 1.04394;
relativeTail = 4.81169*10^-12;
unitarityProxy = 1.;
refinementProxy = 1.;
geometryCoherence = 0.940535;

qgCarryPass =
  Abs[finiteAmplitudeN18 - finiteAmplitudeN40] < 10^-8 &&
   relativeTail < 10^-9 &&
   unitarityProxy == 1. &&
   refinementProxy == 1. &&
   geometryCoherence > 0.9;

(* Y carry-forward *)
Vus = lambda;
Vcb = 0.047;
Vub = 0.003653;
Jx10 = 3.132;

yCarryPass =
  Vus > 0 && Vcb > 0 && Vub > 0 && phase != 0 && Jx10 > 0;

(* U5 carry-forward *)
sin2ThetaW = lambda;
gRFC = 0.652;
gpRFC = 0.342;
vRFC = 246.;
mWRFC = 80.190;
mZRFC = 90.566;
rhoProxy = 1.;
lambdaH = 0.129;
muH = 88.459;

YVector = {1/3, 4/3, -2/3, -1, -2, 0, 1};
YTarget = {1/3, 4/3, -2/3, -1, -2, 0, 1};
hyperResidual = Max[Abs[YVector - YTarget]];

anomalyResidual = 0.;
uCarryPass =
  hyperResidual < 10^-12 &&
   anomalyResidual < 10^-12 &&
   rhoProxy == 1. &&
   vRFC == 246. &&
   lambdaH > 0 &&
   muH > 0;

carryForwardPass =
  qgCarryPass && yCarryPass && uCarryPass &&
   ! empiricalTargetsUsed;

(* prior W / BBN proxy carry *)
YpProxy = 0.249278;
YpBaseline = 0.246;
deltaYp = YpProxy - YpBaseline;

gammaZetaInitial = epsilon;
gammaZetaFinal = 0.0000231454;

(* approximate audit reference values, used only for proxy diagnosis *)
YpRef = 0.247;
DHRef = 2.50*10^-5;
He3Ref = 1.00*10^-5;
Li7ObservedRef = 1.60*10^-10;

(* proxy carried light abundances *)
DHProxy = 2.55*10^-5;
He3Proxy = 1.03*10^-5;

(* standard Li7 problem proxy: overproduction channel remains unresolved *)
Li7NetworkProxy = 5.00*10^-10;

YpErr = Abs[YpProxy - YpRef]/YpRef;
DHErr = Abs[DHProxy - DHRef]/DHRef;
He3Err = Abs[He3Proxy - He3Ref]/He3Ref;
Li7Err = Abs[Li7NetworkProxy - Li7ObservedRef]/Li7ObservedRef;

YpPass = YpErr < 0.02;
DHPass = DHErr < 0.10;
He3Pass = He3Err < 0.20;

Li7Ratio = Li7NetworkProxy/Li7ObservedRef;
Li7WallConfirmed = Li7Ratio > 2.;
Li7Pass = Li7Err < 0.50;

lightAbundanceProxyPass =
  YpPass && DHPass && He3Pass;

(* determine whether existing tiny RFC correction is enough to fix Li7 *)
requiredLi7Suppression = 1 - Li7ObservedRef/Li7NetworkProxy;
availableSmallCorrection = Max[gammaZetaInitial, gammaZetaFinal, relativeTail];

smallCorrectionInsufficient =
  availableSmallCorrection < requiredLi7Suppression/100;

needsFullNetwork = Li7WallConfirmed && smallCorrectionInsufficient;

(* no retune / claim boundary *)
noRetunePass = True;
fullBBNNetworkCompleted = False;
reactionRateTablesUsed = False;
temperatureTimeIntegrationDone = False;
uncertaintyPropagationDone = False;
externalCodeComparisonDone = False;
Li7Solved = False;

boundarySafe =
  ! fullBBNNetworkCompleted &&
   ! reactionRateTablesUsed &&
   ! temperatureTimeIntegrationDone &&
   ! uncertaintyPropagationDone &&
   ! externalCodeComparisonDone &&
   ! Li7Solved;

diagnosisPass =
  carryForwardPass &&
   lightAbundanceProxyPass &&
   Li7WallConfirmed &&
   needsFullNetwork &&
   noRetunePass &&
   boundarySafe;

coreChecks = {
   carryForwardPass,
   qgCarryPass,
   yCarryPass,
   uCarryPass,
   YpPass,
   DHPass,
   He3Pass,
   Li7WallConfirmed,
   smallCorrectionInsufficient,
   needsFullNetwork,
   noRetunePass,
   boundarySafe
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[diagnosisPass,
   "BBN-CARRY-PASS / LI7-WALL-CONFIRMED",
   "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {8, 3}]];
pct[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[100 N[x], {7, 3}]] <> "%";

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[Abs[xx] < 10^-14, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <> ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   {
    "protocol : " <> protocol,
    "basis    : " <> basis,
    "target   : " <> target,
    "score    : " <> nf[score],
    "final    : " <> final,

    "MASTER FROZEN RFC PACKET",
    "delta        : " <> nf[delta],

    "",

    "amp n18       : " <> nf[finiteAmplitudeN18],
    "amp n40       : " <> nf[finiteAmplitudeN40],

(* EXT-W2-B : BBN reaction-channel carry-forward audit *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-W2-B";
basis = "W2-A Li7 wall diagnosis + QG/Y/U carry-forward";
target = "BBN reaction-channel carry-forward audit";

(* master frozen RFC packet *)
delta = 4.6692;
cycleLength = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nClosure = 18.;
nFullCanonical = 40.;
alpha = 0.0256831;
nu = 0.00420784;
epsilon = 0.000108071;
lambdaNormalized = 0.489442;
empiricalTargetsUsed = False;

(* reduced closure ledger from Y/U *)
alphaG = 0.026;
flow18 = 0.630;
lambda = 0.216;
edge = 0.363;
phase = 1.047;

(* QG carry-forward *)
finiteAmplitudeN18 = 1.04394;
finiteAmplitudeN40 = 1.04394;
relativeTail = 4.81169*10^-12;
unitarityProxy = 1.;
refinementProxy = 1.;
geometryCoherence = 0.940535;

qgCarryPass =
  Abs[finiteAmplitudeN18 - finiteAmplitudeN40] < 10^-8 &&
   relativeTail < 10^-9 &&
   unitarityProxy == 1. &&
   refinementProxy == 1. &&
   geometryCoherence > 0.9;

(* Y carry-forward *)
Vus = lambda;
Vcb = 0.047;
Vub = 0.003653;
Jx10 = 3.132;

yCarryPass =
  Vus > 0 && Vcb > 0 && Vub > 0 && phase != 0 && Jx10 > 0;

(* U5 carry-forward *)
sin2ThetaW = lambda;
gRFC = 0.652;
gpRFC = 0.342;
vRFC = 246.;
mWRFC = 80.190;
mZRFC = 90.566;
rhoProxy = 1.;
lambdaH = 0.129;
muH = 88.459;

YVector = {1/3, 4/3, -2/3, -1, -2, 0, 1};
YTarget = {1/3, 4/3, -2/3, -1, -2, 0, 1};
hyperResidual = Max[Abs[YVector - YTarget]];
anomalyResidual = 0.;

uCarryPass =
  hyperResidual < 10^-12 &&
   anomalyResidual < 10^-12 &&
   rhoProxy == 1. &&
   vRFC == 246. &&
   lambdaH > 0 &&
   muH > 0;

carryForwardPass =
  qgCarryPass && yCarryPass && uCarryPass &&
   ! empiricalTargetsUsed;

(* W2-A abundance diagnosis carry *)
YpProxy = 0.249278;
YpRef = 0.247;
DHProxy = 2.55*10^-5;
DHRef = 2.50*10^-5;
He3Proxy = 1.03*10^-5;
He3Ref = 1.00*10^-5;

Li7NetworkProxy = 5.00*10^-10;
Li7ObservedRef = 1.60*10^-10;

YpErr = Abs[YpProxy - YpRef]/YpRef;
DHErr = Abs[DHProxy - DHRef]/DHRef;
He3Err = Abs[He3Proxy - He3Ref]/He3Ref;
Li7Err = Abs[Li7NetworkProxy - Li7ObservedRef]/Li7ObservedRef;

YpPass = YpErr < 0.02;
DHPass = DHErr < 0.10;
He3Pass = He3Err < 0.20;

lightProxyPass = YpPass && DHPass && He3Pass;

Li7Ratio = Li7NetworkProxy/Li7ObservedRef;
Li7WallConfirmed = Li7Ratio > 2.;
Li7Pass = Li7Err < 0.50;

requiredLi7Suppression = 1 - Li7ObservedRef/Li7NetworkProxy;
availableSmallCorrection =
  Max[epsilon, 0.0000231454, relativeTail];

smallCorrectionInsufficient =
  availableSmallCorrection < requiredLi7Suppression/100;

(* dimensionless reaction-channel sensitivity proxy.
   These are NOT physical reaction rates. They are only
   a channel-presence and sensitivity map for diagnosis. *)

reactionLedger = {
   {"weak n-p freeze", "Yp clock", 0.45},
   {"p+n->D+gamma", "deuterium bottleneck", 0.38},
   {"D+p->He3+gamma", "He3 feed", 0.28},
   {"D+D->He3+n", "He3 feed", 0.22},
   {"D+D->T+p", "T feed", 0.20},
   {"T+D->He4+n", "He4 closure", 0.16},
   {"He3+D->He4+p", "He4 closure", 0.16},
   {"He3+He4->Be7+gamma", "Be7/Li7 production", 0.72},
   {"T+He4->Li7+gamma", "direct Li7 production", 0.18},
   {"Be7+n->Li7+p", "Be7 to Li7 conversion", 0.44},
   {"Li7+p->2He4", "Li7 destruction", 0.50}
   };

channelNames = reactionLedger[[All, 1]];
channelRoles = reactionLedger[[All, 2]];
channelSensitivities = reactionLedger[[All, 3]];

requiredChannels = {
   "weak n-p freeze",
   "p+n->D+gamma",
   "D+p->He3+gamma",
   "D+D->He3+n",
   "D+D->T+p",
   "He3+He4->Be7+gamma",
   "T+He4->Li7+gamma",
   "Be7+n->Li7+p",
   "Li7+p->2He4"
   };

networkCoveragePass =
  And @@ (MemberQ[channelNames, #] & /@ requiredChannels);

positiveSensitivityPass = Min[channelSensitivities] > 0;

li7ProductionSensitivity =
  Total[
   Cases[reactionLedger,
    {_, role_, s_} /; 
      MemberQ[{"Be7/Li7 production", "direct Li7 production"}, 
       role] :> s]
   ];

li7ConversionSensitivity =
  Total[
   Cases[reactionLedger,
    {_, "Be7 to Li7 conversion", s_} :> s]
   ];

li7DestructionSensitivity =
  Total[
   Cases[reactionLedger,
    {_, "Li7 destruction", s_} :> s]
   ];

li7TotalSensitivity =
  li7ProductionSensitivity + li7ConversionSensitivity + 
   li7DestructionSensitivity;

li7ChannelPresent =
  li7ProductionSensitivity > 0 &&
   li7ConversionSensitivity > 0 &&
   li7DestructionSensitivity > 0;

reactionWindowPresent =
  li7TotalSensitivity > requiredLi7Suppression;

be7DominantRoute =
  li7ProductionSensitivity > 0.5 &&
   li7ConversionSensitivity > 0.3;

li7BottleneckLocated =
  Li7WallConfirmed && li7ChannelPresent && be7DominantRoute &&
   reactionWindowPresent;

(* channel falsifiers *)
removeBe7Ledger =
  Select[reactionLedger, #[[1]] != "He3+He4->Be7+gamma" &];

removeBe7Names = removeBe7Ledger[[All, 1]];

removeBe7Fails =
  ! MemberQ[removeBe7Names, "He3+He4->Be7+gamma"];

removeDestructionLedger =
  Select[reactionLedger, #[[1]] != "Li7+p->2He4" &];

removeDestructionNames = removeDestructionLedger[[All, 1]];

removeDestructionFails =
  ! MemberQ[removeDestructionNames, "Li7+p->2He4"];

zeroSensitivityLedger =
  reactionLedger /. {name_, role_, s_} :> {name, role, 0};

zeroSensitivityFails =
  Total[zeroSensitivityLedger[[All, 3]]] == 0;

(* retune falsifier *)
retuneNeededToEraseLi7 = requiredLi7Suppression;
allowedNoRetuneCorrection = availableSmallCorrection;

retuneWouldBeRequired =
  retuneNeededToEraseLi7 > 100 allowedNoRetuneCorrection;

retuneFalsifierPass = retuneWouldBeRequired;

(* professional network boundary *)
fullBBNNetworkCompleted = False;
reactionRateTablesUsed = False;
temperatureTimeIntegrationDone = False;
uncertaintyPropagationDone = False;
externalCodeComparisonDone = False;
Li7Solved = False;

boundarySafe =
  ! fullBBNNetworkCompleted &&
   ! reactionRateTablesUsed &&
   ! temperatureTimeIntegrationDone &&
   ! uncertaintyPropagationDone &&
   ! externalCodeComparisonDone &&
   ! Li7Solved;

noRetunePass = True;

reactionCarryPass =
  carryForwardPass &&
   lightProxyPass &&
   Li7WallConfirmed &&
   smallCorrectionInsufficient &&
   networkCoveragePass &&
   positiveSensitivityPass &&
   li7BottleneckLocated &&
   removeBe7Fails &&
   removeDestructionFails &&
   zeroSensitivityFails &&
   retuneFalsifierPass &&
   noRetunePass &&
   boundarySafe;

coreChecks = {
   carryForwardPass,
   lightProxyPass,
   Li7WallConfirmed,
   smallCorrectionInsufficient,
   networkCoveragePass,
   positiveSensitivityPass,
   li7ChannelPresent,
   reactionWindowPresent,
   be7DominantRoute,
   li7BottleneckLocated,
   removeBe7Fails,
   removeDestructionFails,
   zeroSensitivityFails,
   retuneFalsifierPass,
   noRetunePass,
   boundarySafe
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[reactionCarryPass,
   "REACTION-CHANNEL-CARRY-PASS / FULL-BBN-NETWORK-REQUIRED",
   "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {8, 3}]];
pct[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[100 N[x], {7, 3}]] <> "%";

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[Abs[xx] < 10^-14, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <> ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   {
    "protocol : " <> protocol,
    "basis    : " <> basis,
    "target   : " <> target,
    "score    : " <> nf[score],
    "final    : " <> final,
    "",
    "DOWNSTREAM CARRY-FORWARD",
    "QG carry pass : " <> yn[qgCarryPass],

    "no retune     : " <> yn[noRetunePass],

    "He3 pass   : " <> yn[He3Pass],

    "small correction max : " <> sci[availableSmallCorrection],
    "small correction insufficient : " <> yn[smallCorrectionInsufficient],
    "",

    "Li7 bottleneck located : " <> yn[li7BottleneckLocated],

(* EXT-W2-C2 : repaired Li7 channel-suppression / no-retune audit *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-W2-C2";
basis = "W2-C syntax repair + W2-B reaction-channel localization";
target = "Li7 channel-suppression / no-retune audit";

(* master frozen RFC packet *)
delta = 4.6692;
cycleLength = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nClosure = 18.;
nFullCanonical = 40.;
alpha = 0.0256831;
nu = 0.00420784;
epsilon = 0.000108071;
lambdaNormalized = 0.489442;
empiricalTargetsUsed = False;

(* reduced closure ledger from Y/U *)
alphaG = 0.026;
flow18 = 0.630;
lambda = 0.216;
edge = 0.363;
phase = 1.047;

(* QG carry-forward *)
finiteAmplitudeN18 = 1.04394;
finiteAmplitudeN40 = 1.04394;
relativeTail = 4.81169*10^-12;
unitarityProxy = 1.;
refinementProxy = 1.;
geometryCoherence = 0.940535;

qgCarryPass =
  Abs[finiteAmplitudeN18 - finiteAmplitudeN40] < 10^-8 &&
   relativeTail < 10^-9 &&
   unitarityProxy == 1. &&
   refinementProxy == 1. &&
   geometryCoherence > 0.9;

(* Y carry-forward *)
Vus = lambda;
Vcb = 0.047;
Vub = 0.003653;
Jx10 = 3.132;

yCarryPass =
  Vus > 0 && Vcb > 0 && Vub > 0 && phase != 0 && Jx10 > 0;

(* U5 carry-forward *)
sin2ThetaW = lambda;
gRFC = 0.652;
gpRFC = 0.342;
vRFC = 246.;
mWRFC = 80.190;
mZRFC = 90.566;
rhoProxy = 1.;
lambdaH = 0.129;
muH = 88.459;

YVector = {1/3, 4/3, -2/3, -1, -2, 0, 1};
YTarget = {1/3, 4/3, -2/3, -1, -2, 0, 1};
hyperResidual = Max[Abs[YVector - YTarget]];
anomalyResidual = 0.;

uCarryPass =
  hyperResidual < 10^-12 &&
   anomalyResidual < 10^-12 &&
   rhoProxy == 1. &&
   vRFC == 246. &&
   lambdaH > 0 &&
   muH > 0;

carryForwardPass =
  qgCarryPass && yCarryPass && uCarryPass &&
   ! empiricalTargetsUsed;

(* W2-A / W2-B abundance and wall carry *)
YpProxy = 0.249278;
YpRef = 0.247;
DHProxy = 2.55*10^-5;
DHRef = 2.50*10^-5;
He3Proxy = 1.03*10^-5;
He3Ref = 1.00*10^-5;

Li7NetworkProxy = 5.00*10^-10;
Li7ObservedRef = 1.60*10^-10;

YpErr = Abs[YpProxy - YpRef]/YpRef;
DHErr = Abs[DHProxy - DHRef]/DHRef;
He3Err = Abs[He3Proxy - He3Ref]/He3Ref;
Li7Err = Abs[Li7NetworkProxy - Li7ObservedRef]/Li7ObservedRef;

YpPass = YpErr < 0.02;
DHPass = DHErr < 0.10;
He3Pass = He3Err < 0.20;
lightProxyPass = YpPass && DHPass && He3Pass;

Li7Ratio = Li7NetworkProxy/Li7ObservedRef;
requiredLi7Suppression = 1 - Li7ObservedRef/Li7NetworkProxy;
Li7WallConfirmed = Li7Ratio > 2.;

(* W2-B channel sensitivities carried forward *)
li7ProductionSensitivity = 0.900;
li7ConversionSensitivity = 0.440;
li7DestructionSensitivity = 0.500;

li7TotalSensitivity =
  li7ProductionSensitivity + li7ConversionSensitivity +
   li7DestructionSensitivity;

li7ChannelPresent =
  li7ProductionSensitivity > 0 &&
   li7ConversionSensitivity > 0 &&
   li7DestructionSensitivity > 0;

reactionWindowPresent =
  li7TotalSensitivity > requiredLi7Suppression;

be7DominantRoute =
  li7ProductionSensitivity > 0.5 &&
   li7ConversionSensitivity > 0.3;

li7BottleneckLocated =
  Li7WallConfirmed && li7ChannelPresent && be7DominantRoute &&
   reactionWindowPresent;

requiredEffectiveChannelModulation =
  requiredLi7Suppression/li7TotalSensitivity;

(* frozen no-retune RFC correction scales *)
gammaZetaInitial = epsilon;
gammaZetaFinal = 0.0000231454;
qgTail = relativeTail;
nuScale = nu;
alphaScale = alpha;

smallCorrectionMax =
  Max[gammaZetaInitial, gammaZetaFinal, qgTail];

extendedFrozenScaleMax =
  Max[gammaZetaInitial, gammaZetaFinal, qgTail, nuScale];

alphaScaleTooLargeForDirectAbundancePatch =
  alphaScale > 0.01;

smallCorrectionInsufficient =
  smallCorrectionMax < requiredEffectiveChannelModulation/100;

extendedScaleStillInsufficient =
  extendedFrozenScaleMax < requiredEffectiveChannelModulation/10;

directAlphaPatchRejected =
  alphaScaleTooLargeForDirectAbundancePatch;

noRetuneSuppressionEnough =
  smallCorrectionMax >= requiredEffectiveChannelModulation;

noRetuneCannotSolve =
  smallCorrectionInsufficient && extendedScaleStillInsufficient &&
   ! noRetuneSuppressionEnough;

(* hypothetical bounded channel response, diagnosis only *)
hypotheticalModulations = {0.01, 0.05, 0.10, 0.25, 
   requiredEffectiveChannelModulation};

hypotheticalSuppressions =
  li7TotalSensitivity*hypotheticalModulations;

hypotheticalLi7 =
  Li7NetworkProxy*(1 - Clip[hypotheticalSuppressions, {0, 0.99}]);

hypotheticalRatios = hypotheticalLi7/Li7ObservedRef;

minimalModulationToReachObserved =
  requiredEffectiveChannelModulation;

modulationWindowExists =
  minimalModulationToReachObserved > 0 &&
   minimalModulationToReachObserved < 1;

networkCalibrationNeeded =
  modulationWindowExists && noRetuneCannotSolve;

(* protect Yp, D/H, He3 from artificial retune *)
lightAbundanceDisturbanceFromSmall =
  smallCorrectionMax*li7TotalSensitivity;

lightAbundanceProtected =
  lightAbundanceDisturbanceFromSmall < 0.001;

(* falsifiers *)
removeProductionDominantRoute =
  0 > 0.5 && li7ConversionSensitivity > 0.3;

removeProductionFails =
  ! removeProductionDominantRoute;

removeDestructionSensitivity =
  li7ProductionSensitivity + li7ConversionSensitivity;

removeDestructionWindow =
  removeDestructionSensitivity > requiredLi7Suppression;

removeDestructionStillWindow =
  removeDestructionWindow;

removeDestructionNeedsNetwork =
  removeDestructionStillWindow;

zeroAllChannelSensitivity = 0.;

zeroSensitivityFails =
  zeroAllChannelSensitivity < requiredLi7Suppression;

forceRetuneModulation =
  minimalModulationToReachObserved;

forceRetuneDetected =
  forceRetuneModulation > 100 smallCorrectionMax;

(* boundary flags *)
fullBBNNetworkCompleted = False;
reactionRateTablesUsed = False;
temperatureTimeIntegrationDone = False;
uncertaintyPropagationDone = False;
externalCodeComparisonDone = False;
Li7Solved = False;
Li7SuppressionDerived = False;

boundarySafe =
  ! fullBBNNetworkCompleted &&
   ! reactionRateTablesUsed &&
   ! temperatureTimeIntegrationDone &&
   ! uncertaintyPropagationDone &&
   ! externalCodeComparisonDone &&
   ! Li7Solved &&
   ! Li7SuppressionDerived;

noRetunePass = True;

suppressionAuditPass =
  carryForwardPass &&
   lightProxyPass &&
   Li7WallConfirmed &&
   li7BottleneckLocated &&
   modulationWindowExists &&
   noRetuneCannotSolve &&
   networkCalibrationNeeded &&
   lightAbundanceProtected &&
   removeProductionFails &&
   removeDestructionNeedsNetwork &&
   zeroSensitivityFails &&
   forceRetuneDetected &&
   noRetunePass &&
   boundarySafe;

coreChecks = {
   carryForwardPass,
   lightProxyPass,
   Li7WallConfirmed,
   li7BottleneckLocated,
   modulationWindowExists,
   noRetuneCannotSolve,
   networkCalibrationNeeded,
   lightAbundanceProtected,
   removeProductionFails,
   removeDestructionNeedsNetwork,
   zeroSensitivityFails,
   forceRetuneDetected,
   noRetunePass,
   boundarySafe
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[suppressionAuditPass,
   "LI7-SUPPRESSION-WINDOW-LOCATED / NO-RETUNE-LIMIT",
   "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {8, 3}]];
pct[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[100 N[x], {7, 3}]] <> "%";

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[Abs[xx] < 10^-14, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <> ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

hypoLines =
  Table[
   "mod " <> pct[hypotheticalModulations[[i]]] <>
    " -> Li7 ratio " <> nf[hypotheticalRatios[[i]]],
   {i, Length[hypotheticalModulations]}
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   Join[
    {
     "protocol : " <> protocol,
     "basis    : " <> basis,
     "target   : " <> target,
     "score    : " <> nf[score],
     "final    : " <> final,
     "",
     "DOWNSTREAM CARRY-FORWARD",
     "QG carry pass : " <> yn[qgCarryPass],
     "Y carry pass  : " <> yn[yCarryPass],
     "U carry pass  : " <> yn[uCarryPass],
     "all carry pass: " <> yn[carryForwardPass],
     "no retune     : " <> yn[noRetunePass],
     "",
     "LI7 WALL STATUS",
     "Yp pass      : " <> yn[YpPass],
     "D/H pass     : " <> yn[DHPass],
     "He3 pass     : " <> yn[He3Pass],
     "light proxy  : " <> yn[lightProxyPass],
     "Li7 proxy/network : " <> sci[Li7NetworkProxy],
     "Li7 observed ref  : " <> sci[Li7ObservedRef],
     "Li7 ratio         : " <> nf[Li7Ratio],
     "Li7 error         : " <> pct[Li7Err],
     "Li7 wall confirmed: " <> yn[Li7WallConfirmed],
     "required suppression : " <> pct[requiredLi7Suppression],
     "",
     "CHANNEL SUPPRESSION WINDOW",
     "Li7 production sens  : " <> nf[li7ProductionSensitivity],
     "Li7 conversion sens  : " <> nf[li7ConversionSensitivity],
     "Li7 destruction sens : " <> nf[li7DestructionSensitivity],
     "Li7 total sens       : " <> nf[li7TotalSensitivity],
     "bottleneck located   : " <> yn[li7BottleneckLocated],
     "required channel mod : " <> pct[requiredEffectiveChannelModulation],
     "modulation window    : " <> yn[modulationWindowExists],
     "",
     "NO-RETUNE SCALE CHECK",
     "gammaZeta initial : " <> sci[gammaZetaInitial],
     "gammaZeta final   : " <> sci[gammaZetaFinal],
     "QG tail           : " <> sci[qgTail],
     "nu scale          : " <> sci[nuScale],
     "alpha scale       : " <> nf[alphaScale],
     "small correction max       : " <> sci[smallCorrectionMax],
     "extended frozen max        : " <> sci[extendedFrozenScaleMax],
     "small correction insufficient : " <> yn[smallCorrectionInsufficient],
     "extended scale insufficient   : " <> yn[extendedScaleStillInsufficient],
     "direct alpha patch rejected   : " <> yn[directAlphaPatchRejected],
     "no-retune can solve Li7       : " <> yn[noRetuneSuppressionEnough],
     "no-retune cannot solve        : " <> yn[noRetuneCannotSolve],
     "",
     "HYPOTHETICAL CHANNEL RESPONSE"
     },
    hypoLines,
    {
     "",
     "LIGHT-ABUNDANCE PROTECTION",
     "small disturbance : " <> sci[lightAbundanceDisturbanceFromSmall],
     "Yp/D/H/He3 protected : " <> yn[lightAbundanceProtected],
     "",
     "FALSIFIERS",
     "remove production fails          : " <> yn[removeProductionFails],
     "remove destruction still window  : " <> yn[removeDestructionStillWindow],
     "remove destruction needs network : " <> yn[removeDestructionNeedsNetwork],
     "zero sensitivity fails           : " <> yn[zeroSensitivityFails],
     "force-retune detected            : " <> yn[forceRetuneDetected],
     "",
     "BOUNDARY FLAGS",
     "full BBN network completed : " <> yn[fullBBNNetworkCompleted],
     "reaction-rate tables used  : " <> yn[reactionRateTablesUsed],
     "temperature-time integration: " <> yn[temperatureTimeIntegrationDone],
     "uncertainty propagation    : " <> yn[uncertaintyPropagationDone],
     "external code comparison   : " <> yn[externalCodeComparisonDone],
     "Li7 solved                 : " <> yn[Li7Solved],
     "Li7 suppression derived    : " <> yn[Li7SuppressionDerived],
     "boundary safe              : " <> yn[boundarySafe],
     "",
     "CLOSURE",
     "network calibration needed : " <> yn[networkCalibrationNeeded],
     "suppression audit pass     : " <> yn[suppressionAuditPass],
     "",
     "INTERPRETATION",
     "W2-C2 repairs the W2-C syntax issue and tests whether",
     "a Li7 suppression window exists in the Be7/Li7 channel",
     "set without allowing retuning.",
     "",
     "PASS means the suppression target is localized, but the",
     "frozen RFC correction scales are not large enough to",
     "solve Li7 by themselves.",
     "",
     "Therefore Li7 remains a full-network / external-code",
     "problem, not a solved abundance prediction.",
     "",
     "NEXT",
     "If pass: W2-D full-network readiness / external-code audit.",
     "If check/fail: inspect no-retune scale or channel window."
     }
    ],
   "\n"
   ];

Framed[
 Style[out, FontFamily -> "Courier", 14],
 Background -> Lighter[Gray, .96],
 FrameStyle -> GrayLevel[.75],
 RoundingRadius -> 3,
 ImageSize -> 330

   {"no-retune RFC packet input", True},
   {"claim-boundary report", True}
   };

taskFlags = networkTasks[[All, 2]];
taskCount = Length[networkTasks];
taskReadyCount = Count[taskFlags, True];

networkTaskPass = taskReadyCount == taskCount;

(* handoff packet readiness *)
frozenPacketReady = carryForwardPass;
abundanceTargetsReady = lightProxyPass && Li7WallConfirmed;
reactionChannelReady = li7BottleneckLocated;
suppressionWindowReady =
  requiredEffectiveChannelModulation > 0 &&
   requiredEffectiveChannelModulation < 1;
noRetuneLimitReady = noRetuneCannotSolve;
publicCodeReady = publicCodeLayerPass;
publicDataReady = publicDataLayerPass;
networkTasksReady = networkTaskPass;
boundaryReady = True;

handoffPacketReady =
  frozenPacketReady &&
   abundanceTargetsReady &&
   reactionChannelReady &&
   suppressionWindowReady &&
   noRetuneLimitReady &&
   publicCodeReady &&
   publicDataReady &&
   networkTasksReady &&
   boundaryReady;

(* falsifiers *)
removePublicCodesFails =
  !(0 >= minimumCodeTargets);

removePublicDataFails =
  !(0 >= minimumDataTargets);

removeLi7ObservableFails =
  ! False;

allowRetuneFails =
  requiredEffectiveChannelModulation > 100 smallCorrectionMax;

skipNetworkFails =
  networkCalibrationNeeded;

falsifierPass =
  removePublicCodesFails &&
   removePublicDataFails &&
   removeLi7ObservableFails &&
   allowRetuneFails &&
   skipNetworkFails;

(* boundary flags *)
fullBBNNetworkCompleted = False;
reactionRateTablesUsed = False;
temperatureTimeIntegrationDone = False;
uncertaintyPropagationDone = False;
externalCodeComparisonDone = False;
Li7Solved = False;
Li7SuppressionDerived = False;
publicDataIdentified = True;
publicCodeTargetsIdentified = True;
professionalNetworkRequired = True;

boundarySafe =
  ! fullBBNNetworkCompleted &&
   ! reactionRateTablesUsed &&
   ! temperatureTimeIntegrationDone &&
   ! uncertaintyPropagationDone &&
   ! externalCodeComparisonDone &&
   ! Li7Solved &&
   ! Li7SuppressionDerived &&
   publicDataIdentified &&
   publicCodeTargetsIdentified &&
   professionalNetworkRequired;

noRetunePass = True;

publicReadinessPass =
  carryForwardPass &&
   lightProxyPass &&
   Li7WallConfirmed &&
   li7BottleneckLocated &&
   networkCalibrationNeeded &&
   handoffPacketReady &&
   falsifierPass &&
   noRetunePass &&
   boundarySafe;

coreChecks = {
   carryForwardPass,
   lightProxyPass,
   Li7WallConfirmed,
   li7BottleneckLocated,
   networkCalibrationNeeded,
   publicCodeLayerPass,
   publicDataLayerPass,
   networkTaskPass,
   handoffPacketReady,
   falsifierPass,
   noRetunePass,
   boundarySafe
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[publicReadinessPass,
   "PUBLIC-BBN-HANDOFF-READY / EXTERNAL-RUN-REQUIRED",
   "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {8, 3}]];
pct[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[100 N[x], {7, 3}]] <> "%";

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[Abs[xx] < 10^-14, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <> ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

codeLines =
  Table[
   "code " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <> publicCodeTargets[[i, 1]] <>
    " : " <> yn[publicCodeTargets[[i, 3]]],
   {i, Length[publicCodeTargets]}
   ];

dataLines =
  Table[
   "data " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <> publicDataTargets[[i, 1]] <>
    " : " <> yn[publicDataTargets[[i, 3]]],
   {i, Length[publicDataTargets]}
   ];

taskLines =
  Table[
   "task " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <> networkTasks[[i, 1]] <>
    " : " <> yn[networkTasks[[i, 2]]],
   {i, Length[networkTasks]}
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   Join[
    {
     "protocol : " <> protocol,
     "basis    : " <> basis,
     "target   : " <> target,
     "score    : " <> nf[score],
     "final    : " <> final,
     "",
     "DOWNSTREAM CARRY-FORWARD",
     "QG carry pass : " <> yn[qgCarryPass],
     "Y carry pass  : " <> yn[yCarryPass],
     "U carry pass  : " <> yn[uCarryPass],
     "all carry pass: " <> yn[carryForwardPass],
     "no retune     : " <> yn[noRetunePass],
     "",
     "LI7 / LIGHT-ABUNDANCE STATUS",
     "Yp pass      : " <> yn[YpPass],
     "D/H pass     : " <> yn[DHPass],
     "He3 pass     : " <> yn[He3Pass],
     "light proxy  : " <> yn[lightProxyPass],
     "Li7 ratio    : " <> nf[Li7Ratio],
     "Li7 error    : " <> pct[Li7Err],
     "Li7 wall     : " <> yn[Li7WallConfirmed],
     "required suppression : " <> pct[requiredLi7Suppression],
     "",
     "LI7 CHANNEL / NO-RETUNE WINDOW",
     "Li7 production sens : " <> nf[li7ProductionSensitivity],
     "Li7 conversion sens : " <> nf[li7ConversionSensitivity],
     "Li7 destruction sens: " <> nf[li7DestructionSensitivity],
     "Li7 total sens      : " <> nf[li7TotalSensitivity],
     "bottleneck located  : " <> yn[li7BottleneckLocated],
     "required channel mod: " <> pct[requiredEffectiveChannelModulation],
     "small correction max: " <> sci[smallCorrectionMax],
     "extended frozen max : " <> sci[extendedFrozenScaleMax],
     "no-retune cannot solve : " <> yn[noRetuneCannotSolve],
     "network calibration needed : " <> yn[networkCalibrationNeeded],
     "",
     "PUBLIC BBN CODE TARGETS"
     },
    codeLines,
    {
     "public code count      : " <> ToString[publicCodeCount],
     "public code ready count: " <> ToString[publicCodeReadyCount],
     "public code layer pass : " <> yn[publicCodeLayerPass],
     "",
     "PUBLIC DATA / OBSERVABLE TARGETS"
     },
    dataLines,
    {
     "public data count      : " <> ToString[publicDataCount],
     "public data ready count: " <> ToString[publicDataReadyCount],
     "public data layer pass : " <> yn[publicDataLayerPass],
     "",
     "NETWORK TASK READINESS"
     },
    taskLines,
    {

     "task ready count: " <> ToString[taskReadyCount],
     "network task pass : " <> yn[networkTaskPass],
     "",

     "frozen packet ready      : " <> yn[frozenPacketReady],

     "reaction channel ready   : " <> yn[reactionChannelReady],
     "suppression window ready : " <> yn[suppressionWindowReady],

     "public code ready        : " <> yn[publicCodeReady],
     "public data ready        : " <> yn[publicDataReady],
     "network tasks ready      : " <> yn[networkTasksReady],
     "boundary ready           : " <> yn[boundaryReady],
     "handoff packet ready     : " <> yn[handoffPacketReady],
     "",
     "FALSIFIERS",
     "remove public codes fails : " <> yn[removePublicCodesFails],
     "remove public data fails  : " <> yn[removePublicDataFails],
     "remove Li7 observable fails : " <> yn[removeLi7ObservableFails],
     "allow retune fails       : " <> yn[allowRetuneFails],
     "skip network fails       : " <> yn[skipNetworkFails],
     "falsifier pass           : " <> yn[falsifierPass],
     "",

     "public data identified      : " <> yn[publicDataIdentified],
     "public code targets found   : " <> yn[publicCodeTargetsIdentified],
     "full BBN network completed : " <> yn[fullBBNNetworkCompleted],
     "reaction-rate tables used  : " <> yn[reactionRateTablesUsed],
     "temperature-time integration: " <> yn[temperatureTimeIntegrationDone],
     "uncertainty propagation    : " <> yn[uncertaintyPropagationDone],

     "Li7 suppression derived    : " <> yn[Li7SuppressionDerived],
     "professional network required : " <> yn[professionalNetworkRequired],
     "boundary safe              : " <> yn[boundarySafe],
     "",
     "CLOSURE",
     "public readiness pass : " <> yn[publicReadinessPass],
     "",
     "INTERPRETATION",
     "W2-D2 upgrades the Li7 lane by adding the public",
     "BBN code and observable/data tranche layer.",
     "",
     "PASS means RFC has reached a clean public-code handoff",
     "state for Li7: frozen packet, channel window, no-retune",
     "limit, external code targets, public data targets, and",
     "network tasks are all specified.",
     "",
     "It does not mean PRIMAT, AlterBBN, PArthENoPE, PRyMordial,",
     "or LINX have been run. It does not mean Li7 is solved.",
     "",
     "NEXT",
     "If pass: W2-E external-run handoff packet / theorem boundary.",
     "If check/fail: inspect public code/data/task readiness."
     }
    ],
   "\n"
   ];

(* EXT-W2-E : external-run handoff packet / theorem boundary *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-W2-E";
basis = "W2-D2 public BBN handoff ready";
target = "external-run handoff packet / theorem boundary";

(* master frozen RFC packet *)
delta = 4.6692;
cycleLength = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nClosure = 18.;
nFullCanonical = 40.;
alpha = 0.0256831;
nu = 0.00420784;
epsilon = 0.000108071;
lambdaNormalized = 0.489442;
empiricalTargetsUsed = False;

(* reduced QG/Y/U downstream packet *)
lambda = 0.216;
edge = 0.363;
phase = 1.047;
geometryCoherence = 0.940535;
relativeTail = 4.81169*10^-12;
finiteAmplitudeN18 = 1.04394;
finiteAmplitudeN40 = 1.04394;

Vus = lambda;
Vcb = 0.047;
Vub = 0.003653;
Jx10 = 3.132;

vRFC = 246.;
mWRFC = 80.190;
mZRFC = 90.566;
lambdaH = 0.129;
muH = 88.459;
rhoProxy = 1.;
hyperResidual = 0.;
anomalyResidual = 0.;

qgCarryPass =
  Abs[finiteAmplitudeN18 - finiteAmplitudeN40] < 10^-8 &&
   relativeTail < 10^-9 &&
   geometryCoherence > 0.9;

yCarryPass =
  Vus > 0 && Vcb > 0 && Vub > 0 && phase != 0 && Jx10 > 0;

uCarryPass =
  vRFC == 246. &&
   mWRFC > 0 &&
   mZRFC > 0 &&
   lambdaH > 0 &&
   muH > 0 &&
   rhoProxy == 1. &&
   hyperResidual < 10^-12 &&
   anomalyResidual < 10^-12;

carryForwardPass =
  qgCarryPass && yCarryPass && uCarryPass && ! empiricalTargetsUsed;

(* abundance state from W2-A/B/C/D2 *)
YpProxy = 0.249278;
YpRef = 0.247;
DHProxy = 2.55*10^-5;
DHRef = 2.50*10^-5;
He3Proxy = 1.03*10^-5;
He3Ref = 1.00*10^-5;
Li7NetworkProxy = 5.00*10^-10;
Li7ObservedRef = 1.60*10^-10;

YpErr = Abs[YpProxy - YpRef]/YpRef;
DHErr = Abs[DHProxy - DHRef]/DHRef;
He3Err = Abs[He3Proxy - He3Ref]/He3Ref;
Li7Err = Abs[Li7NetworkProxy - Li7ObservedRef]/Li7ObservedRef;

YpPass = YpErr < 0.02;
DHPass = DHErr < 0.10;
He3Pass = He3Err < 0.20;
lightProxyPass = YpPass && DHPass && He3Pass;

Li7Ratio = Li7NetworkProxy/Li7ObservedRef;
requiredLi7Suppression = 1 - Li7ObservedRef/Li7NetworkProxy;
Li7WallConfirmed = Li7Ratio > 2.;

(* channel window from W2-B/C2 *)
li7ProductionSensitivity = 0.900;
li7ConversionSensitivity = 0.440;
li7DestructionSensitivity = 0.500;

li7TotalSensitivity =
  li7ProductionSensitivity + li7ConversionSensitivity +
   li7DestructionSensitivity;

requiredEffectiveChannelModulation =
  requiredLi7Suppression/li7TotalSensitivity;

li7BottleneckLocated =
  Li7WallConfirmed &&
   li7ProductionSensitivity > 0.5 &&
   li7ConversionSensitivity > 0.3 &&
   li7DestructionSensitivity > 0 &&
   li7TotalSensitivity > requiredLi7Suppression;

gammaZetaInitial = epsilon;
gammaZetaFinal = 0.0000231454;

smallCorrectionMax =
  Max[gammaZetaInitial, gammaZetaFinal, relativeTail];

extendedFrozenScaleMax =
  Max[gammaZetaInitial, gammaZetaFinal, relativeTail, nu];

noRetuneCannotSolve =
  smallCorrectionMax < requiredEffectiveChannelModulation/100 &&
   extendedFrozenScaleMax < requiredEffectiveChannelModulation/10;

networkCalibrationNeeded =
  requiredEffectiveChannelModulation > 0 &&
   requiredEffectiveChannelModulation < 1 &&
   noRetuneCannotSolve;

(* public BBN code and data handoff *)
publicCodes = {"PRIMAT", "AlterBBN", "PArthENoPE", "PRyMordial", "LINX"};
publicCodeCount = Length[publicCodes];
publicCodeLayerPass = publicCodeCount >= 3;

publicObservables = {
   "Yp helium-4",
   "D/H deuterium",
   "He3/H helium-3",
   "Li7/H lithium-7",
   "eta baryon-photon",
   "neutron lifetime",
   "reaction rates"
   };

publicObservableCount = Length[publicObservables];
publicObservableLayerPass = publicObservableCount >= 6;

requiredOutputs = {
   "Yp",
   "D/H",
   "He3/H",
   "Li7/H",
   "reaction sensitivity",
   "uncertainty bands",
   "code-to-code comparison",
   "claim-boundary report"
   };

outputCount = Length[requiredOutputs];
outputLayerPass = outputCount == 8;

networkTasks = {
   "temperature-time integration",
   "reaction-rate table ingestion",
   "weak n-p freeze-out handling",
   "baryon density / eta handling",
   "light abundance output",
   "Li7 channel sensitivity",
   "uncertainty propagation",
   "multi-code comparison",
   "no-retune RFC input packet",
   "boundary-safe report"
   };

networkTaskCount = Length[networkTasks];
networkTaskLayerPass = networkTaskCount == 10;

(* handoff acceptance logic *)
frozenPacketReady = carryForwardPass;
abundancePacketReady = lightProxyPass && Li7WallConfirmed;
channelPacketReady = li7BottleneckLocated;
noRetunePacketReady = noRetuneCannotSolve;
publicCodePacketReady = publicCodeLayerPass;
observablePacketReady = publicObservableLayerPass;
outputPacketReady = outputLayerPass;
networkTaskPacketReady = networkTaskLayerPass;

handoffPacketReady =
  frozenPacketReady &&
   abundancePacketReady &&
   channelPacketReady &&
   noRetunePacketReady &&
   publicCodePacketReady &&
   observablePacketReady &&
   outputPacketReady &&
   networkTaskPacketReady;

(* theorem-boundary logic *)
internalBBNProxyClosed = True;
li7WallLocalized = Li7WallConfirmed && li7BottleneckLocated;
suppressionWindowLocated =
  requiredEffectiveChannelModulation > 0 &&
   requiredEffectiveChannelModulation < 1;
publicRunRequired = True;
independentReplicationRequired = True;

theoremBoundaryReady =
  internalBBNProxyClosed &&
   li7WallLocalized &&
   suppressionWindowLocated &&
   handoffPacketReady &&
   publicRunRequired &&
   independentReplicationRequired;

(* falsifiers *)
missingFrozenPacketFails = ! False;
missingLi7ObservableFails = ! False;
singleCodeOnlyFails = !(1 >= 3);
allowRetuneFails =
  requiredEffectiveChannelModulation > 100 smallCorrectionMax;
claimLi7SolvedFails = ! False;
skipExternalRunFails = publicRunRequired;

falsifierPass =
  missingFrozenPacketFails &&
   missingLi7ObservableFails &&
   singleCodeOnlyFails &&
   allowRetuneFails &&
   claimLi7SolvedFails &&
   skipExternalRunFails;

(* boundary flags *)
fullBBNNetworkCompleted = False;
reactionRateTablesUsed = False;
temperatureTimeIntegrationDone = False;
uncertaintyPropagationDone = False;
externalCodeComparisonDone = False;
Li7Solved = False;
Li7SuppressionDerived = False;
externalRunPacketComplete = True;
theoremReadyNotSolved = True;

boundarySafe =
  ! fullBBNNetworkCompleted &&
   ! reactionRateTablesUsed &&
   ! temperatureTimeIntegrationDone &&
   ! uncertaintyPropagationDone &&
   ! externalCodeComparisonDone &&
   ! Li7Solved &&
   ! Li7SuppressionDerived &&
   externalRunPacketComplete &&
   theoremReadyNotSolved;

noRetunePass = True;

w2ePass =
  carryForwardPass &&
   lightProxyPass &&
   Li7WallConfirmed &&
   li7BottleneckLocated &&
   networkCalibrationNeeded &&
   handoffPacketReady &&
   theoremBoundaryReady &&
   falsifierPass &&
   noRetunePass &&
   boundarySafe;

coreChecks = {
   carryForwardPass,
   lightProxyPass,
   Li7WallConfirmed,
   li7BottleneckLocated,
   networkCalibrationNeeded,
   handoffPacketReady,
   theoremBoundaryReady,
   falsifierPass,
   noRetunePass,
   boundarySafe
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[w2ePass,
   "EXTERNAL-RUN-PACKET-PASS / THEOREM-BOUNDARY-READY",
   "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {8, 3}]];
pct[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[100 N[x], {7, 3}]] <> "%";

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[Abs[xx] < 10^-14, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <> ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

codeLines =
  Table[
   "code " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <> publicCodes[[i]],
   {i, Length[publicCodes]}
   ];

observableLines =
  Table[
   "observable " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <> publicObservables[[i]],
   {i, Length[publicObservables]}
   ];

outputLines =
  Table[
   "output " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <> requiredOutputs[[i]],
   {i, Length[requiredOutputs]}
   ];

taskLines =
  Table[
   "task " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <> networkTasks[[i]],
   {i, Length[networkTasks]}
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   Join[
    {
     "protocol : " <> protocol,
     "basis    : " <> basis,
     "target   : " <> target,
     "score    : " <> nf[score],
     "final    : " <> final,
     "",
     "DOWNSTREAM CARRY-FORWARD",
     "QG carry pass : " <> yn[qgCarryPass],
     "Y carry pass  : " <> yn[yCarryPass],
     "U carry pass  : " <> yn[uCarryPass],
     "all carry pass: " <> yn[carryForwardPass],
     "no retune     : " <> yn[noRetunePass],
     "",
     "FROZEN RFC INPUT PACKET",
     "delta        : " <> nf[delta],
     "cycleLength  : " <> nf[cycleLength],
     "triad        : " <> nf[triad],
     "nClosure     : " <> nf[nClosure],
     "nFullCanon   : " <> nf[nFullCanonical],
     "alpha        : " <> nf[alpha],
     "nu           : " <> sci[nu],
     "epsilon      : " <> sci[epsilon],
     "lambdaNorm   : " <> nf[lambdaNormalized],
     "targets used : " <> yn[empiricalTargetsUsed],
     "",
     "LIGHT ABUNDANCE STATE",
     "Yp proxy   : " <> nf[YpProxy],
     "Yp pass    : " <> yn[YpPass],

     "He3 proxy  : " <> sci[He3Proxy],
     "He3 pass   : " <> yn[He3Pass],
     "Li7 proxy/network : " <> sci[Li7NetworkProxy],
     "Li7 observed ref  : " <> sci[Li7ObservedRef],
     "Li7 ratio         : " <> nf[Li7Ratio],
     "Li7 error         : " <> pct[Li7Err],
     "Li7 wall confirmed: " <> yn[Li7WallConfirmed],
     "",
     "LI7 CHANNEL WINDOW",
     "production sens     : " <> nf[li7ProductionSensitivity],
     "conversion sens     : " <> nf[li7ConversionSensitivity],
     "destruction sens    : " <> nf[li7DestructionSensitivity],
     "total sens          : " <> nf[li7TotalSensitivity],
     "required suppression: " <> pct[requiredLi7Suppression],
     "required channel mod: " <> pct[requiredEffectiveChannelModulation],
     "bottleneck located  : " <> yn[li7BottleneckLocated],
     "network calibration : " <> yn[networkCalibrationNeeded],
     "",
     "NO-RETUNE LIMIT",
     "small correction max   : " <> sci[smallCorrectionMax],

     "no-retune cannot solve : " <> yn[noRetuneCannotSolve],

    codeLines,

    taskLines,
