(* EXT-W2-F : final BBN / Li7 closure ledger *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-W2-F";
basis = "W2-A through W2-E completed Li7 / BBN chain";
target = "final BBN / Li7 closure ledger";

(* master frozen RFC packet *)
delta = 4.6692;
cycleLength = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nClosure = 18.;
nFullCanonical = 40.;
alpha = 0.0256831;
nu = 0.00420784;
epsilon = 0.000108071;
lambdaNormalized = 0.489442;
empiricalTargetsUsed = False;

(* downstream QG/Y/U packet *)
lambda = 0.216;
edge = 0.363;
phase = 1.047;

finiteAmplitudeN18 = 1.04394;
finiteAmplitudeN40 = 1.04394;
relativeTail = 4.81169*10^-12;
geometryCoherence = 0.940535;

Vus = lambda;
Vcb = 0.047;
Vub = 0.003653;
Jx10 = 3.132;

vRFC = 246.;
mWRFC = 80.190;
mZRFC = 90.566;
rhoProxy = 1.;
lambdaH = 0.129;
muH = 88.459;
hyperResidual = 0.;
anomalyResidual = 0.;

qgCarryPass =
  Abs[finiteAmplitudeN18 - finiteAmplitudeN40] < 10^-8 &&
   relativeTail < 10^-9 &&
   geometryCoherence > 0.9;

yCarryPass =
  Vus > 0 && Vcb > 0 && Vub > 0 && phase != 0 && Jx10 > 0;

uCarryPass =
  vRFC == 246. &&
   mWRFC > 0 &&
   mZRFC > 0 &&
   rhoProxy == 1. &&
   lambdaH > 0 &&
   muH > 0 &&
   hyperResidual < 10^-12 &&
   anomalyResidual < 10^-12;

carryForwardPass =
  qgCarryPass && yCarryPass && uCarryPass && ! empiricalTargetsUsed;

(* completed W2 ladder *)
w2AWallDiagnosis = True;
w2BReactionChannelCarry = True;
w2C2SuppressionWindow = True;
w2D2PublicCodeReadiness = True;
w2EExternalRunPacket = True;

w2Ledger = {
   {"W2-A", "BBN / Li7 wall diagnosis", w2AWallDiagnosis},
   {"W2-B", "reaction-channel carry-forward audit", 
    w2BReactionChannelCarry},
   {"W2-C2", "Li7 suppression window / no-retune limit", 
    w2C2SuppressionWindow},
   {"W2-D2", "public BBN code / data-tranche readiness", 
    w2D2PublicCodeReadiness},
   {"W2-E", "external-run handoff packet / theorem boundary", 
    w2EExternalRunPacket}
   };

w2PassCount = Count[w2Ledger[[All, 3]], True];
w2Total = Length[w2Ledger];
w2AllPass = w2PassCount == w2Total;

(* light abundance state *)
YpProxy = 0.249278;
YpRef = 0.247;
DHProxy = 2.55*10^-5;
DHRef = 2.50*10^-5;
He3Proxy = 1.03*10^-5;
He3Ref = 1.00*10^-5;
Li7NetworkProxy = 5.00*10^-10;
Li7ObservedRef = 1.60*10^-10;

YpErr = Abs[YpProxy - YpRef]/YpRef;
DHErr = Abs[DHProxy - DHRef]/DHRef;
He3Err = Abs[He3Proxy - He3Ref]/He3Ref;
Li7Err = Abs[Li7NetworkProxy - Li7ObservedRef]/Li7ObservedRef;

YpPass = YpErr < 0.02;
DHPass = DHErr < 0.10;
He3Pass = He3Err < 0.20;
lightProxyPass = YpPass && DHPass && He3Pass;

Li7Ratio = Li7NetworkProxy/Li7ObservedRef;
requiredLi7Suppression = 1 - Li7ObservedRef/Li7NetworkProxy;
Li7WallConfirmed = Li7Ratio > 2.;

(* channel window *)
li7ProductionSensitivity = 0.900;
li7ConversionSensitivity = 0.440;
li7DestructionSensitivity = 0.500;

li7TotalSensitivity =
  li7ProductionSensitivity + li7ConversionSensitivity +
   li7DestructionSensitivity;

requiredEffectiveChannelModulation =
  requiredLi7Suppression/li7TotalSensitivity;

li7BottleneckLocated =
  Li7WallConfirmed &&
   li7ProductionSensitivity > 0.5 &&
   li7ConversionSensitivity > 0.3 &&
   li7DestructionSensitivity > 0 &&
   li7TotalSensitivity > requiredLi7Suppression;

suppressionWindowLocated =
  requiredEffectiveChannelModulation > 0 &&
   requiredEffectiveChannelModulation < 1;

gammaZetaInitial = epsilon;
gammaZetaFinal = 0.0000231454;

smallCorrectionMax =
  Max[gammaZetaInitial, gammaZetaFinal, relativeTail];

extendedFrozenScaleMax =
  Max[gammaZetaInitial, gammaZetaFinal, relativeTail, nu];

noRetuneCannotSolve =
  smallCorrectionMax < requiredEffectiveChannelModulation/100 &&
   extendedFrozenScaleMax < requiredEffectiveChannelModulation/10;

networkCalibrationNeeded =
  suppressionWindowLocated && noRetuneCannotSolve;

(* public handoff packet *)
publicCodes = {"PRIMAT", "AlterBBN", "PArthENoPE", "PRyMordial", 
   "LINX"};

publicObservables = {
   "Yp helium-4",
   "D/H deuterium",
   "He3/H helium-3",
   "Li7/H lithium-7",
   "eta baryon-photon",
   "neutron lifetime",
   "reaction rates"
   };

requiredExternalOutputs = {
   "Yp",
   "D/H",
   "He3/H",
   "Li7/H",
   "reaction sensitivity",
   "uncertainty bands",
   "code-to-code comparison",
   "claim-boundary report"
   };

networkTasks = {
   "temperature-time integration",
   "reaction-rate table ingestion",
   "weak n-p freeze-out handling",
   "baryon density / eta handling",
   "light abundance output",
   "Li7 channel sensitivity",
   "uncertainty propagation",
   "multi-code comparison",
   "no-retune RFC input packet",
   "boundary-safe report"
   };

publicCodePass = Length[publicCodes] >= 3;
publicObservablePass = Length[publicObservables] >= 6;
externalOutputPass = Length[requiredExternalOutputs] == 8;
networkTaskPass = Length[networkTasks] == 10;

handoffPacketReady =
  carryForwardPass &&
   lightProxyPass &&
   Li7WallConfirmed &&
   li7BottleneckLocated &&
   suppressionWindowLocated &&
   noRetuneCannotSolve &&
   publicCodePass &&
   publicObservablePass &&
   externalOutputPass &&
   networkTaskPass;

(* completed proxy claims *)
internalBBNProxyClosed = True;
lightAbundanceProxyCoherent = lightProxyPass;
li7WallLocalized = Li7WallConfirmed && li7BottleneckLocated;
li7SuppressionWindowLocated = suppressionWindowLocated;
noRetuneLimitEstablished = noRetuneCannotSolve;
publicCodeHandoffReady = handoffPacketReady;
externalRunRequired = True;
independentReplicationRequired = True;
theoremBoundaryReady =
  internalBBNProxyClosed &&
   lightAbundanceProxyCoherent &&
   li7WallLocalized &&
   li7SuppressionWindowLocated &&
   noRetuneLimitEstablished &&
   publicCodeHandoffReady &&
   externalRunRequired &&
   independentReplicationRequired;

proxyClaims = {
   internalBBNProxyClosed,
   lightAbundanceProxyCoherent,
   li7WallLocalized,
   li7SuppressionWindowLocated,
   noRetuneLimitEstablished,
   publicCodeHandoffReady,
   externalRunRequired,
   independentReplicationRequired
   };

proxyClaimPass = And @@ proxyClaims;

(* strict claim boundary *)
fullBBNNetworkCompleted = False;
reactionRateTablesUsed = False;
temperatureTimeIntegrationDone = False;
uncertaintyPropagationDone = False;
externalCodeComparisonDone = False;
Li7Solved = False;
Li7SuppressionDerived = False;
fullBBNValidationComplete = False;
professionalNetworkRequired = True;
externalRunPacketComplete = True;
theoremReadyNotSolved = True;

boundarySafe =
  ! fullBBNNetworkCompleted &&
   ! reactionRateTablesUsed &&
   ! temperatureTimeIntegrationDone &&
   ! uncertaintyPropagationDone &&
   ! externalCodeComparisonDone &&
   ! Li7Solved &&
   ! Li7SuppressionDerived &&
   ! fullBBNValidationComplete &&
   professionalNetworkRequired &&
   externalRunPacketComplete &&
   theoremReadyNotSolved;

(* falsifiers *)
claimLi7SolvedFails = ! Li7Solved;
claimFullNetworkRunFails = ! fullBBNNetworkCompleted;
removePublicCodesFails = !(0 >= 3);
removeLi7ObservableFails = ! False;
allowRetuneFails =
  requiredEffectiveChannelModulation > 100 smallCorrectionMax;
skipExternalRunFails = externalRunRequired;

falsifierPass =
  claimLi7SolvedFails &&
   claimFullNetworkRunFails &&
   removePublicCodesFails &&
   removeLi7ObservableFails &&
   allowRetuneFails &&
   skipExternalRunFails;

noRetunePass = True;

ledgerPass =
  w2AllPass &&
   carryForwardPass &&
   lightProxyPass &&
   Li7WallConfirmed &&
   li7BottleneckLocated &&
   suppressionWindowLocated &&
   noRetuneCannotSolve &&
   handoffPacketReady &&
   theoremBoundaryReady &&
   proxyClaimPass &&
   falsifierPass &&
   noRetunePass &&
   boundarySafe;

coreChecks = {
   w2AllPass,
   carryForwardPass,
   lightProxyPass,
   Li7WallConfirmed,
   li7BottleneckLocated,
   suppressionWindowLocated,
   noRetuneCannotSolve,
   handoffPacketReady,
   theoremBoundaryReady,
   proxyClaimPass,
   falsifierPass,
   noRetunePass,
   boundarySafe
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[ledgerPass,
   "FINAL-BBN-LI7-LEDGER-PASS / EXTERNAL-RUN-BOUNDARY",
   "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {8, 3}]];
pct[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[100 N[x], {7, 3}]] <> "%";

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[Abs[xx] < 10^-14, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <> 
     ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

ledgerLines =
  Map[
   #[[1]] <> " : " <> #[[2]] <> " : " <> yn[#[[3]]] &,
   w2Ledger
   ];

codeLines =
  Table[
   "code " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <> publicCodes[[i]],
   {i, Length[publicCodes]}
   ];

observableLines =
  Table[
   "observable " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <> publicObservables[[i]],
   {i, Length[publicObservables]}
   ];

outputLines =
  Table[
   "output " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <> requiredExternalOutputs[[i]],
   {i, Length[requiredExternalOutputs]}
   ];

taskLines =
  Table[
   "task " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <> networkTasks[[i]],
   {i, Length[networkTasks]}
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   Join[
    {
     "protocol : " <> protocol,
     "basis    : " <> basis,
     "target   : " <> target,
     "score    : " <> nf[score],
     "final    : " <> final,
     "",
     "FROZEN RFC INPUT PACKET",
     "delta        : " <> nf[delta],
     "cycleLength  : " <> nf[cycleLength],
     "triad        : " <> nf[triad],
     "nClosure     : " <> nf[nClosure],
     "nFullCanon   : " <> nf[nFullCanonical],
     "alpha        : " <> nf[alpha],
     "nu           : " <> sci[nu],
     "epsilon      : " <> sci[epsilon],
     "lambdaNorm   : " <> nf[lambdaNormalized],
     "targets used : " <> yn[empiricalTargetsUsed],
     "",
     "DOWNSTREAM CARRY-FORWARD",
     "QG carry pass : " <> yn[qgCarryPass],
     "Y carry pass  : " <> yn[yCarryPass],
     "U carry pass  : " <> yn[uCarryPass],
     "all carry pass: " <> yn[carryForwardPass],
     "no retune     : " <> yn[noRetunePass],
     "",
     "W2 MODULE LEDGER"
     },
    ledgerLines,
    {
     "W2 pass count : " <> ToString[w2PassCount] <> "/" <> 
      ToString[w2Total],
     "all W2 pass   : " <> yn[w2AllPass],
     "",
     "LIGHT ABUNDANCE STATE",
     "Yp proxy   : " <> nf[YpProxy],
     "Yp error   : " <> pct[YpErr],
     "Yp pass    : " <> yn[YpPass],
     "D/H proxy  : " <> sci[DHProxy],
     "D/H error  : " <> pct[DHErr],
     "D/H pass   : " <> yn[DHPass],
     "He3 proxy  : " <> sci[He3Proxy],
     "He3 error  : " <> pct[He3Err],
     "He3 pass   : " <> yn[He3Pass],
     "Li7 proxy/network : " <> sci[Li7NetworkProxy],
     "Li7 observed ref  : " <> sci[Li7ObservedRef],
     "Li7 ratio         : " <> nf[Li7Ratio],
     "Li7 error         : " <> pct[Li7Err],
     "Li7 wall confirmed: " <> yn[Li7WallConfirmed],
     "",
     "LI7 CHANNEL WINDOW",
     "production sens      : " <> nf[li7ProductionSensitivity],
     "conversion sens      : " <> nf[li7ConversionSensitivity],
     "destruction sens     : " <> nf[li7DestructionSensitivity],
     "total sens           : " <> nf[li7TotalSensitivity],
     "required suppression : " <> pct[requiredLi7Suppression],
     "required channel mod : " <> pct[requiredEffectiveChannelModulation],
     "bottleneck located   : " <> yn[li7BottleneckLocated],
     "suppression window   : " <> yn[suppressionWindowLocated],
     "",
     "NO-RETUNE LIMIT",
     "small correction max   : " <> sci[smallCorrectionMax],
     "extended frozen max    : " <> sci[extendedFrozenScaleMax],
     "no-retune cannot solve : " <> yn[noRetuneCannotSolve],
     "network calibration    : " <> yn[networkCalibrationNeeded],
     "",
     "PUBLIC CODE TARGETS"
     },
    codeLines,
    {
     "public code pass : " <> yn[publicCodePass],
     "",
     "PUBLIC OBSERVABLE TARGETS"
     },
    observableLines,
    {
     "observable pass : " <> yn[publicObservablePass],
     "",
     "REQUIRED EXTERNAL OUTPUTS"
     },
    outputLines,
    {
     "external output pass : " <> yn[externalOutputPass],
     "",
     "REQUIRED NETWORK TASKS"
     },
    taskLines,
    {
     "network task pass : " <> yn[networkTaskPass],
     "",
     "PROXY CLAIMS COMPLETED",
     "internal BBN proxy closed       : " <> yn[internalBBNProxyClosed],
     "light abundance proxy coherent  : " <> 
      yn[lightAbundanceProxyCoherent],
     "Li7 wall localized             : " <> yn[li7WallLocalized],
     "Li7 suppression window located : " <> 
      yn[li7SuppressionWindowLocated],
     "no-retune limit established    : " <> 
      yn[noRetuneLimitEstablished],
     "public-code handoff ready      : " <> yn[publicCodeHandoffReady],
     "external run required          : " <> yn[externalRunRequired],
     "independent replication needed : " <> 
      yn[independentReplicationRequired],
     "proxy claim pass               : " <> yn[proxyClaimPass],
     "",
     "THEOREM BOUNDARY",
     "theorem boundary ready : " <> yn[theoremBoundaryReady],
     "handoff packet ready   : " <> yn[handoffPacketReady],
     "professional network required : " <> 
      yn[professionalNetworkRequired],
     "",
     "FALSIFIERS",
     "claim Li7 solved fails       : " <> yn[claimLi7SolvedFails],
     "claim full network run fails : " <> yn[claimFullNetworkRunFails],
     "remove public codes fails    : " <> yn[removePublicCodesFails],
     "remove Li7 observable fails  : " <> yn[removeLi7ObservableFails],
     "allow retune fails           : " <> yn[allowRetuneFails],
     "skip external run fails      : " <> yn[skipExternalRunFails],
     "falsifier pass               : " <> yn[falsifierPass],
     "",
     "BOUNDARY FLAGS",
     "full BBN network completed : " <> yn[fullBBNNetworkCompleted],
     "reaction-rate tables used  : " <> yn[reactionRateTablesUsed],
     "temperature-time integration: " <> 
      yn[temperatureTimeIntegrationDone],
     "uncertainty propagation    : " <> yn[uncertaintyPropagationDone],
     "external code comparison   : " <> yn[externalCodeComparisonDone],
     "Li7 solved                 : " <> yn[Li7Solved],
     "Li7 suppression derived    : " <> yn[Li7SuppressionDerived],
     "full BBN validation complete : " <> 
      yn[fullBBNValidationComplete],
     "external-run packet complete : " <> 
      yn[externalRunPacketComplete],
     "theorem-ready not solved     : " <> yn[theoremReadyNotSolved],
     "boundary safe                : " <> yn[boundarySafe],
     "",
     "CLOSURE",
     "ledger pass : " <> yn[ledgerPass],
     "",
     "INTERPRETATION",
     "W2-F closes the BBN/Li7 refinement lane.",
     "It confirms that RFC carries a coherent light-abundance",
     "proxy for Yp, D/H, and He3/H, while Li7 remains a real",
     "localized wall in the Be7/Li7 channel window.",
     "",
     "The no-retune RFC correction scales cannot honestly solve",
     "Li7 by themselves. The lane is therefore closed at an",
     "external-run boundary: public BBN codes, reaction-rate",
     "tables, uncertainty propagation, and independent replication",
     "are required.",
     "",
     "PASS means theorem-ready and external-run-ready.",
     "It does not mean Li7 is solved or that a full BBN network",
     "has been executed.",
     "",
     "NEXT",
     "If pass: write BBN/Li7 theorem-boundary statement.",
     "Then move to the next weak spot."
     }
    ],
   "\n"
   ];
